Curriculum · Urban India · Spoken only

Urban Spoken Hindi

A teach-from-the-page curriculum for children, teens, young adults, and adults. Written so a teacher can open a lesson and start the same day.

Twelve courses. Three timelines on every course. Crash paths for interviews and exam orals. Clear urban Hindi. No shuddh-Hindi shaming.

Use the contents list to jump. In the PDF, those lines are links.

Urban Spoken Hindi · How to teach

How to teach

Every lesson is 60 minutes: warm-up 8, new language 10, guided practice 12, free speaking 18, polish 7, homework 5. After the first ten minutes, students should speak more than the teacher.

Correct the day’s target sentences during guided practice. Do not stop a story for small grammar. Never mock a student. Never treat adult beginners like children. English or home-language instructions are allowed at Beginner; the speech you want back is Hindi. Teach aap vs tum as a social choice. Fix gender only when it is the day’s target or it blocks meaning.

Homework is a voice note or a real line in the city. Reply with one glow and one grow.

Urban Spoken Hindi · Placement

Place a student

Age first, then a 10-minute talk: name, family, a normal day, last Sunday, next weekend, a photo, an opinion. For older students, a team story or why this job.

Silence or one word → Beginner. Short present sentences → Elementary / Everyday. Understands but freezes → Fluency. Can argue and wants polish → Upper or Interview. When in doubt, start easier.

Timelines

6-week starter: two hours a week, only lessons tagged 6-week (the spine). 3-month core: lessons tagged 3-month. 6-month: every lesson. Crash: daily, only crash-tagged lessons, for Interview and teen orals.

Urban Spoken Hindi · Children · Pehli baat

Children · Pehli baat

Children 6–10 Absolute Beginner / Beginner 48 lessons

Namaste, aap/tum, family, tiffin, kirana, and class Hindi. Games first. Full sentences soon.

Who: Urban children aged 6–10 who rarely speak Hindi, or who only answer in English or a home language.

Done when: The child can greet with aap, name family and school things, buy one item at a kirana, ask for help, and give a one-minute ‘Main kaun hoon’.

Place here when: Place here if the child answers with one word, silence, or only English, and needs pictures and movement.

Teacher note: Keep lessons physical. Never force a child to perform before they have rehearsed with a partner. Do not shame English-medium kids for mixing; pull them into Hindi output.

Tests

StartNamaste, name, point to three things and name them in Hindi.
Mid (after the first test lesson)Name, age, family, one polite request, favourite food.
EndOne-minute ‘Main kaun hoon’ plus kirana or help role-play.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Namaste aur naam 6w, 3m, 6m

Greet with namaste and say your name to the teacher using aap.

Say this: नमस्ते। मेरा नाम आन्या है। — Namaste. My name is Aanya. · आपका नाम क्या है? — What is your name? · आपसे मिलकर अच्छा लगा। — Glad to meet you.

Warm-up. Stand in a circle. Palms together. Teacher walks to two children and says Namaste, mera naam ___. Teach. Hold a name card. Model मेरा नाम ___ है. Show आपका नाम क्या है? as a question face. Keep aap for the teacher. Guided. Ball toss: catch, say मेरा नाम ___, ask the next child आपका / तुम्हारा नाम क्या है? Free. Walk-and-meet: three classmates. Namaste, name, milkar accha laga. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. WhatsApp voice note: नमस्ते। मेरा नाम ___ है।

Slips. मैं नाम आन्या → मेरा नाम आन्या है; आपका नाम क्या हो? → आपका नाम क्या है?

Lesson 02 · Aap kaise hain? 6w, 3m, 6m

Ask and answer how you are, and hear aap vs tum.

Say this: आप कैसे हैं? / आप कैसी हैं? — How are you? (aap) · मैं ठीक हूँ। — I am okay. · मैं थक गया हूँ / थक गई हूँ। — I am tired. · तुम कैसे हो? — How are you? (friend)

Warm-up. Face cards: khush, theek, thaka. Children copy the face. Teach. Teacher uses aap with the class. Friends use tum with each other. Point to gender: थका / थकी. Guided. Two lines. Ask aap to the ‘teacher’ line, tum to the friend line. Free. Door role-play: enter class, namaste, aap kaise hain, sit. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Ask one family member आप कैसे हैं? and report their answer.

Slips. आप कैसे हो? → आप कैसे हैं?; मैं ठीक है → मैं ठीक हूँ

Lesson 03 · Main ladka, main ladki 6w, 3m, 6m

Say I am a boy/girl and match hoon / hai to yourself.

Say this: मैं लड़का हूँ। — I am a boy. · मैं लड़की हूँ। — I am a girl. · मैं छोटा हूँ / छोटी हूँ। — I am small/young.

Warm-up. Two corners: ladke, ladkiyan. Walk to your corner and say मैं ___ हूँ. Teach. Board: हूँ for I, है for he/she. Do not lecture gender of every noun. Guided. Pair: point to a classmate — यह लड़का है / यह लड़की है. Then myself. Free. Circle: name + मैं लड़का/लड़की हूँ + एक पसंद later this week. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: मैं ___ हूँ। मेरा नाम ___ है।

Slips. मैं लड़का है → मैं लड़का हूँ; यह लड़की हूँ → यह लड़की है

Lesson 04 · Ghar ke log 6w, 3m, 6m

Name mummy, papa, bhaiya, didi, nani, dadi, and who you live with.

Say this: यह मेरी मम्मी हैं। — This is my mum. · मेरे एक भाई हैं। — I have one brother. · हम फ्लैट में रहते हैं / रहती हूँ। — We live in a flat / I live.

Warm-up. Quick namaste chain. Teach. Family cards. Touch and say यह मेरे/मेरी ___ हैं. Note हैं for elders. Guided. Children draw family. Point and tell a partner. Free. Gallery walk: visit three drawings. पूछो: यह कौन हैं? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Show a family photo and say three Hindi sentences.

Slips. यह मेरी मम्मी है (teacher: elders often get हैं) → यह मेरी मम्मी हैं; मेरे दो भाई है → मेरे दो भाई हैं

Lesson 05 · Kitne saal? 3m, 6m

Say your age and ask a friend’s age.

Say this: तुम्हारी उम्र क्या है? — How old are you? · मैं आठ साल का हूँ / आठ साल की हूँ। — I am eight.

Warm-up. Birthday-cake picture. Count candles in Hindi. Teach. Model साल का / साल की. Write both on the board. Guided. Age mingle: find someone the same age. Free. Pretend Aadhaar desk: tell the ‘officer’ name and age with aap. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: मैं ___ साल का/की हूँ।

Slips. मैं आठ साल हूँ → मैं आठ साल का हूँ / की हूँ; मेरा उम्र आठ है → मेरी उम्र आठ साल है

Lesson 06 · School bag 6w, 3m, 6m

Name bag things and ask for one politely.

Say this: यह पेंसिल है। — This is a pencil. · मुझे पेंसिल चाहिए। — I need a pencil. · ज़रा देना। — Please give it. · शुक्रिया। — Thanks.

Warm-up. Mystery bag: pull out pencil, copy, bottle, eraser, scale, tiffin. Teach. Teach मुझे ___ चाहिए and ज़रा देना. Thank with शुक्रिया, not only thank you. Guided. Pair request with real bags. Free. Lost-and-found table: ask the teacher with aap. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Pack your bag and name five things in Hindi.

Slips. मुझे पेंसिल है चाहिए → मुझे पेंसिल चाहिए; दे पेंसिल → ज़रा पेंसिल देना

Lesson 07 · Class ki permission 6w, 3m, 6m

Ask to come in, drink water, or say you did not understand.

Say this: अंदर आ सकता हूँ / आ सकती हूँ? — May I come in? · माफ़ कीजिए, समझ नहीं आया / आई। — Sorry, I didn’t get it. · पानी पीने जाऊँ? · एक बार फिर बताइए। — Please say it again.

Warm-up. Silent actions: खड़े हो, बैठो, किताब खोलो. Teach. Four survival lines with gestures. Teacher = aap, classmates = tum. Guided. Freeze game: teacher calls a line, children act the situation. Free. Role-play late arrival, missed word, water. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use one permission line in tomorrow’s class.

Slips. मैं नहीं समझ → समझ नहीं आया / आई; रिपीट करो आप → एक बार फिर बताइए

Lesson 08 · Tiffin mein kya hai 6w, 3m, 6m

Talk about real tiffin food and likes.

Say this: मेरे टिफिन में रोटी है। — I have roti in my tiffin. · मुझे इडली पसंद है। — I like idli. · मुझे करेला पसंद नहीं। — I don’t like bitter gourd.

Warm-up. Smell-and-guess cards: banana, doodh, idli, maggi, poha. Teach. Pasand / pasand nahi with a yes-no board. Keep मुझे. Guided. Tiffin survey: पाँच दोस्तों से पूछो, तुम्हें क्या पसंद है? Free. Class favourite graph by speaking, not writing much. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. At dinner say three food sentences in Hindi.

Slips. मैं इडली पसंद है → मुझे इडली पसंद है; मेरे टिफिन रोटी → मेरे टिफिन में रोटी है

Lesson 09 · Kirana: kitne ka 6w, 3m, 6m

Buy one thing: price, packet, and thank you.

Say this: यह कितने का है? / कितनी की है? — How much is this? · एक पैकेट देना। — One packet, please. · थोड़ा कम कर दीजिए। — A little less, please. · पैसे रखिए। — Keep the money / here you go.

Warm-up. Set a desk kirana: biscuit, toffee, nimbu, chips. Teach. Teach कितने का (m) / कितनी की (f). Teacher models aap with the shopkeeper. Guided. Pair: shopkeeper and child. Swap. Free. Whole-class shop: each child buys one item and pays with paper notes. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. At a real kirana, say यह कितने का है? with a parent.

Slips. यह कितना है पैकेट → यह पैकेट कितने का है?; कम करो तुम uncle → थोड़ा कम कर दीजिए

Lesson 10 · Ginti 1 se 10 6w, 3m, 6m

Count objects and say how many.

Say this: कितने हैं? — How many? · सात हैं। — There are seven. · मेरे पास तीन पेंसिल हैं। — I have three pencils.

Warm-up. Clap-count to 10. Whisper back down. Teach. Fingers, then classroom objects. Model ___ हैं. Guided. Count pencils, bottles, erasers in pairs. Free. Shop tray 1–10. Partner asks कितने हैं? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Count five things at home: ___ हैं।

Slips. सात है पेंसिल → सात पेंसिल हैं; मेरे पास दो पेंसिल है → मेरे पास दो पेंसिल हैं

Lesson 11 · Kapde aaj 3m, 6m

Say what you are wearing.

Say this: मैं नीली शर्ट पहन रही हूँ / पहन रहा हूँ। — I am wearing a blue shirt. · ये मेरे जूते हैं। — These are my shoes. · यह ड्रेस है।

Warm-up. Three children stand; class names colours in Hindi. Teach. Shirt, T-shirt, dress, shoes, socks, pant, skirt. Gender of रंग: नीला / नीली. Guided. Change-one-word: मैं ___ ___ पहन रहा/रही हूँ. Free. Garmi hook: गर्मी है। तुम क्या पहन रहे हो? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tomorrow tell someone what you are wearing in Hindi.

Slips. मैं ब्लू शर्ट पहनता → मैं नीली शर्ट पहन रहा/रही हूँ; यह जूता हैं → ये जूते हैं

Lesson 12 · Rang jo dikh rahe hain 3m, 6m

Name eight colours and say I like / I don’t like.

Say this: यह लाल है। — It is red. · मुझे नीला पसंद है। · मुझे भूरा पसंद नहीं।

Warm-up. Colour hunt: touch something लाल when the teacher says लाल. Teach. Crayons. यह ___ है. Then मुझे ___ पसंद है. Guided. Colour bingo with bags and bottles. Free. Describe a classmate’s bottle without naming them. Class guesses. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Find three colours at home: मुझे ___ दिख रहा है।

Slips. यह लाल → यह लाल है; मुझे नीला पसंद हूँ → मुझे नीला पसंद है

Lesson 13 · Shareer aur dard 3m, 6m

Name body parts and say something hurts for the school nurse.

Say this: यह मेरी नाक है। — This is my nose. · मेरे पेट में दर्द है। — My stomach hurts. · सिर दुख रहा है। — I have a headache.

Warm-up. Touch game: नाक, कान, आँख, हाथ, पैर, पेट, सिर. Teach. Nurse line: मेरे ___ में दर्द है. Partner: बैठो, पानी लो. Guided. Simon-style: only Hindi body words. Sit if they wish. Free. Clinic corner: three children visit the nurse. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Teach a sibling three body words in Hindi.

Slips. मेरा पेट दर्द है → मेरे पेट में दर्द है; यह नाक → यह मेरी नाक है

Lesson 14 · Ghar ke kamre 6w, 3m, 6m

Say I live in a flat/house and name rooms.

Say this: मैं फ्लैट में रहता हूँ / रहती हूँ। — I live in a flat. · यह किचन है। — This is the kitchen. · मेरा कमरा छोटा है।

Warm-up. Four chairs as rooms: kitchen, bedroom, bathroom, hall. Teach. Teach फ्लैट, मकान, किचन, बेडरूम, बाथरूम, हॉल, बालकनी. Guided. Point to chair-rooms: यह ___ है. Free. 30-second home tour to a partner. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Walk through home and name three rooms.

Slips. मैं फ्लैट रहता → मैं फ्लैट में रहता/रहती हूँ; यह किचन → यह किचन है

Lesson 15 · Kahan hai? 3m, 6m

Use mein, par, neeche, paas with classroom things.

Say this: किताब मेज़ पर है। — The book is on the table. · बैग कुर्सी के नीचे है। · दरवाज़े के पास है।

Warm-up. Hide a pencil. गरम / ठंडा until you teach the new words. Teach. Place a bottle में, पर, नीचे, पास. Model slowly. Guided. Teacher places; pairs say the sentence. Swap. Free. Treasure hunt: four hidden objects, speak the location. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Hide something at home and tell a parent कहाँ है।

Slips. किताब मेज़ है → किताब मेज़ पर है; बैग में कुर्सी → बैग कुर्सी के नीचे है

Lesson 16 · Colony ka khel 6w, 3m, 6m

Ask to join a game and name cricket, cycling, lagori, catch.

Say this: मुझे भी खेलना है। — I want to play too. · चलो क्रिकेट खेलें। — Let’s play cricket. · गेंद इधर फेंको। — Throw the ball here.

Warm-up. Show-and-tell one small toy or a drawing of a game. Teach. Teach मुझे भी and चलो. Kind voices. Tum with friends. Guided. Share-circle: ask, give, thank. Free. Playground role-play: cricket, skipping, cycling. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Ask someone at home: मुझे भी खेलना है।

Slips. मुझे खेल → मुझे भी खेलना है; दे गेंद → गेंद ज़रा देना

Lesson 17 · Shukriya, maaf kijiye, please 6w, 3m, 6m

Use please, thanks, and sorry with the right person.

Say this: कृपया / प्लीज़ ज़रा हटो। — Please move a bit. · शुक्रिया / धन्यवाद। · माफ़ कीजिए। — Sorry (aap). · सॉरी यार। — Sorry (friend).

Warm-up. Bump-into mime. Freeze. Choose the line. Teach. Board: teacher/elder = माफ़ कीजिए; friend = सॉरी. Both are real. Guided. Card sort: who do you say this to? Free. Corridor role-play: stepped on a bag, late, borrowed a pencil. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use शुक्रिया once today at home or in the building.

Slips. माफ़ करो सर → माफ़ कीजिए; थैंक यू ओनली → शुक्रिया

Lesson 18 · Aaj, kal, parson 3m, 6m

Say today, tomorrow, yesterday and the day name.

Say this: आज सोमवार है। — Today is Monday. · कल मंगलवार है। — Tomorrow is Tuesday. · कल मैं घर पर था / थी। — Yesterday I was at home.

Warm-up. Seven spots on the floor. Jump to आज. Teach. Chant days. Mark रविवार as a home day for many children. Teach कल = yesterday or tomorrow from context; add बीता कल if needed. Guided. What day is drawing / cricket / holiday? Free. Spoken week: each child one day + one activity. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Every morning this week: आज ___ है।

Slips. आज सोमवार → आज सोमवार है; मैं स्कूल जाता सोमवार → मैं सोमवार को स्कूल जाता/जाती हूँ

Lesson 19 · Garmi, sardi, baarish 3m, 6m

Describe weather and what you need.

Say this: आज गर्मी है। — It is hot today. · बारिश हो रही है। — It is raining. · मुझे छाता चाहिए। — I need an umbrella.

Warm-up. Window look or phone photo: sun, rain, grey. Teach. Teach गर्मी, सर्दी, बारिश, धूप, छाता, स्वेटर. Guided. If-then pairs: बारिश → छाता; सर्दी → स्वेटर. Free. Weather news: one child is the reporter. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tomorrow’s weather line on a voice note.

Slips. आज गरम है बहुत ओनली → आज बहुत गर्मी है; बारिश है हो → बारिश हो रही है

Lesson 20 · Paani, bathroom, lift 3m, 6m

Ask for water, the bathroom, or the lift in a building.

Say this: मुझे पानी चाहिए। · बाथरूम कहाँ है? · लिफ्ट कौन से फ्लोर पे जाएगी? — Which floor is the lift going to? · सीढ़ी से चलते हैं।

Warm-up. Walk the room as a building: door, lift corner, bathroom door. Teach. Survival lines with aap for a guard or aunty. Guided. Pair: visitor in a society lobby. Free. Role-play: thirsty, need bathroom, wrong floor. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use one line in your building this week.

Slips. बाथरूम कहाँ → बाथरूम कहाँ है?; मुझे पानी है → मुझे पानी चाहिए

Lesson 21 · Dost 3m, 6m

Talk about a friend and invite them.

Say this: यह मेरा दोस्त है / मेरी सहेली है। · तुम मेरे साथ बैठोगे / बैठोगी? · चल, साथ चलते हैं।

Warm-up. Name one friend in the room. Teach. दोस्त (often boys/mixed), सहेली (girl friend). Don’t police this too hard; teach both. Guided. Invite and accept / say later. Free. Playdate phone: two lines only. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Invite one person in Hindi today.

Slips. यह मेरा फ्रेंड है ओनली → यह मेरा दोस्त है; तुम साथ बैठ → तुम मेरे साथ बैठोगे?

Lesson 22 · Review aur test 1 6w, 3m, 6m

Reuse namaste, name, family, tiffin, and one shop line.

Say this: नमस्ते। मेरा नाम ___ है। घर में ___ हैं। मुझे ___ पसंद है। यह कितने का है?

Warm-up. Station rotation: 2 minutes each — name, family, shop. Teach. Teacher notes who still needs aap repair. Guided. Pairs run the four lines without reading. Free. Mini test: 90 seconds + one kirana ask. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: the four lines standing up.

Slips. Only English words → Keep the Hindi frames even if one word is English

Lesson 23 · Ginti 11 se 20 aur paise 3m, 6m

Count 11–20 and say a small price.

Say this: पंद्रह रुपये। — Fifteen rupees. · बीस रुपये का पैकेट। · वापस पाँच रुपये दीजिए। — Please give five rupees back.

Warm-up. Clap 11–20. Teach. Paper notes: 5, 10, 20. Change language: वापस. Guided. Pair shop with teensy prices. Free. Kirana 2.0: buy two things, add, take change. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Read one price on a packet at home in Hindi.

Slips. पंद्रह रुपया → पंद्रह रुपये; वापस पैसे दो तुम → वापस पैसे दीजिए

Lesson 24 · Kitne baje? 3m, 6m

Tell school-ish time: o’clock and half.

Say this: कितने बजे हैं? — What time is it? · आठ बजे हैं। — It is eight. · साढ़े तीन बजे स्कूल जाता हूँ / जाती हूँ।

Warm-up. Clock arms with cardboard or hands. Teach. Teach बजे, साढ़े, पौने only if the class is ready; else stick to बजे. Guided. Daily timetable: school, tiffin, home. Free. Late-to-school role-play: माफ़ कीजिए, बस लेट थी। Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Say the time you wake up.

Slips. आठ बजा है → आठ बजे हैं; मैं स्कूल जाता आठ → मैं आठ बजे स्कूल जाता/जाती हूँ

Lesson 25 · Mera din 3m, 6m

Tell a simple weekday: wake, school, home, sleep.

Say this: मैं सुबह सात बजे उठता हूँ / उठती हूँ। · फिर स्कूल जाता हूँ। · शाम को मैं होमवर्क करता हूँ / करती हूँ।

Warm-up. Four pictures on the board: उठना, स्कूल, खाना, सोना. Teach. Chain with फिर. Gender of उठा/उठी later; today हूँ forms. Guided. Partner day in 40 seconds. Free. Class chain story: each child adds one step. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell your evening in two Hindi sentences.

Slips. मैं उठता है → मैं उठता/उठती हूँ; फिर मैं स्कूल → फिर मैं स्कूल जाता/जाती हूँ

Lesson 26 · Nashta, khana, raat ka khana 3m, 6m

Name meals and say what you eat.

Say this: नाश्ते में मैं पराठा खाता हूँ / खाती हूँ। · खाना कब है? · मुझे भूख लगी है। — I am hungry.

Warm-up. Meal cards: poha, idli, dal-chawal, maggi, fruit. Teach. भूख लगी / प्यास लगी. Not मैं Hungry हूँ. Guided. Canteen line: मुझे ___ देना. Free. Family dinner table: pass, thank, I don’t want. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. At one meal, say मुझे ___ चाहिए।

Slips. मैं भूख हूँ → मुझे भूख लगी है; नाश्ता मैं डोसा → नाश्ते में मैं डोसा खाता/खाती हूँ

Lesson 27 · Main kar sakta hoon 3m, 6m

Say I can / I can’t for real child skills.

Say this: मैं साइकिल चला सकता हूँ / सकती हूँ। · मैं तैर नहीं सकता / सकती। · क्या तुम यह कर सकते हो?

Warm-up. Mime: cycling, swimming, reading, tying laces. Teach. सकता / सकती. Don’t teach the whole verb chart. Guided. Survey five friends. Free. Talent show: one can, one can’t, no shame. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक सकता, एक नहीं।

Slips. मैं कर सकता है → मैं कर सकता/सकती हूँ; मैं नहीं सकता तैर → मैं तैर नहीं सकता/सकती

Lesson 28 · Meri school 3m, 6m

Name school places: class, ground, office, canteen, washroom.

Say this: मेरी स्कूल बड़ी है। · ग्राउंड वहाँ है। · ऑफिस में जाकर बोलो।

Warm-up. Map the room as a school. Teach. Places + कहाँ है. Guided. Give a visitor a 30-second tour. Free. Lost new child: help them find class 3B. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Name three school places to a parent.

Slips. स्कूल मेरा बड़ा → मेरी स्कूल बड़ी है; ग्राउंड है वहाँ ओनली → ग्राउंड वहाँ है

Lesson 29 · Teacher se baat 6m

Speak to a teacher with aap and ji.

Say this: मैम / सर, एक मिनट। · मेरा होमवर्क पूरा है। · मुझे छुट्टी चाहिए, घर में मेहमान हैं।

Warm-up. Practise standing, hands still, aap. Teach. Repair तुम to आप. Add जी if it helps: मैम जी. Guided. Three situations: homework, leave, didn’t understand. Free. Hot-seat teacher: children take turns. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use one aap line with a real teacher this week.

Slips. मैम तुम सुनो → मैम, एक मिनट सुनिए; होमवर्क किया मैं → मैंने होमवर्क कर लिया है

Lesson 30 · Chhoti phone call 3m, 6m

Make a 20-second call to a friend or a parent.

Say this: हेलो, मैं कबीर बोल रहा हूँ / रही हूँ। · मम्मी घर हैं क्या? · पाँच मिनट में आता हूँ / आती हूँ।

Warm-up. Phone mime. Hold, listen, then speak. Teach. Start with नाम. Don’t teach the whole telecom script. Guided. Pair calls sitting back to back. Free. Wrong number + sorry + bye. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note that sounds like a real call.

Slips. मैं कबीर है → मैं कबीर बोल रहा/रही हूँ; मम्मी है घर → मम्मी घर हैं क्या?

Lesson 31 · Madad chahiye 3m, 6m

Ask for help when something is lost or hard.

Say this: मेरी मदद कीजिए। — Please help me. · मेरी बोतल खो गई। · यह कैसे खोलें?

Warm-up. Hide a bottle. Panic mime, then language. Teach. मदद कीजिए (aap) vs मदद करो (tum). Guided. Pairs: lost, stuck zip, can’t open tiffin. Free. Guard or teacher help role-play. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Ask for one real help at home in Hindi.

Slips. मेरी मदद करो सर → मेरी मदद कीजिए; बोतल लॉस्ट → मेरी बोतल खो गई

Lesson 32 · Ghar ke paas kya hai 3m, 6m

Name park, shop, temple/mosque/church, bus stop near home.

Say this: मेरे घर के पास पार्क है। · किराना दूकान नीचे है। · बस स्टॉप थोड़ा दूर है।

Warm-up. Draw a street with four places. Teach. के पास / के नीचे / थोड़ा दूर. Guided. Partner guesses which colony picture. Free. Give directions from gate to park: सीधे, बाएँ, दाएँ — preview. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Name three nearby places to a parent.

Slips. घर पास पार्क → घर के पास पार्क है; दूकान है नीचे ओनली → किराना दूकान नीचे है

Lesson 33 · Shaniwar, Ravivar 3m, 6m

Talk about Saturday and Sunday without a script.

Say this: शनिवार को मुझे छुट्टी होती है। · रविवार को हम नानी के घर जाते हैं। · मैं टीवी देखता हूँ / देखती हूँ।

Warm-up. Weekend collage of pictures: mall, park, sleep, homework. Teach. को for days. जाते हैं for family we. Guided. Interview: last Sunday two sentences. Free. Plan next Sunday: मैं ___ करना चाहता/चाहती हूँ. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: last Sunday, two lines.

Slips. शनिवार मैं छुट्टी → शनिवार को मुझे छुट्टी होती है; हम जाते नानी → हम नानी के घर जाते हैं

Lesson 34 · Tyohar 3m, 6m

Talk about a festival the child actually celebrates.

Say this: हम दिवाली मनाते हैं। / हम ईद मनाते हैं। / हम क्रिसमस मनाते हैं। · मीठा बहुत खाया। · पटाखे नहीं छोड़ने चाहिए — if the class wants this line.

Warm-up. Festival pictures: diya, eid moon, tree, pongal pot, gurpurab. All welcome. Teach. Allow ‘hum nahi manate’ as a full answer. Guided. Pair: what you wear, what you eat. Free. Class circle: one sentence each, no ranking of festivals. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell one festival sentence at home.

Slips. हम मनाते दिवाली → हम दिवाली मनाते हैं; मीठा खाया बहुत ओनली → मैंने बहुत मीठा खाया

Lesson 35 · Market walk 6m

Walk a market: look, ask, don’t buy yet.

Say this: यह ताज़ा है क्या? · थोड़ा और दिखाइए। · अभी नहीं, धन्यवाद।

Warm-up. Desk market: fruit, flowers, plastic toys. Teach. Polite no is a full skill. Guided. Pairs browse and refuse kindly. Free. Family market: child speaks, parent is silent helper. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use अभी नहीं once this week.

Slips. यह फ्रेश है → यह ताज़ा है क्या?; नहीं चाहिए रुक → अभी नहीं, धन्यवाद

Lesson 36 · Tasveer dekho 3m, 6m

Describe a street or classroom photo.

Say this: मुझे एक गाड़ी दिख रही है। · लड़की बैग पकड़े हुए है। · आकाश में बादल हैं।

Warm-up. One big photo on a phone. Teach. दिख रहा / रही. Count three things. Guided. Pairs: I see, you add. Free. Mystery photo: class guesses the place. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Describe one photo at home in three lines.

Slips. मैं देखता गाड़ी → मुझे एक गाड़ी दिख रही है; बादल है → बादल हैं

Lesson 37 · Feelings jo asli hain 3m, 6m

Name khush, gussa, dar, udaas, sharma without baby voices.

Say this: मैं खुश हूँ। · मुझे गुस्सा आ रहा है। · मुझे डर लग रहा है।

Warm-up. Face cards again, older words. Teach. मुझे ___ आ रहा है vs मैं ___ हूँ. Guided. Story cards: lost toy, won a race, dark room. Free. Partner comfort: कोई बात नहीं, मैं साथ हूँ. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell a parent one real feeling in Hindi.

Slips. मैं डर हूँ → मुझे डर लग रहा है; मैं गुस्सा है → मुझे गुस्सा आ रहा है

Lesson 38 · Pasand, nahi pasand 6w, 3m, 6m

Give a favourite with because (kyunki) in one short reason.

Say this: मुझे आम सबसे पसंद है, क्योंकि मीठा है। · मुझे यह शो पसंद नहीं, क्योंकि बोरिंग है।

Warm-up. Favourite vote: fruit, game, subject. Teach. क्योंकि + one word is enough. Guided. Speed likes: 20 seconds each. Free. Hot-seat: class asks क्यों? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: one like + kyunki.

Slips. मुझे पसंद आम क्योंकि → मुझे आम पसंद है क्योंकि...; बोरिंग है मुझे → मुझे यह पसंद नहीं क्योंकि बोरिंग है

Lesson 39 · Kaun kahan hai 6m

Ask where people are at home.

Say this: पापा कहाँ हैं? · मम्मी किचन में हैं। · भाइया अभी आ रहे हैं।

Warm-up. House chairs again. Teach. हैं for elders and plural. आ रहे / रही. Guided. Phone to home: who is where. Free. Lost-in-mall: announce a missing person kindly. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Ask at home: ___ कहाँ हैं?

Slips. पापा कहाँ है → पापा कहाँ हैं?; मम्मी किचन → मम्मी किचन में हैं

Lesson 40 · Khel ke saath revision 3m, 6m

Reuse old lines in games, no new grammar.

Say this: Any spine sentences from weeks 1–8.

Warm-up. Board race: aap or tum? Teach. Stations: shop, nurse, namaste, tiffin. Guided. Keep moving. Short turns. Free. Winner is the pair that keeps speaking, not the ‘correct’ pair. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Pick one weak line and record it twice.

Slips. Stopping to write rules → Keep mouths moving

Lesson 41 · Kho gayi bottle 6m

Tell a four-line story: lost bottle, asked, found, thanked.

Say this: मेरी बोतल खो गई। · मैंने मैम से पूछा। · वह कैंटीन में मिली। · शुक्रिया।

Warm-up. Pictures in order. Teach. गई / गया by gender of bottle (f) and of speaker if needed. Keep it light. Guided. Retell with a partner, then swap names. Free. New ending: didn’t find it, bought another. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell the story to someone at home.

Slips. बोतल लॉस्ट हो गया → बोतल खो गई; मैं पूछा मैम → मैंने मैम से पूछा

Lesson 42 · Tu kab nahi 6m

Hear when tu sounds rude, and practise not using it with teachers.

Say this: तुम बैठो। — Sit (friend). · आप बैठिए। — Please sit (aap). · तू बैठ — only if you are very close; not for class.

Warm-up. Three puppets: friend, teacher, angry child. Teach. Children sort lines. No shame if home uses tu with kids; school default is tum/aap. Guided. Repair clinic: rewrite rude lines. Free. Role-play: child almost says tu to a guard, switches to aap. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Catch yourself once this week: aap with an adult.

Slips. सर तू देख → सर, आप देखिए; तुम बैठिए मैम → मैम, आप बैठिए

Lesson 43 · Main kaun hoon — taiyari 6w, 3m, 6m

Prepare a one-minute talk: name, age, family, like, school.

Say this: नमस्ते। मेरा नाम ___ है। मैं ___ साल का/की हूँ। मैं फ्लैट में रहता/रहती हूँ। मुझे ___ पसंद है। मैं ___ स्कूल जाता/जाती हूँ।

Warm-up. Listen to the teacher’s one-minute model. Teach. Keywords only, not a paragraph to read. Guided. Rehearse with a partner. Partner asks one question. Free. Two volunteers. Clap for courage. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise twice at home, standing.

Slips. Reading in a tiny voice → Look up and speak; Only name and stop → Add pasand and school

Lesson 44 · Jodi interview 3m, 6m

Ask five questions and report about a partner.

Say this: तुम्हारा नाम क्या है? · तुम्हारी उम्र क्या है? · तुम्हें क्या पसंद है? · तुम स्कूल कैसे आते हो / आती हो?

Warm-up. Question-word race: क्या, कैसे, कहाँ, कौन. Teach. Build a five-question interview. Tum with peers. Guided. Interview, then switch. Free. Report: यह नील है। यह नौ साल का है। इसे ___ पसंद है। Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Interview one family member in Hindi.

Slips. यह नाम नील है → इसका नाम नील है; उसे पसंद है क्रिकेट ओनली → उसे क्रिकेट पसंद है

Lesson 45 · Class manch 6w, 3m, 6m

Perform Main kaun hoon for the group.

Say this: The one-minute talk from the last lesson.

Warm-up. Breathing: flower in, candle out. Teach. Smile, stand, speak to a face. Guided. Half perform; half is a kind audience. Free. Certificates of courage, not marks. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell someone at home what you did.

Slips. Correcting every error on stage → Note two strengths and one next step privately

Lesson 46 · Aakhri bolne ka test 6w, 3m, 6m

Complete a short spoken test and feel proud.

Say this: Teacher questions in full sentences.

Warm-up. Quiet game while individuals test. Teach. Test: namaste, name, age, family, favourite, one role-play (kirana or help). Guided. Under four minutes. Warm voice. Free. Circle: मैं हिंदी बोल सकता/सकती हूँ. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. No homework. Start Elementary when ready.

Slips. Failing a child for accent → Score courage, sentences, and understanding

Lesson 47 · Auto aur bus ki ek line 6m

Say one travel line with a parent: stop, wait, sit.

Say this: यहीं रुकिए। — Stop here. · एक टिकट, चांदनी चौक। · अंदर बैठ जाओ।

Warm-up. Chairs as auto. Driver in front. Teach. Child speaks to driver with aap; parent is the silent wallet. Guided. Pair: wrong stop, say आगे जाना है. Free. Whole class auto: each child one stop name from their city. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Say यहीं रुकिए once on a real ride if safe.

Slips. यहाँ रुक तुम → यहीं रुकिए; टिकट एक दे → एक टिकट दीजिए

Lesson 48 · Janmdin 3m, 6m

Invite and thank at a birthday.

Say this: मेरा जन्मदिन है। · तुम आओगे / आओगी? · केक काटो। शुक्रिया, आए।

Warm-up. Paper cake. Sing if they want — in Hindi or mix. Teach. Invite + polite no: मैं नहीं आ सकती, घर जाना है. Guided. Party roles: host, guest, late guest. Free. Thank-you circle. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Invite one person, even as practice at home.

Slips. तुम आ मेरे बर्थडे → तुम मेरे जन्मदिन पर आओगे?; केक दे → केक ज़रा देना

Urban Spoken Hindi · Children · Sheher ki baat

Children · Sheher ki baat

Children 6–10 Elementary 33 lessons

School day, last Sunday, doctor, metro with a parent, and a two-minute city-life talk.

Who: Children who can already say name, age, and a few likes in Hindi, and can follow a simple spoken class.

Done when: The child can tell a school day, a Sunday, give colony directions, visit a doctor or canteen in role-play, and speak for two minutes.

Place here when: Place here if they already use short Hindi sentences and can do the Children’s Beginner final test.

Teacher note: Start past time (gaya / gayi, kiya / ki) and kyunki. Still use pictures. Still play. Teach gender only when it changes the day’s sentence.

Tests

Start40-second ‘me’, describe a picture, last Sunday in two sentences.
MidSchool day, directions, last weekend.
EndTwo-minute city-life talk plus shop or doctor.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Lamba hello 6w, 3m, 6m

Greet, ask a follow-up, and keep a short Hindi chat going.

Say this: आज कैसा रहा? — How was today? · मेरा दिन अच्छा था। तुम्हारा? · कल क्या किया?

Warm-up. Circle: name + one feeling. Teach. Show that one-word अच्छा stops the chat. Add one fact. Guided. Speed meetings: 30 seconds, rotate. Free. Keep a talk going for one minute without the teacher. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Start a two-line chat at home.

Slips. अच्छा। तुम? → मेरा दिन अच्छा था। तुम्हारा कैसा रहा?; कल कुछ नहीं → कल मैं घर पर था/थी, टीवी देखा

Lesson 02 · Mera sheher, meri gali 6w, 3m, 6m

Describe floor, lift or stairs, neighbours, and the gali.

Say this: हम तीसरी मंज़िल पर रहते हैं। · पड़ोसी की आंटी अच्छी हैं। · गली में कुत्ता रहता है।

Warm-up. Build a tower of books as floors. Teach. मंज़िल, लिफ्ट, सीढ़ी, गली, सोसाइटी. Guided. Partner tour from gate to door. Free. New cousin visits: explain the building. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Three building sentences to a parent.

Slips. हम रहते थर्ड फ्लोर ओनली → हम तीसरी मंज़िल पर रहते हैं; आंटी अच्छा है → आंटी अच्छी हैं

Lesson 03 · Poora school din 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell a school day with pehle, phir, uske baad.

Say this: पहले असेम्बली होती है। · फिर गणित की घंटी। · उसके बाद टिफिन।

Warm-up. Bell mime. Sequence cards. Teach. Linkers on the board. Past optional: आज हुआ. Guided. Retell partner’s day. Free. A day that went wrong: late, no tiffin, rain. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: your real timetable, four lines.

Slips. पहले असेम्बली → पहले असेम्बली होती है; फिर मैं गणित → फिर गणित की क्लास होती है

Lesson 04 · Colony ke log 3m, 6m

Name watchman, doodhwala, neighbour, lift-wala with aap.

Say this: सरकार भैया, गाड़ी आने दो। · आंटी, गेंद इधर गिर गई। · दुद्धवाले अंकल, एक लीटर देना।

Warm-up. Hats or badges for roles. Teach. Aap + name/role. Urban mixed words stay. Guided. Four mini role-plays. Free. Complaint without rudeness: शोर बहुत हो रहा है. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Greet one building person in Hindi.

Slips. वॉचमैन तू खोल → भैया, गेट खोल दीजिए; आंटी गेंद दे → आंटी, गेंद ज़रा दीजिए

Lesson 05 · Canteen order 6w, 3m, 6m

Order, wait, and say it is less/more/spicy.

Say this: एक समोसा और एक जूस। · मिर्ची कम रखिए। · यह ठंडा नहीं है।

Warm-up. Menu on the board with prices. Teach. Order as a list. Complaint stays aap. Guided. Pair canteen. Swap. Free. Queue, money, missing item. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Order one real snack in Hindi this week.

Slips. समोसा एक दे → एक समोसा दीजिए; यह स्पाइसी बहुत → यह बहुत तीखा है

Lesson 06 · Seedhe, baaye, daaye 6w, 3m, 6m

Give directions inside a colony or school.

Say this: सीधे जाइए। · बाएँ मुड़िए। · दूसरी गली में पार्क है।

Warm-up. Tape arrows on the floor. Teach. इए endings with aap. Friends can use जाओ, मुड़ो. Guided. Blindfold walk with spoken directions (safe room). Free. From gate to tuition: full route. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Give a parent directions from the lift to your door.

Slips. लेफ्ट जाओ आप → बाएँ मुड़िए; पार्क है दूसरी → दूसरी गली में पार्क है

Lesson 07 · Pichhla Itvaar 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell last Sunday with gaya/gayi and kiya/ki.

Say this: रविवार को मैं पार्क गया / गई। · हमने आइसक्रीम खाई। · मैंने होमवर्क नहीं किया।

Warm-up. Three photo prompts. Teach. Agree gender with the speaker: गया/गई. खाई with ice cream (f) if you want one clean example. Guided. Pair interviews. Free. Lie detector: two true, one false. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: last Sunday, four sentences.

Slips. मैं गया पार्क → मैं पार्क गया/गई; हम खाया आइसक्रीम → हमने आइसक्रीम खाई

Lesson 08 · Is hafte ka plan 3m, 6m

Say a plan with wala/wali and chahna.

Say this: मैं क्रिकेट खेलने वाला हूँ / वाली हूँ। · हम मॉल जाएँगे। · शायद ट्यूशन रहेगा।

Warm-up. Diary page on the board. Teach. Allow शायद and पता नहीं as full answers. Guided. Weekend planner pairs. Free. Change of plan: नहीं जा पाऊँगा / पाऊँगी. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell one real plan for this week.

Slips. मैं जाने वाला मॉल → मैं मॉल जाने वाला/वाली हूँ; वी विल गो → हम जाएँगे

Lesson 09 · Mausam ke kapde 3m, 6m

Choose clothes for rain, heat, and a function.

Say this: बारिश है, रेनकोट पहन। · शादी है, अच्छे कपड़े पहनो। · यह छोटा हो गया है।

Warm-up. Clothes pile or pictures. Teach. Function vs play clothes. Tum with sibling, aap with guest. Guided. Pack a bag for a trip aloud. Free. Mother-child role-play: argue kindly about a jacket. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tomorrow explain your clothes in two lines.

Slips. रैन है पहन जैकेट → बारिश हो रही है, जैकेट पहन; कपड़ा छोटा गया → यह कपड़ा छोटा हो गया है

Lesson 10 · Doctor ke paas 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell a doctor what hurts and since when.

Say this: डॉक्टर साहब, दो दिन से खांसी है। · बुखार आया था। · दवा कब खानी है?

Warm-up. Clinic setup: chair, paper ‘prescription’. Teach. से for since. खानी / खाना with दवाई (f) — keep one clean pair. Guided. Parent + child + doctor triangle. Free. Chemist after: यह दवाई दिन में दो बार. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Role-play once at home with a parent.

Slips. मुझे कफ़ है दो दिन → दो दिन से खांसी है; दवाई कब खाना → दवाई कब खानी है?

Lesson 11 · Metro ya bus ke saath 3m, 6m

Ride with a parent: card, door, seat, stop.

Say this: कार्ड लगाइए। · दरवाज़ा बंद हो रहा है। · यहाँ सीट खाली है क्या? · अगला स्टेशन ___ है।

Warm-up. Tape a metro door. Beep sound. Teach. Public lines stay aap. Don’t push, don’t shout. Guided. Pairs: lost parent announcement with dignity. Free. Whole-class metro: each stop a child names. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use one metro/bus line on a real ride.

Slips. कार्ड लगा तुम → कार्ड लगाइए; सीट खाली मैं लेता → यह सीट खाली है क्या?

Lesson 12 · Dost ko phone 3m, 6m

Call a friend to plan, cancel, or say you are late.

Say this: मैं लेट हूँ, ट्रैफिक है। · आज नहीं आ पाऊँगा / पाऊँगी। · कल चार बजे पार्क?

Warm-up. Back-to-back phones. Teach. Cancel without ghosting. Tum with friends. Guided. Three calls: plan, late, cancel. Free. Group plan: four children, one time. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Send a real voice note to a friend in Hindi if allowed.

Slips. मैं लेट है → मैं लेट हूँ; नहीं आ सकता आज ओनली → आज नहीं आ पाऊँगा/पाऊँगी

Lesson 13 · Sheher ki pasand jagah 6w, 3m, 6m

Talk about a favourite city place with kyunki.

Say this: मुझे नेहरू पार्क पसंद है क्योंकि झूला है। · मॉल में ठंडक है, भीड़ भी है। · नदी किनारे हम नहीं जाते, दूर है।

Warm-up. Phone photos: park, mall, metro, lake, bookstore. Teach. Place + kyunki + one problem (honest). Guided. Pairs convince each other. Free. Class vote after talks, not before. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: favourite place, 40 seconds.

Slips. मुझे पार्क लाइक → मुझे पार्क पसंद है; क्योंकि झूला → क्योंकि वहाँ झूला है

Lesson 14 · Tasveer se kahani 3m, 6m

Make a three-picture story with pehle / phir / last mein.

Say this: पहले लड़का स्कूल जा रहा था। · फिर बारिश शुरू हुई। · आखिर में वह भीग गया / गई।

Warm-up. Three picture cards per pair. Teach. Present continuous जा रहा था is fine if they can; else गया. Guided. Tell, then change the ending. Free. Audience asks one क्यों? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Draw three boxes and tell the story at home.

Slips. पहले लड़का जाना स्कूल → पहले लड़का स्कूल जा रहा था; लास्ट में वह वेट → आखिर में वह भीग गया/गई

Lesson 15 · Do cheezon mein fark 3m, 6m

Compare two things: school lunch vs home, park vs mall.

Say this: घर का खाना अच्छा लगता है, कैंटीन महँगा है। · पार्क में हवा है, मॉल में AC है। · मुझे यह ज़्यादा पसंद है।

Warm-up. Two objects on the table. Teach. ज़्यादा / कम / लेकिन. Guided. Compare two friends’ tiffins kindly — no body talk. Free. City vs building playground. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Compare two snacks in three sentences.

Slips. यह ज़्यादा गुड → यह ज़्यादा अच्छा है; मॉल बेटर है → मॉल ज़्यादा ठंडा है लेकिन भीड़ है

Lesson 16 · Ghar aur school ke niyam 3m, 6m

Say must / must not for real rules.

Say this: जूते बाहर उतारो। · क्लास में दौड़ना नहीं है। · फोन रात को नहीं चलाना।

Warm-up. Rule or not? Thumbs. Teach. नहीं है / मत / चाहिए. Keep it spoken, not a grammar list. Guided. Invent a kind class rule together. Free. Role-play a child who broke a rule and repairs it. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell one home rule in Hindi.

Slips. नॉट रन इन क्लास → क्लास में दौड़ना नहीं है; जूता उतार → जूते बाहर उतारो

Lesson 17 · Ghar pe madad 6m

Offer help at home without baby talk.

Say this: मैं टेबल लगा दूँ? · कचरा फेंक आता हूँ / आती हूँ। · पानी भर दूँ?

Warm-up. Mime chores: table, dustbin, water, folding. Teach. दूँ / दूँगी later; today simple offers. Guided. Parent tired: child offers two helps. Free. Society dustbin run as a tiny play. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Offer one real help at home in Hindi.

Slips. मैं हेल्प करूँ → मैं मदद करूँ?; कचरा थ्रो → मैं कचरा फेंक आता/आती हूँ

Lesson 18 · Mall ka din 3m, 6m

Talk through a mall: lift, toilet, food court, lost.

Say this: फूड कोर्ट ऊपर है। · टॉयलेट उसी फ्लोर पर है। · मैं रास्ता भूल गया / गई।

Warm-up. Mall map on paper. Teach. Lost-child line for a counter: मेरी मम्मी का फ़ोन ___ है. Guided. Family plan: where to meet if lost. Free. Food-court order revision. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Family meeting-point sentence at home.

Slips. मैं लॉस्ट हो गया → मैं रास्ता भूल गया/गई; टॉयलेट कहाँ यार गार्ड → भैया, टॉयलेट कहाँ है?

Lesson 19 · Bulao aur na bolo 6w, 3m, 6m

Invite and refuse kindly.

Say this: चल न, मेरे घर आ जा। · आज मन नहीं है। · अगली बार ज़रूर।

Warm-up. Invitation cards. Teach. Kind no is complete. No fake yes. Guided. Party, tuition, sleepover (if the class does this). Free. Group: one host, many answers. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise a kind no with a parent.

Slips. नहीं यार रुड → आज नहीं आ पाऊँगा, अगली बार; चल मेरे घर → चल, मेरे घर आ जा

Lesson 20 · Cartoon, YouTube, game 3m, 6m

Talk about what they actually watch or play.

Say this: मैं ___ देखता हूँ / देखती हूँ। · यह वाला लेवल मुश्किल है। · मम्मी फ़ोन रखवा देती हैं।

Warm-up. Title storm on the board — Hindi sentences around English names are fine. Teach. Screen-time fight as language, not a lecture. Guided. Recommend one show in 30 seconds. Free. Debate lite: फ़ोन कितनी देर? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Recommend one thing in a voice note.

Slips. मैं वॉच करता → मैं ___ देखता/देखती हूँ; गेम हार्ड है → यह लेवल मुश्किल है

Lesson 21 · Jab main chhota tha 3m, 6m

One memory from when they were smaller.

Say this: जब मैं छोटा था / छोटी थी, मुझे अंधेरा लगता था। · पहले मैं साइकिल नहीं चला पाता था / पाती थी।

Warm-up. Baby photo optional, or a drawing. Teach. था / थी. Dignity: no mocking old fears. Guided. Pair memories. Free. Then and now: अब मैं ___ सकता/सकती हूँ. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Ask a parent one ‘when you were small’ story and retell.

Slips. जब मैं स्मॉल → जब मैं छोटा था/छोटी थी; मुझे डर लगता डार्क → मुझे अंधेरा लगता था

Lesson 22 · Khoya-paya 6m

Report a lost thing at the school office.

Say this: सर, मेरी पानी की बोतल खो गई। · नीली है, नाम लिखा है। · अगर मिले तो बताइएगा।

Warm-up. Lost-and-found box. Teach. Describe colour + name sticker. Aap in office. Guided. Write nothing; speak the report. Free. Found something: यह किसकी है? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise the three lines standing.

Slips. माई बॉटल लॉस्ट → मेरी बोतल खो गई; नीला है बोतल → बोतल नीली है

Lesson 23 · Nimbu pani banana 6m

Give and follow cooking steps.

Say this: पहले पानी लो। · फिर नींबू निचोड़ो। · थोड़ी चीनी और बर्फ।

Warm-up. Real lemon if possible, else mime. Teach. पहले / फिर / थोड़ा. Commands to a friend: tum. Guided. Partner makes it from speech only. Free. Change the recipe: नमक, पुदीना. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Make it at home and narrate.

Slips. फर्स्ट वाटर → पहले पानी लो; नीबू स्क्वीज़ → नींबू निचोड़ो

Lesson 24 · Team game baat 3m, 6m

Speak during a team game: pass, wait, well done, my turn.

Say this: पास कर। · मेरी बारी। · शाबाश! बहुत अच्छा। · रुको, अभी नहीं।

Warm-up. One-minute catch or board cricket. Teach. Language during play, not after only. Guided. Replay a fight: repair with माफ़ कर, फिर से. Free. Commentate 20 seconds. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use मेरी बारी once in a real game.

Slips. माई टर्न → मेरी बारी; गुड शॉट ओनली → बहुत अच्छा शॉट

Lesson 25 · Mehman class mein 6m

Welcome a visitor and introduce the class.

Say this: नमस्ते, अंदर आइए। · यह हमारी क्लास है। · आज हम कहानी सुन रहे हैं।

Warm-up. A staff member or teddy as visitor. Teach. Aap + आइए. Child host. Guided. Three hosts share the job. Free. Visitor asks two questions; class answers. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise welcome at your front door.

Slips. कम इन → अंदर आइए; यह क्लास हमारा → यह हमारी क्लास है

Lesson 26 · Is hafte ki khabar 6w, 3m, 6m

Give news from the week: school, home, city.

Say this: इस हफ्ते मेरी परीक्षा थी। · कल बिल्डिंग में पानी नहीं आया। · मेरी टीम जीत गई।

Warm-up. Headline cards. Teach. Past + one feeling. Guided. Pair news, then class headlines. Free. Choose one story to stretch to one minute. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. One news voice note.

Slips. इस वीक एग्जाम → इस हफ्ते मेरी परीक्षा थी; वॉटर नहीं आया → पानी नहीं आया

Lesson 27 · Haan, lekin 6w, 3m, 6m

Agree and disagree kindly.

Say this: हाँ, मुझे भी लगता है। · हाँ, लेकिन... · मुझे नहीं लगता। · चलो मान लेते हैं।

Warm-up. Opinion: स्कूल जल्दी शुरू हो? गेंद अंदर? Teach. Disagree at the idea, not the child. Guided. Tiny GD: four children, one topic. Free. Teacher scores listening, not winning. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Disagree once at home without a fight.

Slips. नॉट आई थिंक → मुझे नहीं लगता; यू रॉन्ग → मेरी बात अलग है

Lesson 28 · Agar kal baarish hui 6m

If-then for weather and plans.

Say this: अगर बारिश हुई तो हम घर खेलेंगे। · अगर छुट्टी रही तो नानी के घर। · नहीं तो स्कूल।

Warm-up. Umbrella vs bag. Teach. अगर + तो + नहीं तो. Keep it oral. Guided. Invent three city ifs: traffic, power cut, guest. Free. Weather news with if. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. One if-then about tomorrow.

Slips. इफ रेन तो होम → अगर बारिश हुई तो हम घर रहेंगे; अदरवाइज़ स्कूल → नहीं तो स्कूल जाएँगे

Lesson 29 · Sheher ki ek dikkat 3m, 6m

Name a city problem children actually see.

Say this: सड़क पर बहुत गाड़ियाँ हैं। · कचरा गली में पड़ा है। · हमें ज़ेब्रा क्रॉस से जाना चाहिए।

Warm-up. Photos: traffic, garbage, broken swing. Teach. Problem + one should (चाहिए). Guided. Pairs pick one photo. Free. Class chooses one action they can do (don’t litter). Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. One problem sentence + one चाहिए.

Slips. बहुत ट्रैफिक इज़ → सड़क पर बहुत ट्रैफिक है; कचरा थ्रो नहीं → कचरा नीचे नहीं फेंकना चाहिए

Lesson 30 · Teacher ko phone 6m

A parent-child call to the teacher: child speaks one part.

Say this: नमस्ते मैम, मैं आन्या बोल रही हूँ। · कल मैं नहीं आ सकती, बुखार है। · होमवर्क भेज दूँगी।

Warm-up. Phone back-to-back. Child is the speaker, not only the parent. Teach. Aap throughout. Short, not a story. Guided. Practise sick, late, forgot book. Free. Teacher replies; child confirms. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Rehearse with a parent once.

Slips. मैम आई एम सिक → मैम, मुझे बुखार है, कल नहीं आ सकती; होमवर्क आई विल → होमवर्क भेज दूँगी / दूँगा

Lesson 31 · Review workshop 3m, 6m

Repair the week’s slips and speak longer.

Say this: Reuse doctor, directions, Sunday, invite.

Warm-up. Slip clinic from the teacher’s pad. Teach. No new language. Guided. Stations, 4 minutes each. Free. Open mic: one minute any topic. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Re-record the weakest old voice note.

Slips. New grammar notes → Only repair old lines

Lesson 32 · Showcase rehearsal 6w, 3m, 6m

Rehearse a two-minute meri city / mera din talk.

Say this: Name, school, Sunday, favourite place, one problem, end.

Warm-up. Teacher model, messy on purpose. Teach. Keyword cards only. Guided. Pairs + one question. Free. Half-class dress rehearsal. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Full talk once at home, standing.

Slips. Memorising a paragraph → Speak from five words

Lesson 33 · Elementary manch aur test 6w, 3m, 6m

Give the two-minute talk and a role-play.

Say this: City-life talk plus doctor or kirana.

Warm-up. Quiet waiting game. Teach. Score fluency, aap/tum choice, and courage. Guided. Role-play after the talk. Free. Celebrate. Point to Teens Beginner or stay to repeat. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. No homework. Rest.

Slips. Marking shuddh Hindi → Mark clear meaning and kindness

Urban Spoken Hindi · Teens · School wali Hindi

Teens · School wali Hindi

Teens 11–17 Beginner 34 lessons

Class, tiffin, phone, commute, and a two-minute ordinary day — without nursery talk.

Who: Urban teens who hear Hindi at home or on the street but speak very little, or whose only Hindi is a textbook paragraph.

Done when: The teen can introduce themselves, talk school and weekend, make a phone plan, and repair a rude line (aap vs tum).

Place here when: Place here if they freeze after one word, or if their Hindi is only written exam answers.

Teacher note: Respect their age. No animal songs. Use school, phone, and city. Ban ‘Main Ravi hoon’ as the whole identity.

Tests

StartName, class, one like, how they come to school.
Mid (after test 1 lesson)Intro, weekend, shop or phone.
EndTwo-minute day-in-my-life plus polite repair.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Naam, class, ek spark 6w, 3m, 6m

Say your name, class, and one real spark about yourself — not a full identity speech.

Say this: मैं आन्या, आठवीं बी। — I’m Aanya, 8B. · मुझे क्रिकेट / डांस / कोडिंग अच्छा लगता है। — I like cricket / dance / coding. · तुम्हारा कौन सा सेक्शन है? — Which section are you in?

Warm-up. Stand. 20-second name chain: name + class only. No long intro. Ban Main Ravi hoon as the whole talk. Teach. Board three slots: नाम, क्लास, एक स्पार्क. Model मैं कबीर, नौवीं ए। मुझे मेट्रो में गाना सुनना पसंद है. Teach लगता है vs हूँ. Guided. Speed pairs: ask सेक्शन and one spark. Swap after 40 seconds. Teacher repairs आप हो if anyone uses aap on a classmate wrongly — classmates get tum. Free. Mingle of eight: collect four sparks on a scrap. Report one classmate: यह मीरा है, सातवीं सी, उसे स्केच अच्छा लगता है. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. WhatsApp voice note: नाम, क्लास, एक स्पार्क. Stand and speak; do not read.

Slips. मैं रवि हूँ। (and then freeze) → मैं रवि, नौवीं ए। मुझे ___ अच्छा लगता है; आप कौन सी क्लास हो? → तुम कौन सी क्लास में हो? / आप किस क्लास में हैं?

Lesson 02 · Aaj school kaise raha 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell how school actually went today — tired, okay, or a mess — not only fine.

Say this: आज स्कूल ठीक-ठाक रहा। — School was okay-ish today. · बहुत थक गया / थक गई। — I got really tired. · पीरियड बढ़िया गया, बट कैंटीन में लाइन थी। — The period went well, but the canteen line was long.

Warm-up. Three corners: बढ़िया / ठीक-ठाक / खराब. Walk to the truth of today and say one word. Teach. Teach रहा / रही for the day, गया / गई for a person. Model नहीं सिर्फ फाइन: आज बोरिंग रहा because double Maths. Guided. Pair interview: आज कौन सा पीरियड भारी था? क्यों? Partner answers in two lines, not one English word. Free. Hot-seat: teacher is a parent at the gate. Teen reports the day without fine-only. Class votes: did it sound real? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. At the dinner table say आज स्कूल ___ रहा and one because.

Slips. आज स्कूल फाइन था ओनली → आज स्कूल ठीक-ठाक रहा; मैं थक गया है → मैं थक गया हूँ / थक गई हूँ

Lesson 03 · Ghar ke log, asli 6w, 3m, 6m

Name who actually lives at home and one habit — no baby family song.

Say this: घर में मम्मी, पापा और मैं। — At home: Mum, Dad, and me. · मेरी दीदी ट्यूशन जाती है। — My didi goes to tuition. · नाना वीकेंड पे आते हैं। — Nana comes on weekends.

Warm-up. Silent count on fingers: how many people sleep in your house tonight. Say the number in Hindi. Teach. Cards: मम्मी, पापा, भाई, दीदी, दादी, नानी, चाचा. Teach रहते हैं / रहती हैं and हैं for elders. Flat / PG / joint — all valid. Guided. Draw a quick house map. Tell a partner who is where at 8 pm: पापा अभी ऑफिस से आ रहे हैं. Free. Gallery: visit two maps. Ask यह कौन हैं? क्या करते हैं? No ranking whose family is bigger. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Show a photo or empty frame and say three Hindi lines about who is at home.

Slips. यह मेरी मम्मी है (for an elder, if the class is ready) → यह मेरी मम्मी हैं; घर में है पापा मम्मी मैं → घर में पापा, मम्मी और मैं रहते हैं

Lesson 04 · Class mein atka 6w, 3m, 6m

Survive a stuck moment: I didn’t get it, say it again, I need a leave slip.

Say this: मैम, समझ नहीं आया / आई। — Ma’am, I didn’t get it. · एक बार फिर, धीरे। — Once more, slowly. · लीव स्लिप के लिए ऑफिस जाना है। — I have to go to the office for a leave slip.

Warm-up. Freeze statues: confused face, raised hand, door knock. Unfreeze only with a Hindi line. Teach. Four lines on the board with aap for the teacher. Gender: आया / आई matches the speaker. Ban tu to the teacher. Guided. Card draw: late, missed homework, didn’t hear the question, need water. Partner is the teacher. Free. Corridor + office role-play: leave slip, then back to class. Repair any सर तुम. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use समझ नहीं आया/आई or एक बार फिर once in a real class this week.

Slips. सर, तुम दोबारा बोलो → सर, एक बार फिर बताइए; मैं नहीं समझ → समझ नहीं आया / आई

Lesson 05 · Period kab hai 3m, 6m

Talk timetable: which period, which room, what is next.

Say this: अगला पीरियड केमिस्ट्री है। — Next period is Chemistry. · तीसरे पीरियड में लाइब्रेरी है। — Third period is library. · टाइमटेबल बदल गया क्या? — Did the timetable change?

Warm-up. Shout-free: teacher calls a period number; teens say the subject they actually have if they can, or a sample board. Teach. Teach पीरियड, छुट्टी, डबल, सब्स्टिट्यूट. Clock language: कितने बजे, not कितना बजे. Guided. Pairs rebuild a fake Monday on slips. Ask अगला क्या है? कहाँ जाना है? Free. Chaos bell: substitute teacher. Announce the change standing up in two Hindi sentences. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: आज मेरे ___ पीरियड में ___ है।

Slips. कितना बजे पीरियड → कितने बजे पीरियड है?; अगला पीरियड केमिस्ट्री → अगला पीरियड केमिस्ट्री है

Lesson 06 · Tiffin: tez, thanda, bekaar 6w, 3m, 6m

Give a real tiffin opinion: spicy, cold, good, or skip.

Say this: आज टिफिन ठंडा हो गया। — The tiffin went cold today. · थोड़ा तीखा है, मुझे चल जाएगा। — It’s a bit spicy; I can manage. · यह मुझे पसंद नहीं, स्वैप करें? — I don’t like this — swap?

Warm-up. Mystery lunch cards: rajma, idli, maggi, thepla, leftover pizza. Face: yum / okay / no. Teach. Teach तीखा, फीका, ठंडा, गरम, बासी. मुझे ___ पसंद है. Gender: ठंडा / ठंडी with the food they name if it matters today. Guided. Tiffin court: one speaker, one judge. 30 seconds: what’s inside + one opinion + one ask (salt, water, swap). Free. Canteen vs dabba debate in pairs: आज मैंने ___ लाया/लाई because... Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Open tomorrow’s dabba and send a 15-second voice: तीखा / ठंडा / ठीक.

Slips. मैं टिफिन पसंद है → मुझे यह टिफिन पसंद है; बहुत स्पाइसी है ओनली → बहुत तीखा है

Lesson 07 · Meri gali 3m, 6m

Describe your gali, society, or block without a tourist speech.

Say this: मैं सोसाइटी में रहता हूँ / रहती हूँ। — I live in a society. · गली में किराना और एक मंदिर है। — There’s a kirana and a mandir on the street. · शाम को बहुत हॉर्न होता है। — In the evening there’s a lot of honking.

Warm-up. 30-second soundscape: each teen names one sound from home — कुत्ता, मेट्रो, प्रेसर कुकर, लिफ्ट. Teach. के पास, के नीचे, के सामने. Society / chawl / independent house all get the same dignity. Guided. Partner draws from speech only: gate, park, kirana. Then check. Free. Give a visitor directions from the main road to your building in four short lines. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Walk to your gate and voice-note three things you can see, in Hindi.

Slips. मैं सोसाइटी रहता → मैं सोसाइटी में रहता/रहती हूँ; गली में है किराना ओनली → गली में किराना है

Lesson 08 · Phone: kahan, late, plan 6w, 3m, 6m

Make a short phone burst: where are you, I’m late, what’s the plan.

Say this: तुम कहाँ हो? — Where are you? · मैं लेट हूँ, मेट्रो में हूँ। — I’m late — I’m on the metro. · पांच बजे मॉल के गेट पे मिलते हैं। — Let’s meet at the mall gate at five.

Warm-up. Back-to-back chairs. First 20 seconds: only हेलो and name. No video-call acting. Teach. Teach बोल रहा हूँ / रही हूँ, पहुँच गया / गई, निकल रहा / रही. Friends = tum. Parent = aap if that is their home rule; many urban homes use tum with mum — let them choose and stay consistent. Guided. Three missed-call cards: tuition late, forgot tiffin, plan change. Pair calls. Free. Group of three: one is busy parent, one is friend, one is calling. Register must shift. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Send the teacher a 20-second fake missed-call voice: कहाँ हो + प्लान.

Slips. मैं लेट है → मैं लेट हूँ; मिलते हैं हम मॉल → हम मॉल के गेट पे मिलते हैं

Lesson 09 · Asli shauk 3m, 6m

Talk a real hobby — reels, football, sketch, coding — not stamp collecting unless it’s true.

Say this: मेरा शौक फुटबॉल है। — My hobby is football. · मैं हर शाम आधा घंटा स्केच करती हूँ / करता हूँ। — I sketch for half an hour every evening. · मुझे शॉर्ट्स बनाना अच्छा लगता है। — I like making shorts.

Warm-up. Two truths: write a fake hobby and a real one. Partner guesses. Teach. शौक, अच्छा लगता है, सीख रहा/रही हूँ. Ban the textbook stamp-collection sentence unless someone actually does it. Guided. Speed dating chairs: 45 seconds each — what, when, why one line. Free. Show-and-tell without the object if needed: phone photo of a sketch, a match, a playlist name. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: मेरा असली शौक ___ है, क्योंकि ___.

Slips. मेरा हॉबी है क्रिकेट ओनली → मेरा शौक क्रिकेट है / मुझे क्रिकेट अच्छा लगता है; मैं करता स्केच शाम → मैं शाम को स्केच करता/करती हूँ

Lesson 10 · Canteen ke paise 3m, 6m

Order at the canteen and talk money without freezing on numbers.

Say this: एक समोसा और चाय देना। — One samosa and chai, please. · यह कितने का है? — How much is this? · मेरे पास सिर्फ पचास हैं। — I only have fifty.

Warm-up. Paper notes 10/20/50. Quick कीमत shout for samosa, bun-maska, nimbu. Teach. कितने का / कितनी की. देना to the canteen uncle/aunty with aap if they don’t know them. Change: वापस ___ दीजिए. Guided. Pair shop: order two things, add, pay, take change. If short, बचे हुए पैसे कल दूँगा/दूँगी. Free. Rush hour: four customers, one counter. Polite, loud enough, no cutting the line in English only. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. If you buy something this week, say यह कितने का है? yourself.

Slips. यह कितना है समोसा → यह समोसा कितने का है?; पैसे दो वापस तुम → वापस पैसे दीजिए

Lesson 11 · Rasta: metro, bus, auto 6w, 3m, 6m

Ask and give a city route: metro, bus, or auto.

Say this: यह मेट्रो कहाँ जाएगी? — Where will this metro go? · ऑटोवाले, राजौरी तक कितना लोगे? — Auto-wale, how much to Rajouri? · सीधा जाकर बाएँ मुड़ना। — Go straight and turn left.

Warm-up. Floor tape: platform, bus stop, auto stand. Teens stand on a spot and name it. Teach. सीधा, बाएँ, दाएँ, अगला स्टेशन, उतरना. Aap with the stranger; tum only if they role-play a friend guiding. Guided. Pairs: I am new, I need the mall / tuition / station. Partner gives three steps. Free. Lost-on-Yellow-Line: one phone map (paper). Ask two people, then an auto. End at the gate. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: घर से स्कूल — मेट्रो / बस / ऑटो में दो लाइनें.

Slips. यहाँ से मॉल कैसे → यहाँ से मॉल कैसे जाएँ?; लेफ्ट लेना → बाएँ मुड़ना / बाएँ लेना

Lesson 12 · Pressure, bina drama 3m, 6m

Name exam, fight, or crush-level stress in age-safe Hindi — no gossip show.

Say this: मुझे बहुत प्रेशर लग रहा है। — I’m feeling a lot of pressure. · कल टेस्ट है, नींद नहीं आई। — There’s a test tomorrow; I couldn’t sleep. · दोस्तों में थोड़ी लड़ाई हो गई। — There was a bit of a fight among friends.

Warm-up. Anonymous slip: one pressure word. Teacher reads a few aloud; class only says मुझे भी if true. Teach. लग रहा है, टेंशन, घबराहट. Crush = किसी को लेकर घबराहट or मन नहीं लग रहा — no names, no teasing. Fight stays light: बात बिगड़ गई. Guided. Partner: 40 seconds of truth + one coping line: मैं चलूँगा/चलूँगी, पानी पिया, मम्मी से कहा. Free. Advice hotline: listener only says समझ सकती/सकता हूँ and one kind line. No fixing lives. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Private voice note to the teacher or a parent: एक असली प्रेशर, एक लाइन.

Slips. मैं प्रेशर हूँ → मुझे प्रेशर लग रहा है; मैं स्लीप नहीं किया → मुझे नींद नहीं आई

Lesson 13 · Pichhla weekend 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell last weekend as it happened — sleep, tuition, relatives, phone — not a picnic essay.

Say this: शनिवार को मैं सोता रहा / सोती रही। — On Saturday I just kept sleeping. · रविवार को नानी के घर गए। — On Sunday we went to Nani’s. · थोड़ा होमवर्क भी किया। — I also did a bit of homework.

Warm-up. Timeline on the board: Sat morning / Sat night / Sunday. Teens tick what was true. Teach. Past: गया / गई, किया / की, देखा / देखी. Don’t teach the whole tense table. कल = that weekend from context. Guided. Partner interview: तीन सवाल — कहाँ गए, किसके साथ, बोर हुआ/हुई क्या? Free. 2-minute story in threes. If nothing happened, that is the story: घर पे था/थी, फोन चलाया. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: पिछला शनिवार, तीन वाक्य, आँखें खुली।

Slips. मैं गया मॉल कल → कल मैं मॉल गया / गई; वीकेंड मैं स्लीप → वीकेंड मैं सोता रहा / सोती रही

Lesson 14 · School ke baad 3m, 6m

Talk real after-school plans: tuition, ground, home, series.

Say this: स्कूल के बाद ट्यूशन है। — I have tuition after school. · आज सीधे घर जाऊँगा / जाऊँगी। — I’ll go straight home today. · थोड़ी देर ग्राउंड पे मिलते हैं। — Let’s meet at the ground for a bit.

Warm-up. Human bar graph: tuition / home / ground / shop. Stand in the true line. Teach. के बाद, सीधे, फिर. Future-ish: जाऊँगा / जाऊँगी, करना है. Guided. Pair: change the plan twice — rain, extra class, parent call. Free. Group chat on paper: four friends, one plan that actually fits two of them. Speak it, don’t only write. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. This afternoon tell someone in Hindi: आज स्कूल के बाद ___.

Slips. स्कूल बाद ट्यूशन → स्कूल के बाद ट्यूशन है; मैं जाऊँगा घर सीधे → मैं सीधे घर जाऊँगा / जाऊँगी

Lesson 15 · Uniform vs apne kapde 3m, 6m

Compare uniform days and own-clothes days with a real opinion.

Say this: आज यूनिफॉर्म है, गरम लग रही है। — It’s a uniform day; it feels hot. · कल कलर ड्रेस है। — Tomorrow is colour dress. · मुझे अपने कपड़े ज़्यादा आरामदेह लगते हैं। — My own clothes feel more comfortable.

Warm-up. Two photos or sketches: ironed uniform vs hoodie. Teens point and name कपड़े. Teach. पहन रहा/रही, लग रही है, आरामदेह, टाइट. Gender of यूनिफॉर्म / शर्ट as they use it; fix only if meaning flips. Guided. Pair: PTA day vs Sunday. What do you wear, what do you wish? Free. Fashion-not-shame circle: describe a classmate’s bag or shoes, not bodies. Class guesses who. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tomorrow morning: आज मैं ___ पहन रहा/रही हूँ — one voice line.

Slips. मैं पहनता यूनिफॉर्म → मैं यूनिफॉर्म पहन रहा/रही हूँ; कपड़े आराम है → कपड़े आरामदेह हैं

Lesson 16 · PT, sport, skip 3m, 6m

Talk PT, a sport you play, or the honest I skip PT.

Say this: मुझे पीटी में दौड़ना ठीक लगता है। — I don’t mind running in PT. · मैं क्रिकेट खेलता हूँ / खेलती हूँ। — I play cricket. · आज पीटी स्किप कर दी, पेट दर्द था। — I skipped PT today; I had a stomach ache.

Warm-up. Mime only: football, throw, yoga, sitting-out. Class names the sport in Hindi/Hinglish. Teach. खेलता/खेलती, चोट, पसीना, स्किप. Sitting out is allowed speech: मैं नहीं खेलूँगा/खेलूँगी आज. Guided. Interview: favourite game, worst drill, did you bunk. No moral lecture. Free. Coach vs student role-play: late to ground, no shoes, want to sit. Stay polite, stay true. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: खेलता/खेलती हूँ या आज नहीं — एक सच।

Slips. मैं खेलता है क्रिकेट → मैं क्रिकेट खेलता/खेलती हूँ; पीटी मैं स्किप → मैंने पीटी स्किप कर दी

Lesson 17 · Chemist / doctor, teen 3m, 6m

Handle a chemist or clinic as a teen: symptom, medicine, parent on the way.

Say this: सर, सर्दी-खाँसी की दवाई चाहिए। — Sir, I need a cold-and-cough medicine. · मुझे बुखार है, कल से। — I’ve had a fever since yesterday. · मम्मी आ रही हैं, पाँच मिनट। — Mum is coming, five minutes.

Warm-up. Symptom cards: सिर, पेट, गला, चोट. Point and say दर्द. Teach. मुझे ___ है, कब से, एलर्जी. Aap to doctor and chemist. Don’t invent drug names; साधारण दवाई is enough. Guided. Pair: chemist counter. Age check: यह बच्चों के लिए ठीक है? Teen speaks; parent is late. Free. Clinic: three stations — reception, doctor, chemist. Move with a slip. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. If you visit a chemist this week, say एक लाइन खुद. Else voice-note the fever line.

Slips. मुझे फीवर है ओनली → मुझे बुखार है; दवाई दो सर तुम → सर, दवाई दीजिए

Lesson 18 · Reel, comment, fight 6m

Talk online life in spoken Hindi: reel, comment, a small fight — keep it kind.

Say this: मैंने एक रील बनाई। — I made a reel. · कमेंट में लोग बहुत कड़वे थे। — People were really harsh in the comments. · मैंने रिप्लाई नहीं किया। — I didn’t reply.

Warm-up. Phone-down rule. Show a muted fake comment on the board: मज़ाक vs गाली (no actual slurs). Teach. बनाई / देखा, ब्लॉक कर दिया, रिपोर्ट. Gender of रील / वीडियो as they naturally say. Teach मैंने. Guided. Pair: explain a reel without showing it. Partner asks क्यों वायरल? Free. Repair a fight: two comments, one kind exit line — मैं यह नहीं चाहता/चाहती, बाय. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक ऑनलाइन बात — बिना नाम, बिना गाली।

Slips. मैं रील बनाया → मैंने एक रील बनाई; कमेंट में फाइट हो गया हम → कमेंट में लड़ाई हो गई

Lesson 19 · Chhoti city trip 6m

Tell a small city outing: metro, mall, relative, day picnic — not a India-tour essay.

Say this: हम मेट्रो से इंडिया गेट गए। — We went to India Gate by metro. · रास्ते में बहुत भीड़ थी। — There was a huge crowd on the way. · वापस आने में दो घंटे लगे। — It took two hours to get back.

Warm-up. Ticket stubs or phone photos on desks. Each teen names one place they actually went this year. Teach. गए / गई, भीड़, टिकट, वापस. Fix रस्ते में (teacher writes रस्ते). Guided. Partner builds a 4-beat story: निकले, पहुँचे, खाया, लौटे. Free. Lost-and-found at the mall: announce one missing bottle in Hindi to a guard (aap). Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक छोटी ट्रिप, चार वाक्य.

Slips. हम गए इंडिया गेट मेट्रो → हम मेट्रो से इंडिया गेट गए; भीड़ था बहुत → बहुत भीड़ थी

Lesson 20 · Aap, tum, tu 6w, 3m, 6m

Choose aap, tum, or tu on purpose — and hear when tu to a teacher is rude.

Say this: आप कैसे हैं? — How are you? (aap) · तुम कब आ रहे हो? — When are you coming? (friend) · तुम मुझे टू मत बोलो। — Don’t tu me.

Warm-up. Three hats or badges: टीचर, फ्रेंड, घर. Teacher walks; class must greet with the right pronoun. Teach. Social, not a table: aap + हैं, tum + हो, tu + hai. Tu to teacher = stop and repair. Some homes use tu with siblings — mark the room. Guided. Card sort: principal, canteen uncle you don’t know, best friend, little cousin, auto-wale. Free. Repair clinic: rude lines on slips. Rewrite and perform the polite version. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Catch yourself once this week: switch one तुम to आप with an adult.

Slips. आप कैसे हो? → आप कैसे हैं?; मैम, तुम सुनो → मैम, आप सुनिए

Lesson 21 · Subject pasand kyunki 3m, 6m

Say which subject you like or hate and give a because that is not empty.

Say this: मुझे साइंस अच्छी लगती है क्योंकि लैब मस्त है। — I like Science because the lab is fun. · मैं मैथ्स से डरता हूँ / डरती हूँ। — I’m scared of Maths. · हिंदी लिखना मुश्किल लगता है, बोलना आसान। — Writing Hindi feels hard; speaking is easier.

Warm-up. Subject cards on the floor. Stand on one you like and one you don’t — two rounds. Teach. क्योंकि + real reason: टीचर, मार्क्स, बोरिंग, समझ आता है. Gender: अच्छी लगती / अच्छा लगता with the noun they chose. Guided. Speed: 20 seconds like + kyunki, 20 seconds dislike + kyunki. No because I don’t know. Free. Panel: three ‘teachers’ ask क्यों? Student holds the floor for 40 seconds. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक विषय + क्योंकि + एक असली वजह.

Slips. मुझे साइंस पसंद क्योंकि → मुझे साइंस अच्छी लगती है क्योंकि...; मैं डर हूँ मैथ्स → मुझे मैथ्स से डर लगता है

Lesson 22 · Group project Hindi 3m, 6m

Run a tiny group-project meeting in Hindi: divide work, deadline, complain lightly.

Say this: तुम चार्ट बनाना। मैं लिखूँगी / लिखूँगा। — You do the chart. I’ll write. · कल तक करना है। — It has to be done by tomorrow. · तुमने मैसेज देखा ही नहीं। — You didn’t even see the message.

Warm-up. Form groups of three. 60 seconds: pick a fake project name in Hindi. Teach. तुम / हम, डेडलाइन, बाँटना. Soft complaint vs fight: यार, थोड़ा रिप्लाई कर दिया कर. Guided. Plan on sticky notes, then speak the plan with no notes. Free. One member ‘disappears’. Group leaves a voice-on-desk: कहाँ हो, हम रुक नहीं सकते. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. If you have a real group, send one Hindi task line on WhatsApp.

Slips. तुम करो चार्ट मैं लिख → तुम चार्ट बनाना, मैं लिखूँगा/लिखूँगी; डेडलाइन है कल ओनली → कल तक करना है

Lesson 23 · Din kharab gaya 3m, 6m

Tell a bad-day story that moves: start, hit, end — not only I was sad.

Say this: सुबह से ही सब उल्टा गया। — From the morning everything went wrong. · बस छूट गई, फिर टीचर नाराज़ हो गईं। — I missed the bus, then the teacher got angry. · अब ठीक हूँ, बस थक गया / गई। — I’m okay now, just tired.

Warm-up. Three beats on the board: शुरू, टक्कर, अब. Teens tap the table for each beat. Teach. गया / गई, छूट गई, नाराज़. Fillers allowed: यार, मतलब — but not a 10-second freeze. Guided. Partner story from three picture cards (gate, rain, phone 1%). Must use फिर. Free. Open mic: 60-second bad day. Class only claps; teacher notes one repair for polish. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: खराब दिन, तीन बीट्स, एक मिनट से कम.

Slips. दिन खराब → दिन खराब गया / खराब हो गया; बस मिस हो गई मैं → मेरी बस छूट गई

Lesson 24 · Review + test 1 6w, 3m, 6m

Reuse name+spark, school day, phone plan, and one aap repair for the first speaking test.

Say this: मैं ईशान, नौवीं सी, मुझे फुटबॉल अच्छा लगता है। — I’m Ishaan, 9C; I like football. · आज स्कूल ठीक-ठाक रहा। — School was okay-ish today. · पाँच बजे गेट पे मिलते हैं। — Let’s meet at the gate at five. · मैम, समझ नहीं आया। — Ma’am, I didn’t get it.

Warm-up. Station rotation, 2 minutes: spark, tiffin, phone, rasta. Teach. Teacher marks only: did they speak, aap vs tum, one full sentence. No written exam. Guided. Pairs run the four lines without reading. Swap roles: teacher / friend. Free. Mini test: 90 seconds mera din-ish + one phone or shop. Then one rude line to repair. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note standing up: the four test lines, no script in hand.

Slips. आप कैसे हो? → आप कैसे हैं?; Only English after the first word → Keep the Hindi frames; English nouns are fine

Lesson 25 · Logon ko describe 3m, 6m

Describe people by style, voice, and habit — never body-shaming.

Say this: मेरा दोस्त रेहान बहुत शांत है। — My friend Rehan is very quiet. · दीया हमेशा समय पर आती है। — Diya always comes on time. · उनकी आवाज़ धीमी है, बात साफ़ है। — Their voice is soft; the talk is clear.

Warm-up. Ban list on the board: मोटा, काला as insult, funny-looking. Tear it. Replace with kind hooks. Teach. शांत, तेज़, मददगार, लेट, क्रिएटिव. Clothes and bags okay; bodies not a joke. Guided. Partner describes a film character or a teacher-type without the name. Guess. Free. Kind portrait: 40 seconds on a classmate who gave permission, or on Aanya from a card. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Describe one person at home by habit, not looks — two Hindi lines.

Slips. वो मोटा है (as the whole description) → वो हमेशा जोर से हँसता है / शांत रहता है; दीया आती लेट हमेशा → दीया हमेशा लेट आती है

Lesson 26 · Do schools, do areas 6m

Compare two schools or two areas with lekin, not a winner speech.

Say this: हमारी स्कूल छोटी है, उनकी बड़ी है। — Our school is small; theirs is big. · यहाँ मेट्रो पास है, वहाँ बस ही है। — Metro is close here; there it’s only buses. · कैंटीन उनकी बेहतर है, ग्राउंड हमारा। — Their canteen is better; our ground is ours.

Warm-up. Two area names on the board (real or sample). Teens shout one fact each side. Teach. से / से ज़्यादा, लेकिन, दोनों. No area-shaming. Tuition-heavy vs land-heavy is fine. Guided.
