Pairs: old school vs now, or nani’s gali vs yours. Free. Estate-agent 60 seconds: sell neither; just compare for a new kid. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: दो जगह, दो लेकिन।

Slips. उनकी स्कूल बड़ा है → उनकी स्कूल बड़ी है; यहाँ मेट्रो है पास ओनली → यहाँ मेट्रो पास है

Lesson 27 · Invite / cancel 3m, 6m

Invite a friend and cancel without ghosting — Hindi that sounds 14.

Say this: कल ट्यूशन के बाद चल, ग्राउंड? — After tuition tomorrow, ground? · यार, आज नहीं हो पाएगा। — Yaar, it won’t work today. · सॉरी, मम्मी ने मना कर दिया। — Sorry, Mum said no.

Warm-up. Thumbs: accept / later / no. Teacher calls a plan; class flashes a thumb and a line. Teach. चल, मिलते हैं, हो पाएगा, नेक्स्ट टाइम. Cancel needs a reason that is not fake-illness every time. Guided. Pair texts spoken aloud: invite, delay, new time. Free. Group of four: one host, three answers — yes, no, maybe. Host restates the final plan. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Send one real Hindi invite or cancel to a friend today.

Slips. आज नहीं आ सकता मैं ओनली → आज नहीं हो पाएगा / मैं नहीं आ सकता/सकती; मना कर दिया मम्मी → मम्मी ने मना कर दिया

Lesson 28 · Ticket counter 6m

Buy a metro or bus ticket and handle a small counter problem.

Say this: एक टोकन, जूही वाला। — One token, towards Juhi / the named station. · कार्ड में पैसे कम हैं। — There’s not enough money on the card. · वापस कितने मिले? — How much change did I get?

Warm-up. Desk window. Queue of five. No jumping. Teach. टोकन, कार्ड रिचार्ज, रिटर्न. Aap to the staff. Numbers spoken, not whispered in English only. Guided. Pair: wrong station name, then repair. Machine vs human window. Free. Rush: card fail, friend waiting, train in 2 minutes. Two Hindi lines + move. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Next metro/bus, say the destination in Hindi yourself.

Slips. टोकन दो एक → एक टोकन दीजिए; कार्ड में मनी नहीं → कार्ड में पैसे कम हैं

Lesson 29 · Chhote ko help 3m, 6m

Help a younger child or a lost junior without sounding like a boss.

Say this: टेंशन मत ले, मैं साथ हूँ। — Don’t stress; I’m with you. · तेरी क्लास उधर है। — Your class is that way. · वॉशरूम सामने है, पूछना हो तो बोल। — Washroom is in front; say if you need to ask.

Warm-up. Height mime: talk to a class-4 kid. Softer voice, still Hindi. Teach. तुम / तू with a much younger kid is common — but teach a kind tum first. Don’t scare them with aap comedy. Guided. Lost junior cards: bus, water, bully (keep light: कोई छेड़ रहा है — tell a teacher). Free. Buddy walk from gate to 6A. Helper speaks; junior only nods, then tries one line. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Help one younger person this week with a Hindi line, or voice-note the buddy walk.

Slips. टेंशन मत आपके → टेंशन मत ले / मत लो; क्लास है उधर तेरा → तेरी क्लास उधर है

Lesson 30 · Khabar jo suni 6m

Retell a piece of news you actually heard — school, colony, or phone — and mark rumour.

Say this: मैंने सुना कि कल छुट्टी हो सकती है। — I heard there might be a holiday tomorrow. · पक्का नहीं, बस व्हाट्सऐप पे था। — Not sure — it was just on WhatsApp. · टीचर ने कहा कि डेट बदल गई। — The teacher said the date changed.

Warm-up. Two bins: सुना and पक्का. Teens drop a slip in one. Teach. मैंने सुना, हो सकता है, टीचर ने कहा. Ban sharing a real student’s private news. Guided. Pair: take a headline and downgrade it to rumour language, then upgrade if a teacher said it. Free. Colony auntie vs school notice. Perform both in 20 seconds each. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक खबर + पक्का या सिर्फ़ सुना.

Slips. कल छुट्टी है पक्का (when it was a forward) → मैंने सुना, पक्का नहीं; सुना मैं कि → मैंने सुना कि...

Lesson 31 · Agar time mila 3m, 6m

Talk hypothetical spare time: if I get time — games, sleep, help at home.

Say this: अगर समय मिला तो सोऊँगा / सोऊँगी। — If I get time I’ll sleep. · थोड़ा गेम भी खेलूँगा / खेलूँगी। — I’ll also play a bit of a game. · मम्मी को मदद करनी चाहिए, पता है। — I know I should help Mum.

Warm-up. Magic extra hour. Silent wish, then speak it. Teach. अगर + तो, चाहिए vs करूँगा. Keep it light; this is not a career class. Guided. Pairs deal cards: rain, no tuition, guests at home. If-then out loud. Free. Circle: one selfish wish, one kind wish. Both valid. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tonight: अगर समय मिला तो ___ — say it once at home.

Slips. अगर टाइम मिला I will sleep → अगर समय मिला तो सोऊँगा/सोऊँगी; मैं चाहिए मदद → मुझे मदद करनी चाहिए

Lesson 32 · Dreams, bina pressure 6w, 3m, 6m

Talk a future wish or say I don’t know yet — that answer is full, not lazy.

Say this: अभी पक्का नहीं पता। — I don’t know for sure yet. · शायद डिज़ाइन, शायद कुछ और। — Maybe design, maybe something else. · मुझे लोगों की मदद वाली नौकरी अच्छी लगेगी। — I’d like a job that helps people.

Warm-up. Board: अभी नहीं पता is written big. Anyone may stand under it first. Teach. बन्ना / बनना चाहता/चाहती, शायद, अभी नहीं. No ranking of doctor vs gamer. Ban क्या बनोगे interrogation voice. Guided. Pair: one dream + one I don’t know. Partner only asks एक छोटी सी बात, not twenty whys. Free. Open chairs: 40 seconds. Clap for अभी नहीं पता as hard as for a big dream. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक सपना या अभी नहीं पता — दोनों पूरे जवाब हैं.

Slips. मैं डॉक्टर बनना है → मैं डॉक्टर बनना चाहता/चाहती हूँ; पता नहीं (and shrug silence) → अभी पक्का नहीं पता, बाद में सोचना

Lesson 33 · Review games 3m, 6m

Reuse the term’s Hindi in fast, teen games — still no nursery songs.

Say this: एक लाइन में बताओ। — Tell it in one line. · गलत नहीं, दोबारा कोशिश। — Not wrong — try again. · मेरी बारी। — My turn.

Warm-up. Hot potato (ball or bottle): catch = name + spark or tiffin line. Teach. Teacher only referees. Stations: phone, rasta, aap-repair, weekend. Guided. Quiz in pairs: 60 seconds, they keep score by speaking, not writing essays. Free. Relay: four teens, one story — school day that goes wrong, then a plan. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Pick the weakest lesson and send a 20-second retry voice.

Slips. मेरी टर्न → मेरी बारी; गलत हो गया शट अप → कोई बात नहीं, दोबारा बोल

Lesson 34 · Showcase: mera din 6w, 3m, 6m

Give a two-minute ordinary day plus one polite repair — beginner finale.

Say this: सुबह मैं ___ उठता हूँ / उठती हूँ। — In the morning I get up at ___. · स्कूल ___ रहा। — School was ___. · शाम को ___। — In the evening ___. · धन्यवाद, कोई सवाल? — Thanks — any question?

Warm-up. Silent walk-through of the day with four freeze poses. Then add words. Teach. Remind gender on उठा/उठी only if it blocks. Hinglish nouns stay. Examiner-style aap if a guest comes. Guided. Pairs rehearse 90 seconds. Partner asks one follow-up. Free. Showcase: two-minute day + repair a planted rude line (tu to teacher). Class glow + one grow. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Final voice note: पूरा दिन, खड़े होकर, बिना पढ़े।

Slips. मैं उठता है सात → मैं सात बजे उठता/उठती हूँ; आप हो मेरे टीचर → आप मेरे टीचर हैं

Urban Spoken Hindi · Teens · Samajhta hoon, bol nahi paata

Teens · Samajhta hoon, bol nahi paata

Teens 11–17 Intermediate fluency 32 lessons

The largest Indian teen group for Hindi. Opinions, stories, GD manners, photo talk, less freeze.

Who: Teens who follow Hindi films, class, and family talk — but go quiet when they must speak in full sentences.

Done when: They can speak for two minutes, join a kind GD, describe a photo, and disagree without a fight.

Place here when: Place here if they can do the Teens Beginner test but still plan every word in English first.

Teacher note: Do not reteach hai/hain unless it blocks meaning. Push talking time. Correct after the turn, not during it.

Tests

Start60-second familiar topic. Note the freeze points.
MidIntro, photo, opinion, tiny GD.
EndSpeech or GD plus photo. Score fluency and interaction.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Freeze normal hai 6w, 3m, 6m

Name the freeze out loud and restart with a filler that is Hindi, not silence.

Say this: रुको, सोचने दो। — Wait, let me think. · मतलब... हाँ। — I mean… yes. · वो शब्द नहीं आ रहा, ऐसे समझो ___। — The word isn’t coming; take it like ___.

Warm-up. Teacher times a 10-second silent freeze, then models मतलब / यानी / ना instead of English-only um forever. Teach. Teach restart lines. Ban sorry-I-can’t as the first move. Fillers are tools; a 15-second blank is the problem. Guided. Partner: mid-sentence the teacher claps. Speaker must use a restart, not laugh-quit. Free. 60-second topic they know (tiffin, metro). Score only: did they restart once? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: 40 seconds on school gate. If you freeze, keep the restart on the recording.

Slips. long English um then quit → मतलब... एक सेकंड, फिर से; सॉरी सर नहीं आ रहा (and sit) → शब्द नहीं आ रहा, ऐसे बोलता/बोलती हूँ...

Lesson 02 · 60-sec main 6w, 3m, 6m

Hold a 60-second self-talk: now, school, one spark — without a memorised paragraph.

Say this: मैं ज़ारा, ग्यारहवीं, साउथ दिल्ली। — I’m Zara, class 11, South Delhi. · रोज़ मेट्रो से आती हूँ। — I come by metro every day. · अभी मुझे बोर्ड का थोड़ा डर है, बाकी ठीक हूँ। — I’m a bit scared of boards right now; otherwise I’m okay.

Warm-up. Timer visible. First round 30 seconds only, any Hindi. Teach. Structure on three fingers: अब कौन, स्कूल, एक सच्ची बात. Not a CV. Gender on आती/आता. Guided. Pairs face-to-face, timer, no notes. Partner raises a finger at 40s as a nudge, not a correction. Free. Two volunteers to the front. Class notes one clear sentence, not accent. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. WhatsApp: exactly 60 seconds. Don’t re-record more than twice.

Slips. मैं हूँ ज़ारा ग्यारहवीं साउथ (one breath dump) → मैं ज़ारा। ग्यारहवीं। साउथ दिल्ली से आती/आता हूँ; I am studying in... (whole minute English) → मैं ___ में पढ़ती/पढ़ता हूँ, मतलब...

Lesson 03 · Raay kyunki 6w, 3m, 6m

Give an opinion with kyunki that a classmate can answer.

Say this: मुझे लगता है स्कूल देर से शुरू होना चाहिए, क्योंकि नींद पूरी नहीं होती। — I think school should start later because we don’t get enough sleep. · कैंटीन महँगी है। — The canteen is expensive. · सहमत नहीं, क्योंकि बस भी लेट होती है। — I don’t agree, because the buses run late too.

Warm-up. Vote with feet: PTA homework vs phone ban. No talking yet. Teach. मुझे लगता है + क्योंकि + एक वजह. Then एक उदाहरण. Don’t teach essay linkers today. Guided. Pair: 40s opinion, 20s partner kyunki-back. Must not only हाँ यार. Free. Four chairs: topic phone in class. Each must add क्योंकि or लेकिन. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक राय + क्योंकि + एक छोटा उदाहरण.

Slips. मुझे लगता स्कूल लेट होना → मुझे लगता है स्कूल देर से शुरू होना चाहिए; क्योंकि... (and freeze) → क्योंकि नींद नहीं आती / कैंटीन महँगी है / ...

Lesson 04 · Haan, lekin, nahi lagta 6w, 3m, 6m

Disagree without a fight: haan, lekin, mujhe nahi lagta.

Say this: हाँ, समझा, लेकिन मुझे उल्टा लगता है। — Yeah I get it, but I think the opposite. · मुझे नहीं लगता कि यह फेयर है। — I don’t think this is fair. · तेरी बात में दम है, फिर भी... — You’ve got a point, still…

Warm-up. Soft / hard / silent. Practise the face that stays kind. Teach. Board the three gears. Tum with friends. No tu jab. Repair आप गलत हो to मुझे नहीं लगता. Guided. Opinion tennis: uniforms, extra class Saturday. Must return with लेकिन or नहीं लगता. Free. Group of four: one hot take. Others must use three different gears. No shouting. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use हाँ लेकिन once today in a real chat — Hindi words, even if the topic is English class.

Slips. तुम गलत हो यार (as the whole reply) → मुझे नहीं लगता / हाँ लेकिन...; आई डोंट थिंक सो ओनली → मुझे नहीं लगता कि यह ठीक है

Lesson 05 · Kahani jo chale 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell a story that moves: then, after that, in the end — not three frozen facts.

Say this: पहले हम लेट निकले। — First we left late. · फिर मेट्रो भीड़ में फँस गई। — Then the metro got stuck in a crush. · आखिर में टीचर ने कुछ नहीं कहा। — In the end the teacher didn’t say anything.

Warm-up. Four pictures in the wrong order. Teams shout the Hindi order. Teach. पहले, फिर, उसके बाद, आखिर में. Past gender: फँस गया / गई if they name themselves. Guided. Partner gets three random slips (phone died, rain, cousin). Must connect with फिर. Free. 2-minute open story. If they list, teacher only says कहानी चलाओ — no grammar stop. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक असली घटना, पहले-फिर-आखिर।

Slips. मेट्रो फँस गया (if they mean the crowd/train as भीड़) → मेट्रो भीड़ में फँस गई / हम फँस गए; फिर... फिर... फिर... (no end) → आखिर में ___

Lesson 06 · Photo jaise oral 6w, 3m, 6m

Describe a photo the way an oral examiner wants: place, people, maybe.

Say this: यह किसी स्कूल के गेट की फोटो लगती है। — This looks like a school-gate photo. · दो छात्र बैग लिए खड़े हैं। — Two students are standing with bags. · शायद घंटी बजने वाली है। — Maybe the bell is about to go.

Warm-up. One still from a phone: canteen or metro. 20 seconds silent look. Teach. लगती है, दिख रहे हैं, शायद. Don’t invent a novel. Examiner Hindi: fewer English verbs. Guided. Pairs: 45s describe, 15s partner adds what was missed. Free. Mystery photo: half the class doesn’t see it. They draw from speech, then compare. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Pick a gallery photo at home: तीन हिंदी लाइनें, एक शायद।

Slips. यह फोटो स्कूल गेट → यह स्कूल गेट की फोटो लगती है; दो स्टूडेंट्स स्टैंडिंग → दो छात्र खड़े हैं

Lesson 07 · GD bina shor 6w, 3m, 6m

Join a group discussion without shouting over people.

Say this: मैं एक बात रखना चाहता हूँ / चाहती हूँ। — I’d like to put one point. · ज़रा खत्म करने दो। — Let me just finish. · रेहान की बात से सहमत हूँ। — I agree with Rehan.

Warm-up. Four on a square. Topic: canteen prices. First round they will shout — freeze, reset. Teach. Turn-taking lines. Aap if it is a formal GD; tum if it is friends. No personal roast. Guided. Token object = floor. You speak only with the token. 30 seconds max. Free. Full GD 4 minutes: phones in class. Teacher scores interruptions, not ideas. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: 30 seconds — how to enter a GD, not the topic itself.

Slips. सुन यार मैं बोल रहा (over them) → ज़रा खत्म करने दो / एक बात रखना चाहता/चाहती हूँ; सहमत हूँ रेहान से ओनली → मैं रेहान से सहमत हूँ

Lesson 08 · Sabak unchi awaaz 3m, 6m

Explain one school lesson aloud in Hindi a friend could follow.

Say this: आज साइंस में हमने पानी के चक्र की बात की। — In Science today we did the water cycle. · आसान भाषा में, भाप बनती है, फिर बारिश। — In easy language: it becomes vapour, then rain. · अगर यह याद रहे तो पेपर में आ जाएगा। — If this stays in your head, it’ll come in the paper.

Warm-up. Pick a real subject from this week. 20s English think, then Hindi only. Teach. मतलब, यानी, आसान भाषा में. Examiner may want Hindi words; Hinglish nouns (evaporation) can be glossed. Guided. Teach-back pairs: one explains, one is the sleepy friend who asks यानी? Free. Mini class: 90 seconds at the board with no notes. Audience asks one doubt in Hindi. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Explain one homework question to a sibling in Hindi, 40 seconds.

Slips. पानी साइकिल वो ना → पानी का चक्र, यानी भाप और बारिश; आज हमने किया photosynthesis ओनली → आज हमने प्रकाश-संश्लेषण किया, मतलब पेड़ खाना बनाते हैं

Lesson 09 · Phone jo solah lage 3m, 6m

Sound sixteen on the phone: not a textbook dialogue, not a five-year-old.

Say this: हेलो, अर्जुन बोल रहा हूँ। ईशान है क्या? — Hi, Arjun here. Is Ishaan there? · मैं निकल चुका हूँ, दस मिनट। — I’ve already left — ten minutes. · ओके बेस, बाद में मिलते हैं। — Okay bye, see you later.

Warm-up. Back-to-back. First call they will sound like a textbook — replay a teen model. Teach. बोल रहा/रही, निकल चुका/चुकी, बाद में. Parent call vs friend call: aap/tum shift in 5 seconds. Guided. Cards: ask for notes, cancel ground, tell mum the metro stalled. Free. Three-way: friend, then mum joins. Register must change mid-call. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Send the teacher a 25-second call that sounds like you, not a book.

Slips. मैं अर्जुन है बोल रहा → मैं अर्जुन बोल रहा/रही हूँ; ईशान है क्या होम → ईशान घर पर हैं क्या? / ईशान है?

Lesson 10 · Fare baanto 3m, 6m

Split an auto, snack, or movie fare in spoken Hindi without awkward silence.

Say this: कितना हुआ कुल? — What’s the total? · चार से बाँटें तो पचास-पचास। — Split four ways, fifty each. · मैंने पे कर दिया, तुम मुझे उधार देना। — I paid; you owe me.

Warm-up. Paper bills on a desk. Shout-add a samosa bill. Teach. कुल, बाँटना, उधार, मेरे पास छुट्टे नहीं. Numbers in Hindi at least for the total. Guided. Pair: auto 180, three people. Then canteen 90, two people, one forgot the wallet. Free. Awkward friend who never pays. Kind firm line: यार, आज अपना हिस्सा दे दे. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Next split bill, say कुल कितना हुआ out loud in Hindi.

Slips. बाँट दो पैसे → चार से बाँटें / अपना हिस्सा दे दे; I paid you give later → मैंने पे कर दिया, तुम बाद में देना

Lesson 11 · Shikayat, tameez se 3m, 6m

Make a polite complaint: cold tiffin-rice, wrong item, noisy neighbour study time.

Say this: माफ़ कीजिए, यह ठंडा आ गया है। — Excuse me, this came cold. · मैंने यह नहीं माँगा था। — I didn’t ask for this. · क्या दोबारा ला सकते हैं? — Could you bring it again?

Warm-up. Rage vs polite. Same fact, two voices. Class picks which gets the food replaced. Teach. माफ़ कीजिए + problem + ask. Aap at canteen/society office. Don’t tu the staff. Guided. Pair: wrong uniform house, then cold chai, then PTA date clash — last one to an adult. Free. Front desk: one teen complains, one staff. If rude, restart. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise one complaint line at home on a cold chapati — joke allowed, Hindi required.

Slips. यह ठंडा है यार तुम → माफ़ कीजिए, यह ठंडा आ गया है; मैंने नहीं माँगा यह → मैंने यह नहीं माँगा था

Lesson 12 · Afwaah vs khabar 3m, 6m

Mark rumour against news when teens retell school or city talk.

Say this: यह सिर्फ़ अफ़वाह लगती है। — This just sounds like a rumour. · नोटिस बोर्ड पर लिखा था, तो पक्का। — It was on the notice board, so it’s confirmed. · फॉरवर्ड मत करना। — Don’t forward it.

Warm-up. Two headlines: holiday forward vs principal circular. Sort. Teach. अफ़वाह, पक्का, लिखा था, सुना है. Ethics: no real classmate’s name in a rumour drill. Guided. Upgrade/downgrade pairs: take a spicy line and make it responsible. Free. Press corner: 60s — one rumour you will not spread, one news you will. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक फॉरवर्ड जिसका आपने इंतज़ार किया — अफ़वाह या खबर?

Slips. कल छुट्टी है भेज दो → पक्का नहीं, फॉरवर्ड मत करना; अफ़वाह है यह पक्का → यह अफ़वाह लगती है, पक्का नहीं

Lesson 13 · Agar main principal 6w, 3m, 6m

Speak as if you were principal for a day — one change, one reason, one limit.

Say this: अगर मैं प्रिंसिपल होता / होती, तो पीटी रोज न रखता / रखती। — If I were principal, I wouldn’t keep PT every day. · लेकिन टेस्ट हटा नहीं सकती, मार्क्स भी चाहिए। — But I can’t remove tests; we still need marks. · पहले छात्रों से पूछती / पूछता। — I’d ask the students first.

Warm-up. Chair as the big chair. 15-second wish, no speech yet. Teach. अगर मैं होता/होती तो. One change only. Age-safe: timings, canteen, phones, fans — not punishment fantasies. Guided. Pairs challenge: partner is a parent at PTA. Defend the change with क्योंकि. Free. Open mic 70 seconds: change + limit + one student voice you would hear. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक बदलाव, एक लेकिन।

Slips. अगर मैं प्रिंसिपल होता तो I will... → अगर मैं प्रिंसिपल होता/होती तो ___; टेस्ट हटा दूँगा पूरा → टेस्ट हटा नहीं सकती/सकता, लेकिन...

Lesson 14 · Film / series review 3m, 6m

Review a film or series in Hindi: hook, one scene, would I watch again.

Say this: मैंने वह सीरीज़ देखी। — I watched that series. · एक सीन ऐसा था कि रुक नहीं सकते। — There was one scene you can’t pause. · दूसरी बार नहीं देखूँगा / देखूँगी। — I won’t watch it a second time.

Warm-up. Title dump in 10 seconds. No 20-minute plot. Teach. देखी / देखा, किरदार, उबाऊ, ज़बरदस्त. Spoilers last, and warn पहले. Guided. Pair: 50s review. Partner may only ask अंत कैसा? or दोबारा? Free. Two teams, same well-known film. One likes, one doesn’t. लेकिन required. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: नाम, एक सीन, दोबारा हाँ या नहीं.

Slips. मैंने देखा वह सीरीज़ (if they mean the show as feminine in their speech, keep consistent) → मैंने वह सीरीज़ देखी; बहुत नाइस था ओनली → बहुत बढ़िया थी / मज़ा आया

Lesson 15 · School kaise aata hoon 3m, 6m

Tell the commute as a story: leave home, squeeze, reach gate.

Say this: सात बजकर बीस पर निकलता हूँ / निकलती हूँ। — I leave at 7:20. · मेट्रो में खड़े रहना पड़ता है। — You have to stand in the metro. · गेट पे जूते चेक होते हैं। — Shoes get checked at the gate.

Warm-up. Map four dots: bed, gate, vehicle, school. Touch and name. Teach. निकलना, चढ़ना, उतरना, पड़ना. Gender on निकला/निकलती. Auto vs van vs walk all count. Guided. Partner commute interview: कितनी देर, सबसे खराब हिस्सा, एक ट्रिक. Free. Same route, monsoon day. 60-second variant. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tomorrow on the way, voice-note one live line: अभी मैं ___ हूँ.

Slips. मैं जाता स्कूल मेट्रो → मैं मेट्रो से स्कूल जाता/जाती हूँ; खड़े रहना पड़ता → मेट्रो में खड़े रहना पड़ता है

Lesson 16 · Badon se baat 6w, 3m, 6m

Talk to adults — teacher, guard, PTA uncle, shop — with aap that doesn’t sound scared.

Say this: सर, एक मिनट मिल सकता है? — Sir, could I have a minute? · मैं समझा/समझाऊँ साफ़ भाषा में। — I’ll explain in plain language. · जी, मैं दोहराऊँ? — Ji, shall I repeat?

Warm-up. Posture 10 seconds: not a salute comedy, just still hands. Teach. आप + हैं + जी. Content can stay teen. Don’t over-shuddh. Tu to adult = instant repair. Guided. Hot seats: guard (late entry), class teacher (leave), society uncle (ball on car — keep light). Free. Double act: teen + parent at PTA. Teen speaks first; parent only if asked. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use one aap line with a real adult this week and tick it.

Slips. सर, तुम फ्री हो? → सर, आप फ्री हैं क्या? / एक मिनट मिल सकता है?; मैं बोलूँगा फास्ट → मैं साफ़-साफ़ बोलता/बोलती हूँ

Lesson 17 · CBSE-style situation 6w, 3m, 6m

Handle a board-oral style situation in Hindi: lost bag, late bus, help a stranger.

Say this: अगर मेरा बैग खो जाए, तो मैं ऑफिस जाऊँगा / जाऊँगी। — If my bag is lost, I’ll go to the office. · मैं शांत रहूँगा / रहूँगी और गार्ड को बताऊँगा। — I’ll stay calm and tell the guard. · मदद के लिए धन्यवाद। — Thank you for the help.

Warm-up. Envelope situations like orals: 30s prep, no essay. Teach. अगर + तो, पहले, फिर. Examiner Hindi: fewer yaar, more complete verbs. Hinglish nouns okay. Guided. Pairs draw a slip, 50s answer, partner asks एक सवाल. Free. Front: two situations. Teacher is the examiner face — kind, timed, no mid-correct. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक सिचुएशन, पहले-फिर, 50 सेकंड.

Slips. बैग लॉस्ट तो I will go office → अगर बैग खो जाए तो मैं ऑफिस जाऊँगा/जाऊँगी; मैं पैनिक → मैं घबराऊँगा/घबराऊँगी नहीं, पहले गार्ड को बताऊँगा

Lesson 18 · Floor pakdo 3m, 6m

Hold the floor for 90 seconds when someone tries to jump in.

Say this: एक सेकंड, खत्म करूँ। — One second, let me finish. · मेरी बात अधूरी है। — I haven’t finished my point. · अब आपकी बारी। — Now it’s your turn.

Warm-up. Partner tries to interrupt on purpose after 15 seconds. First round they will fold. Teach. Hold lines + then yield. Kind, not a fight. Gender on करूँ / रही हूँ. Guided. Trios: speaker, interrupter, referee who only says बारी. Free. Topic: Sunday markets. Speaker must land an ending sentence, then hand over. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. In any class today, finish one Hindi sentence even if someone overlaps.

Slips. यार रुक मैं... (then give up) → एक सेकंड, खत्म करूँ — मेरी बात अधूरी है; अब तुम बोल (snapped) → अब आपकी / तुम्हारी बारी

Lesson 19 · Sheher ki dikkat 3m, 6m

Talk one city problem you actually feel: air, crowd, water, parking — with a small ask.

Say this: यहाँ हवा खराब है, दौड़ना मुश्किल। — The air is bad here; running is hard. · मेट्रो अच्छी है, लेकिन स्टेशन तक पहुँचना बुरा है। — The metro is good, but getting to the station is bad. · अगर बस समय पे आए तो... — If the bus came on time…

Warm-up. Window or phone photo of a jam. One word each. Teach. समस्या, चाहिए, होना चाहिए. Not a political rally. Teen-scale ask: mask, earlier van, society park time. Guided. Pairs pick one problem, one example from this week, one realistic ask. Free. Mohalla meeting of four. One minute each, then one shared line. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक शहर-दिक्कत + एक छोटा सुझाव.

Slips. पॉल्यूशन बहुत है ओनली → यहाँ हवा बहुत खराब है; बस आना चाहिए टाइम पे → बस समय पर आनी चाहिए

Lesson 20 · Exam oral picture 6w, 3m, 6m

Do a timed exam-oral picture: 1 minute describe, 30 seconds what happens next.

Say this: फोटो में एक कैंटीन दिख रही है। — The photo shows a canteen. · लड़की पैसे गिन रही है। — A girl is counting money. · अगले पल शायद कुछ गिर जाएगा। — Next, something might fall.

Warm-up. Exam silence: 20s look, no talking. Paper-style photo (canteen / bus stop). Teach. दिख रही है, अगले पल, शायद. Full sentences. Gender of the people in the picture. Guided. Pairs time each other. Partner may not help with English words until the end. Free. Front oral: describe + next. Teacher asks क्यों लगता है? once. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Home photo, same timing: 60 + 30, send the file.

Slips. फोटो में कैंटीन → फोटो में एक कैंटीन दिख रही है; शे इज काउंटिंग मनी → लड़की पैसे गिन रही है

Lesson 21 · Teamwork boliye 3m, 6m

Talk teamwork as it is: who did what, who vanished, what you’d do next time.

Say this: हमने साथ चार्ट बनाया। — We made the chart together. · एक ने काम नहीं किया, कहना मुश्किल था। — One person didn’t work; it was hard to say. · अगली बार काम पहले बाँटेंगे। — Next time we’ll split the work first.

Warm-up. Lego-or-paper tower in 90 seconds, no English. Then sit. Teach. साथ, बाँटना, जिम्मेदारी. Soft honesty > roast. No real name-and-shame of a classmate in the room. Guided. Retell the tower: roles. Then a school project version. Free. Mediation: two partners, one missing third (empty chair). Speak to the chair in Hindi. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक टीम बात — बिना नाम के, एक सबक।

Slips. उसने कुछ नहीं किया Stupid → उसने काम नहीं किया, मुझे कहना मुश्किल लगा; नेक्स्ट टाइम वी विल स्प्लिट → अगली बार काम पहले बाँटेंगे

Lesson 22 · Imaandari 3m, 6m

Talk honesty in teen life: copied homework, extra change, a lie to a parent — no sermon voice.

Say this: मैंने होमवर्क कॉपी कर लिया, अब पछतावा है। — I copied the homework; I regret it now. · दुकानवाले ने ज़्यादा पैसे दे दिए। — The shopkeeper gave too much change. · मम्मी से झूठ बोल दिया कि टेस्ट आसान था। — I lied to Mum that the test was easy.

Warm-up. Anonymous slips in a bowl. Teacher reads two. No hunt for who wrote. Teach. पछतावा, सच बोलना, वापस करना. Keep age-safe; no crime stories. Guided. Pairs: what would you say at the kirana if you got extra twenty. Free. Hot-seat: teacher is the parent. Teen repairs the lie in Hindi. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Private note: एक छोटी ईमानदारी जो इस हफ़्ते करोगे — हिंदी में।

Slips. मैं कॉपी किया होमवर्क → मैंने होमवर्क कॉपी कर लिया; झूठ बोला मैं मम्मी → मैंने मम्मी से झूठ बोल दिया

Lesson 23 · Future insaan 3m, 6m

Talk the human future, not only a job title: how you want to treat people.

Say this: मैं वैसा इंसान बनना चाहता हूँ / चाहती हूँ जो समय पे आए। — I want to be the kind of person who shows up on time. · पैसा ज़रूरी है, लेकिन चिल्लाना नहीं। — Money matters, but not shouting. · अभी नहीं पता नौकरी, पता है दोस्तों के साथ कैसा रहना है। — I don’t know the job yet; I know how I want to be with friends.

Warm-up. Two columns: नौकरी and इंसान. Start in column two. Teach. बनना चाहता/चाहती, वैसा जो. अभी नहीं पता still valid from beginner — reuse it. Guided. Pair: one job maybe, one human must. Partner may not say that's not a career. Free. Circle: 40s. Clap for human answers as much as for engineer. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: नौकरी शायद, इंसान पक्का — दो लाइनें.

Slips. मैं सफल बनना है → मैं सफल इंसान बनना चाहता/चाहती हूँ; जॉब नहीं पता so nothing → नौकरी अभी नहीं पता, इंसान वाला पता है

Lesson 24 · Mid test 6w, 3m, 6m

Mid-course speaking test: 60s self, one photo, one opinion, tiny kind GD.

Say this: मैं ___ हूँ, मैं ___ से आता/आती हूँ। — I’m ___; I come from ___. · फोटो में ___ दिख रहा/रही है। — In the photo ___ is visible. · मुझे लगता है ___ क्योंकि ___। — I think ___ because ___. · मैं सहमत हूँ / मुझे नहीं लगता। — I agree / I don’t think so.

Warm-up. Stations warm-up: 90 seconds each, no scores yet. Teach. Teacher pad: freeze count, kyunki present, aap/tum fit, story moves. Not spelling. Guided. Pairs simulate the four parts with a kitchen timer. Free. Test: 60s self + photo 45s + opinion 40s + 3-person GD 3 minutes. Glow and one grow. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Retake only the weakest part as a voice note.

Slips. freeze then English paragraph → रुको, सोचने दो — then Hindi frames; आप सहमत हो? → आप सहमत हैं? / तुम सहमत हो?

Lesson 25 · Short mein sametna 3m, 6m

Summarise a long talk, a chapter, or a group chat in five spoken lines.

Say this: आसान भाषा में तीन बातें हैं। — In easy language there are three points. · सबसे ज़रूरी बात यह है कि... — The most important thing is… · बाकी डिटेल छोड़ सकते हैं। — We can drop the rest of the detail.

Warm-up. Teacher rambles 40 seconds on canteen. Teens hold up 3 fingers when they have the core. Teach. सबसे ज़रूरी, यानी, छोड़ सकते हैं. Not a list of twenty. Guided. Pairs: one tells a 90s weekend; other summarises in 20s. Swap. Free. Group-chat printout (fake). Summarise the fight in four lines, no extra heat. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Summarise today’s real class in five Hindi lines on voice.

Slips. उन्होंने कहा कि... कि... कि... (never ends) → सबसे ज़रूरी बात यह है कि...; समरी यह है ओनली इंग्लिश → आसान भाषा में: एक, दो, तीन

Lesson 26 · Do zindagi 3m, 6m

Compare two lives kindly: cousin in another city, hostel vs home, last year vs now.

Say this: मैं घर से आता हूँ, मेरा कज़िन हॉस्टल में है। — I come from home; my cousin is in a hostel. · उसके पास ज़्यादा समय है, मेरे पास मम्मी का खाना। — He/she has more time; I have Mum’s food. · दोनों ठीक हैं, बस अलग। — Both are fine — just different.

Warm-up. Venn in the air: two circles with hands. One fact each side. Teach. उसके पास / मेरे पास, जबकि, दोनों. No poor-them, no flex. Guided. Pairs pick two lives they know. 70 seconds. Must use लेकिन once. Free. Front: last-year-me vs now. 60s. Class asks one kind question. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: दो ज़िंदगियाँ, कोई हार-जीत नहीं.

Slips. वो लकी है I am not → उसके पास ___ है, मेरे पास ___ है; दोनों डिफरेंट ओनली → दोनों ठीक हैं, बस अलग

Lesson 27 · Announcement 6m

Give a short school or society announcement that people can actually act on.

Say this: ध्यान दीजिए। — Please pay attention. · कल पीटीए है, तीन बजे हॉल में। — PTA is tomorrow at three in the hall. · कृपया समय पर आइए। — Please come on time.

Warm-up. Bad announcement first: mumbled, no time. Class fails to act. Reset. Teach. ध्यान दीजिए, कृपया, स्थान, समय. Aap to a crowd. Write three beats only. Guided. Pairs write nothing — speak an announcement: lost bottle, match cancelled, van late. Free. Mic (bottle): stand, say, sit. If someone missed the time, it failed. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Record a 20-second announcement a junior could follow.

Slips. कल PTA है ओनली → कल पीटीए है, तीन बजे, हॉल में; एटेंशन प्लीज़ इंग्लिश होल → ध्यान दीजिए — फिर हिंदी में बात

Lesson 28 · Kathin sawal 3m, 6m

Stay in Hindi when the question is hard or personal — stall, then answer small.

Say this: यह थोड़ा पर्सनल है। — That’s a bit personal. · पूरा नहीं पता, इतना पता है... — I don’t know the whole thing; I know this much… · यह सवाल मुझे अच्छा नहीं लगा। — I didn’t like that question.

Warm-up. Teacher asks an easy one, then a too-big one (marks, family money). Model the stall. Teach. पर्सनल, इतना पता है, टालना. Age-safe: they may refuse marks or home fights. No forcing crush talk. Guided. Pairs: one is a nosy relative. Other uses a stall + a small true piece or a wall. Free. Hot-seat: two hard questions. Score courage and kindness, not secrets. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise one stall line at home if a relative pokes — Hindi first.

Slips. I don’t want to say (only English) → यह थोड़ा पर्सनल है; पता नहीं (freeze) → पूरा नहीं पता, इतना पता है कि...

Lesson 29 · Kind humour 6m

Use light humour in Hindi that doesn’t punch down.

Say this: मज़ाक में कह रहा हूँ / रही हूँ। — I’m saying this as a joke. · मैं खुद पर हँस सकता हूँ / सकती हूँ। — I can laugh at myself. · यह मज़ाक उस पर नहीं है। — That joke isn’t on them.

Warm-up. Two jokes: self vs classmate body. Class votes which stays. Teach. मज़ाक में, खुद पर. Ban look-jokes. Timing: smile, then the line, then check the room. Guided. Pairs write nothing: turn a bad-day story into one kind funny line. Free. Open mic 40s: one self-joke. If anyone’s face drops, repair aloud. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक खुद-पर मज़ाक, पाँच वाक्य से कम.

Slips. तेरी शक्ल (as humour) → मैं खुद लेट हो गया/गई, मेरी कहानी; जोक था यार क्यों सीरियस → मज़ाक में कहा, अगर बुरा लगा तो सॉरी

Lesson 30 · Exam mock, oral 6m

Sit a full short oral mock: intro, picture, situation, one opinion.

Say this: नमस्ते, मैं ___। — Namaste, I’m ___. · फोटो में ___। — In the photo ___. · अगर मैं वहाँ होता / होती... — If I were there… · धन्यवाद। — Thank you.

Warm-up. Exam room walk: chair, timer, no phone. Breathe. Teach. Register: examiner aap, fewer fillers, but matlab once is human. Gender on होता/होती. Guided. Pairs run the four parts with a printed photo and a situation slip. Free. Teacher mock for two students. Rest mark a simple rubric: start, keep going, end. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Home mock once with a parent or mirror, same four parts, send the best take.

Slips. नमस्ते आई एम → नमस्ते, मैं ___ हूँ / मैं ___; अगर मैं वहाँ था → अगर मैं वहाँ होता/होती तो...

Lesson 31 · Manch lite 6m

Public speaking lite: stand, open, one story, close — two minutes, not a debate.

Say this: आज मैं एक छोटी सी बात रखना चाहता हूँ / चाहती हूँ। — Hello — I want to put one small point today. · मेरे साथ यह हुआ... — This happened to me… · इतना ही, शुक्रिया। — That’s all — thanks.

Warm-up. Stand still 15 seconds in silence. Then sit. That’s half the fear. Teach. Open-story-close. Hands may move. Notes: three words max on a slip. Guided. Pairs coach the open and the close only. Free. Two-minute talks: topic my gali or one exam feeling. Clap the close, not the jokes only. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note standing: open, one story, close. Watch the last word.

Slips. आज I want to talk about → आज मैं ___ के बारे में दो मिनट बोलूँगा/बोलूँगी; इतना ही (and run) → इतना ही, शुक्रिया — then walk

Lesson 32 · Fluency finale 6w, 3m, 6m

Finale: two minutes fluent, a kind GD, and a photo — freeze is smaller than week one.

Say this: मैं दो मिनट में अपनी बात खत्म करूँगा / करूँगी। — I’ll finish in two minutes. · एक उदाहरण यह है... — One example is… · अब तुम्हारी राय? — Now your view?

Warm-up. Replay week-one freeze memory in 10 seconds. Laugh, then work. Teach. Remind restart lines, kyunki, aap/tum. No new grammar. Guided. Pairs: 2-min life talk + 3-min GD on phones in school. Free. Showcase to a guest or another class if possible: speech + photo + one hard question. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Final voice: two minutes, one topic they choose, one glow they write for themselves.

Slips. week-one silence → रुको, सोचने दो — मतलब...; आप क्या सोचते हो? → आप क्या सोचते हैं?

Urban Spoken Hindi · Teens · Lamba bolna, exam oral, manch

Teens · Lamba bolna, exam oral, manch

Teens 11–17 Upper Intermediate 27 lessons

Extempore, Hindi oral long turns, rebuttal, and a crash path for school orals.

Who: Teens who can already talk about school life and now need structure for Hindi orals, debates, and a short speech.

Done when: They can do a 3-minute long turn, a pair discussion, a short debate, and handle follow-up questions.

Place here when: Place here after Intermediate, or if they already argue in Hindi but ramble or stay too English-mixed for an oral.

Teacher note: A 2–3 week crash uses only lessons tagged crash. Still rehearse standing. Teach when Hinglish is fine and when the examiner wants Hindi.

Tests

Start2-minute opinion with an example and the other side.
MidOral-style long turn plus follow-up.
EndSpeech or debate plus GD.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Upper kaisi lagti hai 6w, 3m, 6m

Hear what upper-intermediate Hindi sounds like: longer turns, cleaner register when needed, fillers that think.

Say this: मैं अपनी बात तीन पॉइंट में रखूँगा / रखूँगी। — I’ll put my point in three parts. · एक तरफ़ यह है, दूसरी तरफ़ वह। — On one side this, on the other that. · मैं अभी निष्कर्ष पर नहीं पहुँचा / पहुँची। — I haven’t reached a conclusion yet.

Warm-up. Play or model 40 seconds of ramble vs 40 seconds of shaped talk on the same canteen topic. Teach. Shape: पॉइंट, उदाहरण, दूसरी तरफ़. Hinglish fine in GD; flag when an examiner wants Hindi verbs. Guided. Pairs rewrite a messy 20-second rant into three beats, then speak it. Free. Open: 90 seconds on my city morning. Class only marks shape, not accent. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक टॉपिक, तीन पॉइंट, नब्बे सेकंड.

Slips. मतलब ना यार वो ना (whole minute) → तीन पॉइंट: एक... दो... तीन...; I have three points → मैं तीन पॉइंट में रखूँगा/रखूँगी

Lesson 02 · Extempore, teen 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Do a 2-minute extempore on an urban teen slip: 30s prep, then stand.

Say this: विषय यह है... — The topic is… · मैं अनुभव से बोल रहा हूँ / रही हूँ। — I’m speaking from experience. · इसीलिए मेरी राय यह है। — That’s why my view is this.

Warm-up. Slips in a bowl: मेट्रो, ट्यूशन, फोन बैन, पीटीए, दोस्ती. Draw, breathe. Teach. Prep: definition in one line, one story, one other side. No essay quotes. Guided. Pairs time 30s prep + 90s talk. Partner asks nothing until the end. Free. Front extempore for four students. Crash energy: next slip immediately if they finish early. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Home: two random household objects as topics, 90s each, send the braver one.

Slips. विषय... (then English speech) → विषय यह है ___। मैं हिंदी में रखता/रखती हूँ; पूरा भाषण याद किया → एक अनुभव, एक राय — याद मत करो

Lesson 03 · Debate dhaancha 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Run a short debate with a visible structure: define, reason, example, close.

Say this: सभापति, मैं पक्ष में बोल रहा हूँ / रही हूँ। — Chair, I am speaking for the motion. · मेरा पहला तर्क यह है। — My first argument is this. · उदाहरण के लिए, हमारी स्कूल की कैंटीन। — For example, our school canteen. · इसीलिए प्रस्ताव सही है। — That is why the motion stands.

Warm-up. Two walls: पक्ष / विपक्ष. Topic: स्कूल में फोन पूरी तरह बंद होने चाहिए. Stand first, no talking. Teach. Four boxes on the board: परिभाषा, तर्क, उदाहरण, बंद. Aap to the chair. Tum only in the huddle. Guided. Pairs write nothing. 60 seconds to fill the four boxes out loud. Partner checks a box was skipped. Free. 4 v 4, one minute each. Chair (a teen) uses ध्यान दीजिए. Teacher only times. Crash: swap sides for a second 40-second round. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक प्रस्ताव, चार बॉक्स, 70 सेकंड।

Slips. सर मैं फॉर द मोशन → सभापति, मैं पक्ष में बोल रहा/रही हूँ; मेरा पॉइंट इज दैट → मेरा पहला तर्क यह है कि...

Lesson 04 · Pratyuttar 3m, 6m, crash

Rebut without a personal attack: hear them, name the hole, put your fact.

Say this: आपकी बात सुनी। — I heard your point. · लेकिन इसमें एक कमी है। — But there is a gap in it. · तथ्य यह है कि... — The fact is… · व्यक्ति पर नहीं, बात पर। — Not the person — the point.

Warm-up. Partner says a weak claim (canteen is free actually). First instinct roast. Freeze. Reset. Teach. सुनी, कमी, तथ्य. Ban तेरी IQ. If they used tu in a formal debate, repair to aap. Guided. Card: opponent line. 40 seconds rebuttal only — no new full speech. Free. Lightning: teacher throws a claim. Two teens rebut in 20 seconds each, then sit. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Record a 30-second rebuttal to: होमवर्क बेकार है।

Slips. तुम गलत हो पूरा → आपकी बात में एक कमी है; पर्सनल हो गए यार → व्यक्ति पर नहीं, बात पर

Lesson 05 · Lamba turn 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Hold a 3-minute long turn: hook, two examples, the other side, land.

Say this: मैं तीन मिनट लूँगा / लूँगी। — I will take three minutes. · पहला उदाहरण घर से है, दूसरा स्कूल से। — First example from home, second from school. · दूसरी तरफ़ लोग यह कहते हैं... — On the other side people say… · मैं यहीं बंद करता हूँ / करती हूँ। — I will stop here.

Warm-up. Clock on the board. 3 minutes of silence sitting — feel the length. Then stand. Teach. Map: हुक, दो उदाहरण, दूसरी तरफ़, लैंड. Fillers मतलब / यानी allowed once each, not a freeze. Guided. Pairs: listener only ticks the four parts. No mid-help. Free. Front long turn on ट्यूशन. If they finish at 1:20, teacher says अभी दो उदाहरण. Crash: second topic immediately. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Home long turn, 3:00, one urban topic, send uncut.

Slips. एक मिनट में खत्म और बैठे → पहला उदाहरण... दूसरा उदाहरण...; दूसरी तरफ़... (then freeze) → दूसरी तरफ़ लोग यह कहते हैं कि..., फिर भी मेरी राय...

Lesson 06 · Follow-up sawal 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Answer an examiner follow-up without restarting the whole speech.

Say this: आपका मतलब ___ से है क्या? — Do you mean ___? · एक छोटा सा उदाहरण और देता हूँ / देती हूँ। — I’ll give one more small example. · यह मेरे पिछले पॉइंट से जुड़ता है। — This connects to my last point.

Warm-up. Teacher asks क्यों? after a 20-second opinion. First they will rebuild from namaste. Stop them. Teach. Connective: जैसा मैंने कहा, उससे आगे. Don’t repeat the intro. Gender on देता/देती. Guided. Pairs: 40s opinion, then three follow-ups (क्यों, और कौन, अगर नहीं). Free. Hot-seat oral: 90s long turn + three follow-ups. Crash pace: next student while they still stand. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: 40s राय, then answer a parent’s क्यों? in 20s.

Slips. नमस्ते मैं फिर से शुरू करूँ → जैसा मैंने कहा... उससे आगे; क्यों? ... I think because → क्योंकि... एक और उदाहरण यह है

Lesson 07 · Current affairs, seedhi 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Talk a current city or school-level affair in simple Hindi — no TV-anchor theatre.

Say this: हाल की एक बात यह है कि... — One recent thing is… · मुझे पूरा डेटा नहीं, इतना पता है। — I don’t have the full data; I know this much. · इससे छात्रों पर यह असर पड़ता है। — This affects students like this.

Warm-up. Two slips: a real school notice vs a national headline. Pick the one they can actually speak. Teach. हाल की, असर, हो सकता है. Ban fake expert voice. If they only saw a forward, mark it अफ़वाह. Guided. Pairs: 60s — what happened, who is affected, one teen-scale opinion. Free. Open: 90s on metro fare / water tanker / board date — whatever is live this month. Other side in one line. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक हाल की बात, तीन लाइनें, बिना चैनल-वॉइस।

Slips. न्यूज़ में आया ना वो → हाल की एक बात यह है कि...; पूरा पॉलिटिकल भाषण → इतना पता है, छात्रों पर असर यह है

Lesson 08 · Social media, soch ke 3m, 6m

Give a reasoned take on social media: not I like reels, not delete-all morality.

Say this: सोशल मीडिया से मैं दोस्तों से जुड़ा रहता हूँ / जुड़ी रहती हूँ। — Social media is how I stay tied to friends. · लेकिन कमेंट वाली दुनिया बहुत तेज़ है। — But the comment world is very fast. · मैं टाइम लिमिट रखता हूँ / रखती हूँ। — I keep a time limit.

Warm-up. Phone face-down. 20 seconds: one good, one cost, no speech yet. Teach. फ़ायदा, नुक़सान, सीमा. Reasoned = क्योंकि + example of a fight or a useful note dump. Guided. Pairs steel-man the other side for 40 seconds (even if they love reels). Free. GD of five: should class groups be muted after 9 pm. Token for the floor. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक फ़ायदा, एक नुक़सान, एक निजी नियम.

Slips. सोशल मीडिया खराब है पूरा → नुक़सान यह है..., फ़ायदा यह है...; I use it for... whole minute → मैं इससे ___ के लिए इस्तेमाल करता/करती हूँ

Lesson 09 · Process bolo 3m, 6m

Explain a process in order: how you get ready, book a token, or submit homework online.

Say this: पहले यह करते हैं। — First you do this. · फिर अगला कदम यह है। — Then the next step is this. · अगर यहाँ अटक गए तो यह करो। — If you get stuck here, do this.

Warm-up. Teacher mimes metro-token badly out of order. Class shouts कौन सा कदम गायब. Teach. पहले, फिर, उसके बाद, आखिर में, अगर अटके. Examiner likes sequence words more than slang. Guided. Pairs: how to enter society after 10, or how to upload a PDF. Listener draws steps. Free. Front process: 90 seconds, no notes. If they skip a step, audience says रुक, कदम. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice-note a real process at home in four steps (chai, lock, or printer).

Slips. तो वो ना करके फिर वो → पहले... फिर... उसके बाद...; अगर stuck तो → अगर यहाँ अटक गए तो यह करो

Lesson 10 · Narrate, detail ke saath 6w, 3m, 6m

Narrate a known event with sensory detail — sound, wait, one face — not a plot dump.

Say this: मैं वहाँ खड़ा / खड़ी था जब घंटी की आवाज़ आई। — I was standing there when the bell sound came. · भीड़ की गंध और बारिश की बूंदें साथ थीं। — Crowd-smell and raindrops were together. · एक आदमी ने सिर्फ़ इतना कहा: चलो। — A man only said: let’s go.

Warm-up. Close eyes 20 seconds: last time at a station. Name one sound, one wait. Teach. जब, जहाँ, जितना. Detail ≠ poetry class. Gender on खड़ा/खड़ी, था/थी. Guided. Pairs: 90s narration of a real wait (PTA bench, clinic, metro). Partner asks एक रंग या आवाज़? Free. Open: 2-minute city wait. If it stays abstract, teacher only says आवाज़ कहाँ है. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक इंतज़ार, दो सेंसर डिटेल, नब्बे सेकंड।

Slips. हम गए फिर आए → मैं वहाँ खड़ा/खड़ी था/थी जब...; बहुत भीड़ थी ओनली → भीड़ में किसी की कोहनी लगी, फिर...

Lesson 11 · Formal vs informal 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Switch register on purpose: friend WhatsApp vs examiner vs principal.

Say this: यार, कल मत आना वो पीरियड। — Yaar, don’t come for that period tomorrow. · कल वह पीरियड नहीं होगा। — That period will not happen tomorrow. · मैं निवेदन करना चाहता हूँ / चाहती हूँ। — I wish to make a request.

Warm-up. Same fact, three badges: फ्रेंड, ओरल, ऑफिस. Walk to a badge and speak. Teach. यार / जी / निवेदन. Hinglish stays with friends. Examiner wants Hindi verbs. Principal gets aap + हैं, never tu. Guided. Pairs translate a slang line up, then a stiff line down, without sounding fake-purani Hindi. Free. Hot-seat: teacher calls the room. Teen must redo the last sentence in 8 seconds. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Send two versions of the same news: friend 15s, teacher 15s.

Slips. मैम, यार वो ना होमवर्क → मैम, होमवर्क के बारे में एक निवेदन है; एग्जामिनर को यार सुन → आप क्या जानना चाहते हैं?

Lesson 12 · Abstract GD 3m, 6m, crash

Do a GD on an abstract word — success, freedom, fairness — with a school example so it stays human.

Say this: सफलता मेरे लिए शांति भी हो सकती है। — Success for me can also be peace. · आज़ादी का मतलब देर रात फोन नहीं। — Freedom doesn’t mean phone all night. · न्याय तब जब दोनों तरफ सुना जाए। — Fairness is when both sides get heard.

Warm-up. Word on the board: सफलता. 15 seconds silent. No quote from a poster. Teach. परिभाषा + स्कूल उदाहरण + खतरा (when the word is used to shame). Keep aap in formal GD. Guided. Groups of five, token on the table. 5 minutes. If it floats away, someone must say उदाहरण. Free. Debrief: who defined, who only moralised. Crash: new word ईमान immediately. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक अमूर्त शब्द + एक स्कूल उदाहरण, 50 सेकंड।

Slips. सक्सेस मीन्स मनी ओनली → सफलता मेरे लिए ___ है, उदाहरण स्कूल से...; आजादी मतलब कुछ भी करो → आज़ादी की एक सीमा यह है कि...

Lesson 13 · Parent ko manao 3m, 6m

Persuade a parent in Hindi: ask, reason, limit, listen — not a tantrum dub.

Say this: मैं एक बात रखूँ / रखूँगा, आप सुनिए। — I’ll put one thing; please listen. · मेरी वजह यह है। — My reason is this. · अगर आप नहीं मानते तो एक बीच का रास्ता... — If you don’t agree, a middle path…

Warm-up. Two chairs: teen and parent. First 20 seconds they will whine in English. Reset. Teach. निवेदन, वजह, बीच का रास्ता, आप. Age-safe asks: extra hour out, trip, phone — not dating scripts. Guided. Pairs: 90s. Parent must say एक डर. Teen must answer the डर, not repeat the want. Free. Open: PTA-style. Class votes: did the parent feel heard? That is the score. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise one real small ask at home in Hindi this week — or voice the rehearsal.

Slips. मम्मी प्लीज ना यार → मम्मी, आप सुनिए, मेरी वजह यह है; आप नहीं समझते (snap) → अगर आप नहीं मानते तो बीच का रास्ता यह है

Lesson 14 · Data ki baat 6m

Talk simple numbers in Hindi: a survey, marks band, canteen price rise — without fake statistics.

Say this: कक्षा में बारह में से आठ सहमत थे। — Eight out of twelve in class agreed. · दाम लगभग तीस से बढ़कर पचास हो गए। — The price rose from about thirty to fifty. · मैं प्रतिशत पक्का नहीं बोल सकता / सकती। — I can’t say the percent for sure.

Warm-up. Quick hand survey: कितने लोग मेट्रो से आते हैं. Count aloud in Hindi. Teach. में से, लगभग, बढ़कर, प्रतिशत अगर आता है. Honesty line: पक्का नहीं. No invented 73.6%. Guided. Pairs turn the hand survey into a 40-second report. Free. Press: canteen samosa 20 to 35. Speak the rise + one human effect, no drama graph. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Do a 6-person WhatsApp poll and report it in a 30-second Hindi voice.

Slips. बहुत लोगों ने कहा ओनली → बारह में से आठ ने कहा; प्राइस गो अप 30 टू 50 → दाम लगभग तीस से पचास हो गए

Lesson 15 · Journalist interview 3m, 6m, crash

Interview a classmate like a junior reporter: open, two follow-ups, fair close.

Say this: मैं दो मिनट का इंटरव्यू ले रहा हूँ / रही हूँ। — I’m doing a two-minute interview. · आप यह कब से कर रहे हैं / रही हैं? — How long have you been doing this? · आखिरी सवाल, फिर मैं समेटता हूँ / समेटती हूँ। — Last question, then I’ll wrap.

Warm-up. Press-badge paper. 10 seconds: sit at an angle, not a police lamp. Teach. खुला सवाल, कब से, क्यों, आखिरी. Aap if it is formal-show; tum if friend-profile. Don’t trap them. Guided. Pairs: hobby or commute. Interviewer may not give their own speech. Free. Hot-seat: one volunteer. Two interviewers, 90s each. Crash: switch seats, new angle. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Record a 90-second interview of a family member, Hindi questions.

Slips. तो बताओ ना यार पूरा → आप यह कब से कर रहे/रही हैं?; आई हैव वन क्वेश्चन → मेरा पहला सवाल यह है...

Lesson 16 · Ethics dilemma 3m, 6m

Talk through an age-safe ethics bind: leaked paper rumour, extra change, friend bunk — and land a choice.

Say this: दो रास्ते हैं, दोनों में कीमत है। — There are two paths; both have a cost. · अगर मैं चुप रहा / रही तो दोस्त बच सकता है। — If I stay quiet, a friend might be okay. · मैं टीचर को बिना नाम बताऊँगा / बताऊँगी। — I would tell the teacher without names.

Warm-up. Slip dilemmas (no real names). 20 seconds think, no vote yet. Teach. अगर, तो, कीमत, बिना नाम. Keep it teen-scale. No crime-show plots. Gender on रहा/रही. Guided. Pairs must name both costs before choosing. Partner attacks the choice kindly. Free. Circle: one dilemma. Three speakers, 40s each. No winner clap — clap the both-costs move. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक दुविधा, दो कीमतें, एक चुनाव — 60s.

Slips. मैं टेल नहीं करूँगा कभी → अगर चुप रहा/रही तो कीमत यह है...; टीचर को स्निच → मैं बिना नाम के बात रखूँगा/रखूँगी

Lesson 17 · Slides, padho mat 6w, 3m, 6m

Speak with a slide or poster you do not read out — title, one line, look up.

Say this: स्लाइड पर सिर्फ़ तीन शब्द हैं। — There are only three words on the slide. · मैं पढ़ नहीं रहा / रही, बता रहा / रही हूँ। — I’m not reading; I’m telling. · यह तस्वीर इसलिए है क्योंकि... — This picture is here because…

Warm-up. Bad demo: teacher reads a paragraph on the board in a dead voice. Class names what died. Teach. तीन शब्द, आँखें ऊपर, तस्वीर वजह. If they have no projector, a paper poster is the slide. Guided. Pairs make a 3-word slide on paper (canteen / metro / PTA). 50s talk, partner covers the paper if they read. Free. Front: 90s with the paper on a chair. If they read, restart from the title only. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. One paper slide at home, 60s voice while the camera sees your face, not the paper.

Slips. पूरा पैरा पढ़ दिया → स्लाइड पर तीन शब्द — बाकी मैं बोलता/बोलती हूँ; यह स्लाइड शोज दैट → यह तस्वीर इसलिए है क्योंकि...

Lesson 18 · Hostile Q and A 3m, 6m, crash

Handle a sharp or unfair question: stall, reframe, answer small, don’t match the heat.

Say this: सवाल थोड़ा तेज़ है, फिर भी जवाब देता हूँ / देती हूँ। — The question is sharp; I’ll still answer. · अगर आप यह पूछ रहे हैं तो मेरा जवाब यह है। — If that’s what you’re asking, my answer is this. · मैं गरम नहीं होऊँगा / होऊँगी। — I won’t heat up.

Warm-up. Teacher asks a rude-but-safe question (you just memorised that). Model the stall smile. Teach. तेज़ सवाल, दोहराऊँ, छोटा जवाब. No clap-backs that shame. Aap stays on if the room is formal. Guided. Pairs: one is hostile uncle at PTA. Other has 20s. Then swap. Free. Panel of three. Audience throws two sharp questions. Score: stayed Hindi, stayed cool. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise one stall+reframe on voice: यह तेज़ है, फिर भी...

Slips. तुम क्या बक रहे हो (back) → सवाल तेज़ है, मेरा जवाब छोटा है...; I don’t like your tone → मैं गरम नहीं होऊँगा/होऊँगी, जवाब यह है

Lesson 19 · Compare views 3m, 6m

Compare two views fairly: a classmate’s and yours, or two news takes — then choose.

Say this: एक नज़रिया यह है कि फोन बैन ठीक है। — One view is that a phone ban is fine. · दूसरा नज़रिया यह है कि आपात में चाहिए। — The other view is you need it in an emergency. · मैं दूसरे के करीब हूँ, क्योंकि... — I sit closer to the second, because…

Warm-up. Two quote cards on the wall. Teens stand nearer the one they can steel-man, not the one they love. Teach. नज़रिया, करीब, फिर भी. Fair = you can say the other side so they would nod. Guided. Pairs: 40s other side, 40s own side. If they can’t do the first, they may not do the second. Free. Open compare: tuition every day vs twice a week. Must end with मैं ___ के करीब हूँ. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: दो नज़रिए, बिना गाली, एक चुनाव.

Slips. वो पागल है, मैं सही → एक नज़रिया..., दूसरा..., मैं दूसरे के करीब हूँ; ऑदर साइड इज रॉन्ग → दूसरे नज़रिए में दम यह है कि...

Lesson 20 · Subah sheher ki 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell a city-morning story that a listener can see: light, van, kirana shutter, school gate.

Say this: सुबह छह बजे गली अभी शांत होती है। — At six the gali is still quiet. · किराने का शटर आधा खुला रहता है। — The kirana shutter stays half open. · स्कूल गेट पे जूतों की आवाज़ इकट्ठी हो जाती है। — At the school gate shoe-sounds gather.

Warm-up. Eyes closed: the last real morning. One smell, one vehicle. Teach. होती है for habit, हुई for one day. Pick one and stay. Detail over a pile of adjectives. Guided. Pairs: 2 minutes. Partner sketches the route from speech. Free. Open 2:30 city morning. If it becomes I woke I brushed, teacher says गली कहाँ गई. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tomorrow morning, one live 20s voice from the gate or the van.

Slips. मैं उठा फिर ब्रश फिर स्कूल → सुबह गली में ___ होता है, फिर गेट पे...; वेरी ब्यूटीफुल मॉर्निंग → किराने का शटर आधा खुला रहता है

Lesson 21 · Impromptu jodi 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Do an impromptu pair talk: 20s prep together, then 2 minutes neither may freeze alone.

Say this: हम दो पॉइंट बाँटते हैं। — We’ll split two points. · तुम पहला, मैं बचाता हूँ / बचाती हूँ। — You take the first; I’ll catch the rest. · हाँ, और जोड़ूँ? — Yes — shall I add?

Warm-up. Random pairs. Topic slip. 20 seconds forehead-together, then stand. Teach. Rescue lines: हाँ, और; जैसा तुमने कहा. If one dies, the other must start in 2 seconds. Friends = tum. Guided. Rounds: canteen, metro, PTA. New partner each time. Crash pace. Free. Show two pairs. Class notes who rescued whom — that is the win. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Home: 90s with a sibling or a pillow partner, rescue line once.

Slips. तू बोल मैं सोचता (then silence) → तुम पहला पॉइंट, मैं जोड़ता/जोड़ती हूँ; उसे बोलने दो पूरा फ्रीज → हाँ, और जोड़ूँ? — then a Hindi line

Lesson 22 · Uncertainty boli 3m, 6m

Use uncertainty language that sounds intelligent, not weak: shayad, lagta hai, pukka nahi.

Say this: शायद यह सही है, पक्का नहीं। — This may be right; I’m not sure. · मुझे लगता है, अभी डेटा कम है। — I think so; the data is still thin. · मैं अनुमान लगा रहा हूँ / रही हूँ। — I’m estimating.

Warm-up. Teacher states a wild number as fact. Teens must downgrade it with one uncertainty phrase. Teach. शायद, लगभग, लगता है, अनुमान. Upper skill: not everything needs पक्का. Examiner likes this more than fake confidence. Guided. Pairs take three headlines and add a doubt layer without emptying the point. Free. Panel: city claim. Must include one hedge and one thing they will still stand on. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: एक दावा + एक शायद + एक चीज़ जो पक्की है।

Slips. पक्का सौ प्रतिशत (when they guessed) → शायद, पक्का नहीं; आई एम नॉट श्योर ओनली → मुझे लगता है, अभी पक्का नहीं कह सकता/सकती

Lesson 23 · Hansne ki timing 6m

Time humour in a speech: after a hard point, not on someone’s body, then return to sense.

Say this: यह बात भारी है, एक हल्की सी बात... — That’s a heavy point — one light line… · हँसी के बाद मैं वापस मुद्दे पर आता हूँ / आती हूँ। — After the laugh I come back to the point. · अगर हँसी नहीं आई तो चलता है। — If the laugh doesn’t come, that’s fine.

Warm-up. Two 20-second versions of the same metro story: joke first vs joke after the wait. Vote which landed. Teach. Timing beats joke quality. Ban look-jokes again. Return line is mandatory. Guided. Pairs add one light line to a serious 40s (exam fear). Partner says वापस आओ if they never return. Free. Open 90s: one humour, then the point. If the room goes quiet, use चलता है and finish. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice: भारी बात, एक हल्की लाइन, वापसी — 50s.

Slips. जोक, जोक, जोक, कोई पॉइंट नहीं → हल्की बात — फिर मुद्दे पर; हँसी नहीं आई तो पैनिक → अगर हँसी नहीं आई तो चलता है

Lesson 24 · Poora speech mock 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Give a full 3-minute speech mock with follow-up: the 6-week and 3-month finale.

Say this: सभापति, विषय यह है... — Chair, the topic is… · मैं अनुभव, तर्क और दूसरी तरफ़ रखूँगा / रखूँगी। — I’ll put experience, a reason, and the other side. · धन्यवाद। सवालों के लिए तैयार हूँ। — Thank you. I’m ready for questions.

Warm-up. Exam walk: stand, water, phone away. 30s prep on a known bank of topics. Teach. Full shape from the term. Register: formal aap. Hinglish noun okay, English verbs not the whole minute. Guided. Pairs: speech + two follow-ups. Rubric in the air: start, examples, other side, land, Q. Free. Two or three public mocks. Class writes one glow, one grow. This is the non-crash finale too. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Send the best 3-minute take plus one follow-up you ask yourself.

Slips. धन्यवाद (and sit before the end) → मैं यहीं बंद करता/करती हूँ। धन्यवाद; सवाल... English answer → सवाल का जवाब हिंदी में: ...

Lesson 25 · Crash day 1: oral skeleton crash

Exam crash day 1: rebuild intro, picture, situation, and aap in one tight hour.

Say this: नमस्ते, मैं ___ , कक्षा ___। — Namaste, I’m ___, class ___. · फोटो में सबसे पहले ___ दिख रहा / रही है। — In the photo the first thing visible is ___. · अगर यह स्थिति हो तो पहले मैं ___। — If this situation happens, first I will ___.

Warm-up. Timer from minute one. No new theory. Paper photos and situation envelopes ready. Teach. Skeleton only: 30s intro, 60s photo, 50s situation. Repair आप हो on the spot. Guided. Carousel: three stations, four minutes each, rotate. Partner is examiner face. Free. Two full skeletons at the front. Rest mark freeze count. Homework is another skeleton tonight. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tonight: one photo from the kitchen, full skeleton, uncut voice.

Slips. लंबा परिचय, फोटो अधूरी → छोटा परिचय, फोटो पूरी, स्थिति साफ़; आप कैसे हो सर → आप कैसे हैं, सर

Lesson 26 · Crash day 2: long turn + GD crash

Exam crash day 2: 3-minute long turn and a 5-minute kind GD on the same theme.

Say this: मैं फ़्लोर लेता हूँ / लेती हूँ, तीन मिनट। — I’ll take the floor for three minutes. · जीडी में मैं एक बात जोड़ूँगा / जोड़ूँगी। — In the GD I’ll add one point. · मैं किसी की बात नहीं काटूँगा / काटूँगी। — I won’t cut anyone.

Warm-up. Theme of the day on the board (phones / uniform / city air). 2 minutes silent map. Teach. Long-turn map + GD enter line. Formal aap in GD if it is an exam room. Guided. Every teen does 3:00 once. Listener only ticks parts. Then groups of four, 5:00 GD. Free. One showcase GD. Teacher notes interruptions. Second long turn if time — new angle, not a rewrite. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice: 3-minute long turn on today’s theme, then 20s enter-a-GD line.

Slips. GD में चिल्लाना → एक बात जोड़ूँगा/जोड़ूँगी — टोकन के बाद; लॉन्ग टर्न एक मिनट → तीन मिनट: उदाहरण तक जाओ

Lesson 27 · Crash day 3: full mock + close crash

Exam crash day 3: full oral mock, one hostile follow-up, and a calm close — then stop.

Say this: मैं तैयार हूँ। — I am ready. · यह आखिरी सवाल है, छोटा जवाब देता हूँ / देती हूँ। — This is the last question; I’ll give a short answer. · धन्यवाद, समय देने के लिए। — Thank you for the time.

Warm-up. Room set as oral: chair, timer, one photo, one slip. Water. No coaching faces from friends. Teach. Whole battery: intro, photo, situation or speech, two follow-ups, one sharp Q, close. Remind stall line. Guided. Half the class sits the mock; half marks a four-box rubric. Swap if the hour allows. Free. Last ten minutes: one glow each, no new tips dump. Crash ends. They know the shape. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Sleep. Optional: one 3-minute calm recap voice, not a third panic mock.

Slips. एग्जाम के बाद नई थ्योरी → आकृति याद है — नई बात मत डालो; बाय बाय सर → धन्यवाद, समय देने के लिए

Urban Spoken Hindi · Young adults · Campus se shuru

Young adults · Campus se shuru

Young adults 18–25 Beginner 30 lessons

College, commute, money, hostel or PG, and a human talk about this year.

Who: Urban young adults starting spoken Hindi seriously — campus, first job, or a year in a Hindi-using city.

Done when: They can move around campus in Hindi, split a bill, call a classmate, and give a two-minute year talk.

Place here when: Place here if they still need full-sentence building on adult topics.

Teacher note: Keep the register adult. Money and stress belong here. No children’s games unless they ask.

Tests

StartIntro, course or job, area of the city.
MidCampus help, commute, weekend, opinion.
EndShowcase plus problem role-play.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Bade logon wala namaste 6w, 3m, 6m

Greet a faculty member, a guard, and a classmate with adult namaste — aap where the room is formal.

Say this: नमस्ते। मेरा नाम आन्या है, फाइनल ईयर। — Namaste. I’m Aanya, final year. · आप कैसे हैं? — How are you? (aap) · आपसे मिलकर अच्छा लगा। — Good to meet you. · यार, कैसे हो? — Hey, how are you? (friend)

Warm-up. Two-minute stand-and-meet: name, course or first job, area of the city. No circle games. Teach. Three columns on the board: faculty / office or guard / classmate. Model आप हैं, तुम हो. Ban आप हो on day one. Guided. Rotate 40-second scenes. Partner upgrades the rude or tiny line. Free. Door: late to a lab, first day in a PG, meeting a senior in the corridor. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note, standing: namaste, name, course or job, city area — 25 seconds.

Slips. आप कैसे हो? → आप कैसे हैं?; मैं नाम आन्या → मेरा नाम आन्या है

Lesson 02 · College ya kaam ka din 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell an ordinary weekday on campus or in a first job without a school-child timetable chant.

Say this: मेरा दिन नौ बजे शुरू होता है। — My day starts at nine. · पहले लेक्चर, फिर लैब। — Lecture first, then lab. · शाम को मैं प्रोजेक्ट करता हूँ / करती हूँ। — In the evening I work on a project. · ऑफिस से सात बजे निकलता हूँ / निकलती हूँ। — I leave work at seven.

Warm-up. Each person says only the first and last thing they did yesterday. Teach. Chain with पहले / फिर / शाम को. Gender: करता / करती, जाता / जाती. Don’t chart every verb. Guided. Partner day in 50 seconds. Listener asks एक सवाल. Free. Hot-seat: ‘Kal ka din batao’ — campus or job, not a fantasy CEO diary. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tonight record four sentences: wake, travel, main block, night.

Slips. मैं जाता कॉलेज नौ → मैं नौ बजे कॉलेज जाता / जाती हूँ; मैं प्रोजेक्ट करता है → मैं प्रोजेक्ट करता / करती हूँ

Lesson 03 · Canteen, office, lab, hostel 6w, 3m, 6m

Name campus places and send someone there with aap or tum as the person requires.

Say this: कैंटीन उस विंग में है। — The canteen is in that wing. · ऑफिस पहली मंज़िल पर है। — The office is on the first floor. · लैब लॉक है क्या? — Is the lab locked? · हॉस्टल गेट से सीधा। — Straight from the hostel gate.

Warm-up. Map the room: canteen corner, office desk, lab table, hostel door. Teach. के पास / पर / में / से सीधा. Guard and clerk get aap; friends get tum. Guided. Visitor tour: 40 seconds, three places, one warning (भीड़, लॉक, लाइन). Free. Lost first-year: find the exam office, then the mess. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Walk your real campus or building and voice-note three places.

Slips. कैंटीन है वहाँ ओनली → कैंटीन उस विंग में है; ऑफिस कहाँ → ऑफिस कहाँ है?

Lesson 04 · Lecture: doubt hai, dobara 3m, 6m

Stop a lecture politely, say you have a doubt, and ask for the line again.

Say this: सर / मैम, मुझे एक डाउट है। — Sir/Ma’am, I have a doubt. · ज़रा दोबारा समझाइए। — Please explain it once more. · यह स्लाइड समझ नहीं आई। — I didn’t get this slide. · क्या मैं एक सवाल पूछ सकता हूँ / सकती हूँ? — May I ask a question?

Warm-up. Teacher talks too fast on purpose for 20 seconds. Hands up only with Hindi. Teach. Campus Hinglish डाउट is fine here. Register is aap. Gender: आया / आई, सकता / सकती. Guided. Pairs: missed word, missed formula, sitting at the back. Free. Whole-class lecture mime: three people interrupt well, one interrupts like a fight. Repair. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use one doubt line in a real class or meeting this week, or rehearse to a roommate.

Slips. सर तुम रिपीट करो → सर, ज़रा दोबारा समझाइए; मैं नहीं समझ → यह स्लाइड समझ नहीं आई

Lesson 05 · Bill baanto, UPI karo 6w, 3m, 6m

Split a canteen or mess-extra bill and finish the pay on UPI without freezing on numbers.

Say this: बिल कितना हुआ? — What’s the bill? · चार लोगों में बाँटते हैं। — Let’s split it four ways. · मैं अपना हिस्सा UPI कर देता हूँ / देती हूँ। — I’ll UPI my share. · पेमेंट आ गया, चेक कर लेना। — The payment’s come, check once.

Warm-up. Phone-calculator out. Yesterday’s real snack prices if they remember. Teach. Hinglish UPI, split, bill stay. Teach हिस्सा, बाँटना, आ गया. aap with the counter; tum with friends. Guided. Pair: one orders, one pays, one forgot their wallet. Free. Table of four: uneven bill, one person is broke, one pays and collects. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Next shared tea or snack: say the split line in Hindi, then pay.

Slips. मैं पे कर देता ओनली इंग्लिश → मैं अपना हिस्सा UPI कर देता / देती हूँ; बिल बाँटो तुम अंकल → भैया, बिल कितना हुआ?

Lesson 06 · Classmate ko phone 6w, 3m, 6m

Call a classmate: who you are, what you need, when you hang up.

Say this: हेलो, मैं रोहन बोल रहा हूँ / रही हूँ। — Hello, this is Rohan. · कल की स्लाइड्स हैं क्या? — Do you have yesterday’s slides? · दस मिनट में कैंटीन आ जा। — Come to the canteen in ten minutes. · ओके, कट करता हूँ। बाय। — Okay, I’ll cut. Bye.

Warm-up. Back-to-back chairs. No looking. Ten seconds of silence first so they feel the phone. Teach. Start with नाम. Tum with classmates. Teach a close: कट करता हूँ, मिलते हैं. Guided. Three calls: notes, timing, wrong number. Free. Missed call + callback: तुमने फोन किया था? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note that sounds like a real 20-second call, not a speech.

Slips. मैं रोहन है → मैं रोहन बोल रहा / रही हूँ; स्लाइड्स सेंड करो ना bare → कल की स्लाइड्स भेज सकते हो क्या?

Lesson 07 · Metro, bus, share 6w, 3m, 6m

Get through a city commute: ticket or card, crowd, the stop you want.

Say this: मेट्रो कार्ड में पैसे कम हैं। — There’s little money on the metro card. · यह बस यूनिवर्सिटी जाएगी? — Will this bus go to the university? · शेयर ऑटो में एक सीट है क्या? — Is there one seat in the share auto? · अगले स्टेशन पर उतरना है। — I have to get off at the next station.

Warm-up. Chairs as a metro row. One person is the door. Push without language, then add lines. Teach. aap with driver and staff. Gender: जाना है / उतरना है stays. Teach भीड़, सीट, कार्ड. Guided. Pair: wrong bus, packed coach, last-stop panic. Free. Commute story: home to campus in five sentences, including one problem. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. On one real ride this week, say one Hindi line to staff or a friend.

Slips. यहाँ रुक तुम → यहीं रुकिए; मैं उतरता अगला → मुझे अगले स्टेशन पर उतरना है

Lesson 08 · Family, ab adult hoon 3m, 6m

Talk about family as a grown person — hostel, PG, or still at home — without a nursery family song.

Say this: घर में माँ-पापा हैं। मैं हॉस्टल में हूँ। — My parents are at home. I’m in a hostel. · माँ पूछती हैं, खाना खाया? — Mum asks, have you eaten? · घरवालों को प्लेसमेंट की टेंशन है। — The family is tense about placements. · मैं अब अपने फैसले खुद करता हूँ / करती हूँ। — I make my own calls now.

Warm-up. One true sentence: who you live with this year. Teach. हैं for elders. खुद, फैसला, टेंशन. Allow ‘ghar pe tension hai’ as adult talk, not drama class. Guided. Partner: one proud line, one hard line about family, no roast of anyone’s home. Free. Phone-home role-play: you are late, broke, or changing a course. Stay kind and clear. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell a family member one real update in Hindi this week.

Slips. माँ पूछता है → माँ पूछती हैं; मैं फैसला करता है → मैं फैसले खुद करता / करती हूँ

Lesson 09 · Shopping aur return 3m, 6m

Buy something you actually need and return or exchange it without shrinking.

Say this: यह शर्ट लेनी है, एम साइज़। — I want this shirt, size M. · बिल और टैग रखना है क्या? — Should I keep the bill and tag? · एक्सचेंज हो जाएगा? — Can it be exchanged? · कल लाया / लाई था, फिट नहीं बैठा। — I brought it yesterday, it doesn’t fit.

Warm-up. Desk shop: earphones, bottle, shirt on a chair. Teach. aap with staff. लाया / लाई, बैठा / बैठी. Hinglish bill, tag, exchange are city-normal; keep the frame Hindi. Guided. Pair: buy, then return the next day. Free. Tough staff: no bill, sale item, size gone. Stay polite, don’t fold. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use एक बिल or एक एक्सचेंज line in a real shop, or rehearse at a cupboard.

Slips. एक्सचेंज करो तुम → एक्सचेंज हो जाएगा क्या?; मैं लाया कल शर्ट → मैं कल यह शर्ट लाया / लाई था / थी

Lesson 10 · Is mahine kya hai 3m, 6m

Name this month’s real load: internals, rent, fest, travel home.

Say this: इस महीने दो इंटरनल हैं। — There are two internals this month. · पंद्रह तारीख को रेंट देना है। — Rent is due on the 15th. · फेस्ट के बाद घर जाऊँगा / जाऊँगी। — I’ll go home after the fest. · प्लेसमेंट का फॉर्म अभी भरना है। — I still have to fill the placement form.

Warm-up. Phones away. Each person lists three calendar facts, not wishes. Teach. को for dates. देना है / भरना है / जाना है. Don’t teach the whole future table. Guided. Pair calendar: clash of exam and trip. Negotiate. Free. Month briefing to a roommate: what’s heavy, what can wait. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: this month in four sentences, including one money or exam fact.

Slips. इस महीने इंटरनल → इस महीने दो इंटरनल हैं; मैं जाऊँगा घर फेस्ट → फेस्ट के बाद घर जाऊँगा / जाऊँगी

Lesson 11 · Stress, FOMO, campus 3m, 6m

Name campus feelings in adult words: stress, FOMO, comparison, sleep.

Say this: मुझे टेंशन हो रही है। — I’m feeling tense. · सबकी इंटर्नशिप लग गई, FOMO हो रहा है। — Everyone got an internship; I’m getting FOMO. · नींद पूरी नहीं हो रही। — I’m not sleeping enough. · एक दिन में सब नहीं होता। — You don’t get everything done in one day.

Warm-up. Silent 30 seconds. Then one word each: टेंशन, थकान, ठीक, तुलना. Teach. मुझे ___ हो रहा है vs मैं ___ हूँ. FOMO stays; don’t force a shuddh substitute. Guided. Partner: one feeling + one small next step, not a TED talk. Free. Canteen talk: friend is spiralling on Instagram placements. You answer in Hindi, human. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Send a 20-second honest voice note: कैसे हो, सच में.

Slips. मैं टेंशन हूँ → मुझे टेंशन हो रही है; सबके पास इंटर्न मैं नो → सबकी इंटर्नशिप लग गई, मुझे FOMO हो रहा है

Lesson 12 · Faculty ko pehle bolo, phir type 3m, 6m

Speak a formal ask to faculty first, then turn it into a short mail or message — speech leads.

Say this: मैम, दो मिनट मिल सकते हैं? — Ma’am, could I have two minutes? · मैं अटेंडेंस के बारे में पूछना चाहता हूँ / चाहती हूँ। — I want to ask about attendance. · मैंने मेल ड्राफ्ट किया है, ज़रा सुन लीजिए। — I’ve drafted a mail; could you hear it? · धन्यवाद, समय देने के लिए। — Thank you for the time.

Warm-up. No laptops yet. Stand as if outside a cabin. Teach. aap + जी. Kill ‘Hi ma’am please mark my attendance’ as the whole personality. Speak, then write six lines max. Guided. Pairs: leave, mark, project deadline, recommendation. Partner is the faculty. Free. Hot-seat cabin. After the talk, they dictate the mail in spoken Hindi, not English first. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Speak one real faculty ask aloud, then send or save the short message.
