Slips. मैम यू देयर → मैम, दो मिनट मिल सकते हैं?; अटेंडेंस मार्क कर दो → मैम, अटेंडेंस के बारे में बात करनी थी

Lesson 13 · Pichhla weekend 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell last Saturday and Sunday in past time, including one ordinary detail.

Say this: शनिवार को मैं सोता रहा / सोती रही। — I slept a lot on Saturday. · रविवार को हम मॉल गए। — We went to the mall on Sunday. · मैंने असाइन्मेंट पूरा किया। — I finished the assignment. · कुछ खास नहीं किया, आराम किया। — Nothing special; I rested.

Warm-up. Two photos on a phone: crowd, empty room. What happened? Teach. गया / गई, किया / की, रहा / रही. ‘Kuchh khaas nahi’ is a full adult answer. Guided. Partner interview: three questions about last weekend, then swap. Free. Lie-and-catch: one false detail. Class asks until they find it — still in Hindi. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: last weekend, five sentences, one time word in each.

Slips. मैं गया मॉल रविवार → रविवार को हम मॉल गए; मैं असाइन्मेंट किया → मैंने असाइन्मेंट पूरा किया

Lesson 14 · Campus pe madad 6w, 3m, 6m

Ask for help on campus: office, senior, classmate, guard — right register.

Say this: क्या आप मेरी मदद कर सकते हैं? — Could you help me? · फॉर्म कहाँ जमा करना है? — Where do I submit the form? · यार, यह पोर्टल कैसे खुलता है? — Yaar, how does this portal open? · सर, यह विंडो किसकी है? — Sir, whose window is this?

Warm-up. Each person names one thing they actually needed help with this month. Teach. मदद कीजिए / कर सकते हैं (aap); मदद कर ना (tum, friends). Portal, form stay. Guided. Stations: exam cell, senior in the society, classmate, security. Free. You are new. Get from the gate to the right office in six lines. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Ask one real campus or building person for help in Hindi this week.

Slips. सर मदद करो → सर, क्या आप मदद कर सकते हैं?; फॉर्म कहाँ → फॉर्म कहाँ जमा करना है?

Lesson 15 · Rent, split, broke 3m, 6m

Talk student money: rent, split groceries, being short this week — without shame theatre.

Say this: रेंट बाँटना है, पंद्रह तारीख तक। — We have to split rent by the 15th. · इस हफ्ते पैसा टाइट है। — Money is tight this week. · किराने का बिल मेरे UPI पर आ गया। — The kirana bill came on my UPI. · उधार बाद में दिया तो चल जाएगा। — If you pay me back later, that’s fine.

Warm-up. Anonymous slip: one money stress. Teacher reads a few aloud with no names. Teach. टाइट, उधार, बाँटना, हिस्सा. Dignity. Nobody has to disclose real numbers. Guided. Roommate money meeting: wifi, gas, extra guest. Free. Broke friend at canteen: how to say no to a plan without a fight. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. One money sentence you could actually say to a roommate, recorded once.

Slips. मैं ब्रोके हूँ ओनली → इस हफ्ते पैसा टाइट है; तू पैसे दे अभी → जब हो, अपना हिस्सा भेज देना

Lesson 16 · Gym ya sofa 6m

Talk body and routine as an adult: gym, walk, or no gym — no fitness-ad voice.

Say this: मैं जिम जाता हूँ / जाती हूँ, हफ्ते में तीन दिन। — I go to the gym three days a week. · समय नहीं मिलता, सिर्फ चलता हूँ / चलती हूँ। — I don’t get time; I only walk. · आज बॉडी सो रही है। — The body’s tired today. · तुम साथ चलोगे / चलोगी? — Will you come along?

Warm-up. Stand or sit: who moved their body yesterday? One word. Teach. हफ्ते में ___ दिन. Allow ‘main nahi jaata’ as a complete, unapologetic line. Guided. Pair plans: morning walk vs night hostel stairs vs nothing this week. Free. Friend shames your food. Answer in Hindi without a speech on health. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tomorrow night: two sentences on what you did or didn’t do with your body.

Slips. मैं जिम जाता है → मैं जिम जाता / जाती हूँ; बॉडी टायर्ड → आज बॉडी सो रही है / थकान है

Lesson 17 · Sheher nikalna 3m, 6m

Plan a city outing: place, money, last metro, who is late.

Say this: चलो शहर घूमते हैं। — Let’s go out in the city. · आखिरी मेट्रो दस पैंतालीस की है। — The last metro is at 10:45. · प्रति व्यक्ति कितना लगेगा? — How much per person? · देर मत करना, गेट पर मिलते हैं। — Don’t be late; we meet at the gate.

Warm-up. Board three real city places students name. Vote by speaking, not by emoji. Teach. प्रति व्यक्ति, आखिरी, गेट पर. Tum with friends. One person must say a budget. Guided. Group of three: one is broke, one has a curfew, one wants night food. Free. Plan collapses: rain or last-metro miss. Repair the evening in Hindi. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Propose one real outing line to a friend in Hindi, even if the plan is next month.

Slips. चलो आउटिंग ओनली → चलो शहर घूमते हैं; लास्ट मेट्रो मिस मत → आखिरी मेट्रो मत चूकना

Lesson 18 · ID khoi, internal late 6w, 3m, 6m

Handle a campus problem: lost ID, late to an internal, missed biometric — clear, not dramatic.

Say this: मेरा आईडी कार्ड खो गया। — My ID card is lost. · सर, मैं इंटरनल के लिए पाँच मिनट लेट हूँ। — Sir, I’m five minutes late for the internal. · बायोमेट्रिक नहीं लगा। — The biometric didn’t mark. · डुप्लिकेट के लिए कहाँ जाऊँ? — Where do I go for a duplicate?

Warm-up. Teacher drops a card on the floor. Panic mime, then language. Teach. खो गया / गई. aap in the office. Short reason, then the ask. No ten-minute story. Guided. Pairs: ID, late internal, locked lab, wrong room. Free. Office window: clerk is busy. You hold your line and repeat once. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Rehearse the ID or late line once standing, as if the clerk is in front of you.

Slips. सर आई एम लेट → सर, मैं पाँच मिनट लेट हूँ; आईडी लॉस्ट हो गया मेरा → मेरा आईडी कार्ड खो गया

Lesson 19 · Campus ki raay 3m, 6m

Give a campus opinion with because, and hear the other side without a fight.

Say this: मुझे लगता है अटेंडेंस सख्त है। — I think attendance is strict. · क्योंकि लोग बीमार भी होते हैं। — Because people also get ill. · है ना, लेकिन लैब बिना आए नहीं चलती। — True, but the lab doesn’t work if you don’t come. · मैं इससे सहमत नहीं। — I don’t agree with that.

Warm-up. One prompt on the board: mess food / 75% attendance / placements start too early. Teach. मुझे लगता है + क्योंकि + है ना लेकिन. Teach सहमत / सहमत नहीं. No debate-club shouting. Guided. Pairs: 90 seconds each side, then swap opinions on purpose. Free. Tiny GD of four: one topic, everyone speaks once before anyone speaks twice. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: one campus opinion + kyunki + the other side in one line.

Slips. मुझे लगता अटेंडेंस → मुझे लगता है अटेंडेंस सख्त है; आई डोंट अग्री → मैं इससे सहमत नहीं

Lesson 20 · Review aur test 6w, 3m, 6m

Reuse help, commute, weekend, and one opinion — then a short spoken check.

Say this: मदद कीजिए। पिछले शनिवार को ___। मेट्रो में ___। मुझे लगता है ___।

Warm-up. Station rotation, three minutes: office help, metro, weekend, opinion. Teach. Teacher pad: who still says आप हो, who still plans every word in English. Guided. Pairs run the four beats without reading. Free. Mid test: two minutes + one problem role-play (ID or bill). Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Re-record the weakest of the four beats, standing.

Slips. Only English glue → Keep Hindi frames; one English noun is fine; आप कैसे हो still → आप कैसे हैं

Lesson 21 · Hostel, PG, roommate 3m, 6m

Talk hostel or PG life: water, noise, landlady, lights-out, sharing a fridge.

Say this: पानी ऊपर के फ्लोर पर नहीं आ रहा। — Water isn’t coming on the upper floor. · रूममेट रात भर लैपटॉप चलाते हैं / चलाती हैं। — My roommate runs the laptop all night. · आंटी, वाई-फाई फिर गया है। — Aunty, the wifi is down again. · फ्रिज में मेरा डिब्बा पीछे है। — My box is at the back of the fridge.

Warm-up. One true hostel/PG/home-share sentence. Paying-guest and home students both speak. Teach. aap with warden and landlady. Tum with roommate unless the house is formal. चलाते / चलाती. Guided. Complaint without a war: water, noise, guests, cleaning turn. Free. House meeting of three: one rule you want, one you can drop. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Say one real share-house line this week, or voice-note it if you live alone.

Slips. आंटी वाई-फाई फिक्स करो → आंटी, वाई-फाई फिर गया है, देख दीजिए; रूममेट नॉइज़ी है ओनली → रूममेट रात भर लैपटॉप चलाते हैं

Lesson 22 · Library aur project 3m, 6m

Ask for a seat, a book, a deadline, and a fair split of project work.

Say this: यहाँ बैठ सकते हैं क्या? — Can we sit here? · यह बुक इश्यू होगी? — Can this book be issued? · सबमिशन शुक्रवार को है। — Submission is on Friday. · काम बाँट लेते हैं, मेरा हिस्सा स्लाइड्स। — Let’s split the work; my part is slides.

Warm-up. Table as silent library. Whisper only after the teacher models volume. Teach. इश्यू, सबमिशन stay. हिस्सा, बाँटना. Gender of बुक often यह किताब / यह बुक. Guided. Pair: one is free-riding. Ask for work in Hindi, not a paragraph of English anger. Free. Project meet: three people, ten minutes of class time, one spoken plan at the end. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Send your group one Hindi line about who does what this week.

Slips. यहाँ बैठ सकते → यहाँ बैठ सकते हैं क्या?; तू स्लाइड्स कर दे पूरा → काम बाँट लेते हैं, स्लाइड्स मैं लेता / लेती हूँ

Lesson 23 · Part-time job ki Hindi 6m

Handle a campus or café part-time shift in Hindi: late, swap, pay, customer.

Say this: आज शिफ्ट पाँच से नौ है। — Today’s shift is five to nine. · क्या कोई स्वैप करेगा? — Will someone swap? · स्टाइपेंड कब आएगा? — When will the stipend come? · भैया, दो मिनट, ऑर्डर नोट कर लेता हूँ / लेती हूँ। — Two minutes, I’ll note the order.

Warm-up. Who has earned money this year? One sentence, no numbers required. Teach. aap with customers and a manager; tum with co-workers if they are peers. Guided. Scenes: late to shift, wrong order, pay delayed. Free. You need a day off for an internal. Ask without a speech. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: your shift or a friend’s shift in four Hindi lines.

Slips. मैनेजर आई विल बी लेट → सर, आज मैं बीस मिनट लेट पहुँचूँगा / पहुँचूँगी; पैसा कब → स्टाइपेंड कब आएगा?

Lesson 24 · Fest aur club 6m

Talk a college fest or society: volunteer, stall, crowd, a fight over work.

Say this: मैं ड्रामा सोसाइटी में हूँ। — I’m in the drama society. · फेस्ट में वॉलंटियर था / थी, गेट पर। — I volunteered at the fest, on the gate. · भीड़ संभालनी पड़ी। — We had to handle a crowd. · मीटिंग बिना बताए मत छोड़ना। — Don’t skip the meeting without saying.

Warm-up. Name one club you are in or refused. ‘Main kisi club mein nahi’ is complete. Teach. वॉलंटियर, सोसाइटी stay. पड़ी / पड़ा. Formal aap only if a faculty coord is in the scene. Guided. Stall role-play: sell a ticket, refuse a pass, call a senior. Free. After-fest debrief: one thing that worked, one mess, no English dump. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell someone at home what your fest or club actually does, in Hindi.

Slips. मैं सोसाइटी में हूँ ओनली → मैं ड्रामा सोसाइटी में हूँ; भीड़ हैंडल किया → भीड़ संभालनी पड़ी

Lesson 25 · Clinic, dispensary 3m, 6m

Speak at a campus clinic: symptom, since when, certificate, pharmacy.

Say this: मुझे दो दिन से बुखार है। — I’ve had fever for two days. · पेट खराब है, खाना नहीं उतर रहा। — My stomach is off; food isn’t sitting. · सर्टिफिकेट चाहिए, इंटरनल है। — I need a certificate; I have an internal. · यह दवाई कब तक लेनी है? — How long do I take this medicine?

Warm-up. Body scan: head, stomach, throat, sleep. Point, then name. Teach. को / से for time. aap with the doctor. Gender: रहा / रही on उतर रहा. Guided. Pair clinic: fever, injury, anxiety that won’t let you sit an exam — keep it respectful. Free. Pharmacy window after: time, food, drowsiness. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. If you are well, still record the fever-and-certificate lines once.

Slips. मैं फीवर हूँ → मुझे बुखार है; सर्टिफिकेट दे दो → सर / मैम, सर्टिफिकेट चाहिए

Lesson 26 · Agar internship ho 6m

Talk about an internship you have, want, or missed — first-job Hindi, not a brochure.

Say this: अगर इंटर्नशिप लग गई तो मैं शहर शिफ्ट करूँगा / करूँगी। — If I get the internship, I’ll shift cities. · वहाँ हिंदी में बात करनी पड़ सकती है। — I may have to speak Hindi there. · स्टाइपेंड कम है, लेकिन सीखने को मिलेगा। — The stipend is low, but I’ll learn. · नहीं लगी तो प्रोजेक्ट से दिखाऊँगा / दिखाऊँगी। — If it doesn’t come, I’ll show a project.

Warm-up. Hands: got / waiting / not this year. All three are normal in this room. Teach. अगर + तो. पड़ सकती है. No corporate cartoon: ‘I want to add value to your esteemed org’. Guided. Pair: tell the internship in 60 seconds as if to an aunt, then as if to a panel. Free. Offer is unpaid far from home. Think aloud in Hindi: take or leave, one reason. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: internship status + one Hindi sentence about why it matters.

Slips. इफ आई गेट इंटर्न → अगर इंटर्नशिप लग गई तो...; आई विल लर्न अ लॉट → वहाँ बहुत कुछ सीखने को मिलेगा

Lesson 27 · Woh insaan jo yaad rahe 6m

Speak about a person you admire with proof, not a slogan.

Say this: मैं अपने दादा को देखता हूँ / देखती हूँ। — I look at my grandfather. · वह शांत रहते हैं, लेकिन काम पूरा करते हैं। — He stays calm, but he finishes the work. · मेरी सीनियर साफ़ बोलती हैं। — My senior speaks clearly. · मुझे वह आदत लेनी है। — I want that habit.

Warm-up. Name is enough to start. No celebrity compulsory; a lab tech or a parent is better. Teach. रहते हैं for elders. आदत, Saboot: one thing they do, not ‘they are inspirational’. Guided. Partner asks क्यों two times. Answer with actions. Free. Ninety seconds: who, what they do, what you stole from them. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell the person, or tell a roommate about them, in five Hindi lines.

Slips. वे वेरी इंस्पायरिंग हैं → वह शांत रहते हैं और काम पूरा करते हैं; मुझे लाइक है उनको → मैं उनसे यह आदत लेना चाहता / चाहती हूँ

Lesson 28 · Ek khabar jo samjha sakun 6m

Explain one news item you actually understand, in campus Hindi, not TV-anchor theatre.

Say this: आज की बात यह है कि मेट्रो का किराया बढ़ा। — Today’s thing is that metro fares went up. · इससे छात्रों का खर्च बढ़ेगा। — Students’ costs will go up. · कुछ लोग कह रहे हैं यह ज़रूरी है। — Some people say it’s needed. · मुझे पूरा नहीं पता, लेकिन इतना समझ आया। — I don’t know it all, but I got this much.

Warm-up. Phones: one headline each. If they didn’t read news, use campus news: bus, wifi, strike. Teach. बढ़ा / बढ़ी, कह रहे हैं. Honour मुझे पूरा नहीं पता. Kill copied English headlines said in a Hindi accent. Guided. Pair: 60-second briefing, then one question. Free. Class chooses two stories. Two briefings, then ‘other side’ in one line. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tomorrow: 40-second voice note on one story, ending with what you don’t know.

Slips. मेट्रो प्राइस इनक्रीज़ → मेट्रो का किराया बढ़ा; आई डोंट नो मuch → मुझे पूरा नहीं पता, लेकिन इतना समझ आया

Lesson 29 · Is saal ka workshop 6w, 3m, 6m

Build a two-minute talk on this year: campus or first job, one problem, one opinion, a close.

Say this: नमस्ते। इस साल मैं ___ में हूँ। — Namaste. This year I’m in ___. · एक दिक्कत यह रही कि ___। — One problem was ___. · मुझे लगता है ___। — I think ___. · आगे मैं ___ करना चाहता हूँ / चाहती हूँ। — Next I want to ___.

Warm-up. Teacher gives a messy model, not a perfect paragraph. Teach. Five keywords on a card, not a script. Repair aap/tum only if they greet a formal room. Guided. Pairs + one question from the listener. Free. Half-class dress rehearsal, standing. Clap for finishing, not for shuddh words. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Full talk once at home, standing, phone on the table.

Slips. Memorising a paragraph → Speak from five words; Only name and stop → Add a problem and an opinion

Lesson 30 · Finale — yeh saal 6w, 3m, 6m

Give the year talk and one problem role-play; leave knowing the next course.

Say this: Two-minute year talk plus ID, bill, or faculty cabin.

Warm-up. Quiet waiting. Water. Names in a hat if the group is shy. Teach. Score courage, full sentences, aap/tum choice — not accent theatre. Guided. Role-play after the talk: lost ID or split bill or late internal. Free. Circle: मैं हिंदी में अपने साल की बात कर सकता / सकती हूँ. Point to Fluency if they already understand more than they say. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. No homework. Rest. If they still build every sentence from English, Fluency is next.

Slips. Failing someone for Hinglish in canteen → Mark the formal scene separately; Correcting on stage → Note one glow and one grow after

Urban Spoken Hindi · Young adults · Jo aata hai, use bolo

Young adults · Jo aata hai, use bolo

Young adults 18–25 Intermediate fluency 24 lessons

Kill the freeze. Proof stories, GDs, aunt-test explanations, meeting one-liners.

Who: Young adults who understand Hindi films and family talk and still translate every sentence from English before they speak.

Done when: They can talk for three minutes, tell a proof story, join a GD, and explain their subject in plain Hindi.

Place here when: The default course for ‘I understand Hindi but I can’t speak’ at this age.

Teacher note: No mid-sentence correction. Simpler Hindi is a win. Then send them to Interview if they have placements that need Hindi.

Tests

Start90 seconds on a known topic. Time the first pause.
MidIntro, STAR, opinion, GD.
EndShowcase plus a hard question.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Samajhta hoon, ab bolo 6w, 3m, 6m

Admit you already understand, then speak before the English draft arrives.

Say this: मैं समझता हूँ / समझती हूँ, अटक जाता हूँ / जाती हूँ। — I understand; I get stuck. · पहले हिंदी में कोशिश। — Hindi first. · अंग्रेज़ी बाद में। — English later. · एक वाक्य काफी है। — One sentence is enough.

Warm-up. 90 seconds on ‘mera campus / meri pehli job’. Teacher times the first long pause. No mid-sentence fix. Teach. Rule of the course: silence after a question is allowed for three seconds, then any Hindi noun + hai. Kill the inner translator out loud. Guided. Pair: speaker may not say ‘wait’ in English. Listener only nods or asks क्यों? Free. Rapid objects: canteen tray, metro card, offer letter — 20 seconds each, no planning. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note with zero script: 40 seconds on today. Send the first take.

Slips. Wait, how do I say → एक शब्द बोलो, फिर वाक्य; Long English draft, then Hindi → पहले अधूरा हिंदी वाक्य

Lesson 02 · Campus ya pehli naukri — main kaun 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell who you are now: course or first job, city, what you actually do all day.

Say this: मैं सिविल में हूँ, तीसरे साल। — I’m in civil, third year. · अभी इंटर्न हूँ, रिपोर्टिंग सीख रहा हूँ / रही हूँ। — I’m an intern right now, learning reporting. · दिन का बड़ा हिस्सा लैब या एक्सेल में जाता है। — A big part of the day goes in the lab or Excel. · घर से बाहर पहला साल है। — It’s the first year away from home.

Warm-up. Cross out cartoon lines: ‘I am a passionate go-getter’. Teach. हूँ / रहा हूँ. One concrete noun per sentence. Excel, lab, intern stay. Guided. Partner writes only the English ghosts they hear, then the speaker retries in Hindi. Free. Two minutes. If they freeze, point to the board: नाम, जगह, काम, एक सच. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Re-record the intro. Cut any sentence you cannot explain to an aunt.

Slips. आई एम अ हार्डवर्किंग स्टूडेंट → मैं सिविल में हूँ, लैब का काम करता / करती हूँ; मेरा दिन बहुत बिज़ी रहता → दिन का बड़ा हिस्सा लैब में जाता है

Lesson 03 · Proof wali kahani 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell a proof story: what was on, what you did, what changed — human, not a corporate STAR cartoon.

Say this: एक बार ग्रुप में काम रुक गया था। — Once the group work had stopped. · मैंने मीटिंग बुलाई। — I called a meeting. · हमने काम बाँटा। — We split the work. · सबमिशन टाइम पर हुआ। — The submission happened on time.

Warm-up. Each person names a small real win: fest gate, lab fix, late roommate, a customer. Teach. Shape on the board: कब, मैंने क्या किया, फिर क्या हुआ. The English word STAR only if it helps; never as a chant. Guided. Ninety seconds. Listener asks और तुमने क्या किया? if the story is only ‘we’. Free. Second round: same story, half the time, still with one I-did line. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Write five Hindi keywords only. Tell the story once to a wall, once to a phone.

Slips. सिचुएशन वाज़ चैलेंजिंग → काम रुक गया था; आई टुक द इनिशिएटिव → मैंने मीटिंग बुलाई

Lesson 04 · Padhai-kaam ki raay 6w, 3m, 6m

Hold an opinion on study or work for a minute, with one example from this year.

Say this: ऑनलाइन क्लास से मुझे कम समझ आता है। — I understand less from online class. · ऑफिस में देर तक बैठना काम नहीं है। — Sitting late in the office is not the work. · इंटर्नशिप बिना पे के भी कभी सीख देती है। — An unpaid intern can still teach you, sometimes. · लेकिन यह सबके लिए सही नहीं। — But this is not right for everyone.

Warm-up. Board: WFH, attendance, unpaid intern, night-outs before internals. They pick, they speak. Teach. लेकिन / इसके बावजूद / उदाहरण. No essay. One story beat is better than three slogans. Guided. Devil’s pair: you must argue the side you dislike for 45 seconds. Free. Open chair: 70 seconds, then two questions from the room. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: opinion, example, other side — under 80 seconds.

Slips. आई थिंक ऑनलाइन इज़ बैड → ऑनलाइन क्लास से मुझे कम समझ आता है; इन कनक्लूज़न → मेरी बात बस इतनी है कि...

Lesson 05 · GD, insaan ki tarah 6w, 3m, 6m

Join a group discussion like a person: enter, add, disagree, let someone in.

Say this: मैं एक बात जोड़ूँ? — May I add something? · आपकी बात सही है, बस एक फर्क है। — You’re right; there’s just one difference. · राघव, तुम कुछ कहना चाहते थे। — Raghav, you wanted to say something. · समय कम है, निष्कर्ष की तरफ चलें? — Time is short; shall we move to a close?

Warm-up. Four chairs. Topic they care about: intern stipend, campus politics, city rents. Teach. Teach entry and invite-in. Ban the phrase ‘As I mentioned earlier’ on loop. aap in a formal GD; tum if the room is friends. Guided. Two-minute GD. Teacher only notes who never entered and who ate the air. Free. Second GD, new topic, roles: quiet one must open, loud one must invite. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Alone, practise the entry line and the invite-in line twice each.

Slips. सो बेसिकली आई फील → मुझे एक बात कहनी है; यू आर रॉन्ग → मैं इससे सहमत नहीं, क्योंकि...

Lesson 06 · Subject chachi ko samjhao 6w, 3m, 6m

Explain your subject or job to an aunt who is sharp and not in your field.

Say this: चीज़ी बात में, मैं पुलों की मजबूती देखता हूँ / देखती हूँ। — Simply: I look at how strong bridges are. · एक्सेल में नंबर नहीं, कहानी देखनी होती है। — In Excel you have to see the story, not just the number. · मेरा काम लोगों को फोन पर शांत करना भी है। — My job is also to calm people on the phone. · जटिल शब्द बाद में। पहले काम। — Jargon later. Work first.

Warm-up. Teacher is the aunt: ‘Beta yeh MBA / coding / law hai kya?’ They may not hide in English. Teach. चीज़ी बात में / आसान भाषा में. If they cannot do it, they don’t own the subject yet — that’s the point, not shame. Guided. Pair: 90 seconds, then aunt asks क्यों तीन बार. Free. Hot-seat aunt. Jargon jar: every English technical word costs a Hindi example. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Call or voice-note a family member: your subject in six simple Hindi sentences.

Slips. इट्स लाइक अ सॉफ्टवेयर किंड ऑफ → मैं एक ऐप ठीक करता / करती हूँ जिससे लोग टिकट काट सकें; वेरी टेक्निकल है आंटी → आसान भाषा में, मैं ___ करता / करती हूँ

Lesson 07 · Lab ya office ka phone 3m, 6m

Make and take a short work or lab call: name, reason, next step, close.

Say this: नमस्ते, मैं अदिति, सिविल लैब से बोल रही हूँ। — Hello, this is Aditi from the civil lab. · सैंपल तैयार नहीं आया। — The sample hasn’t come. · क्या चार बजे तक हो जाएगा? — Will it be done by four? · ठीक है, मैं मेल पर लिख देती हूँ। — Okay, I’ll put it on mail.

Warm-up. Back-to-back. First round they may read; second they may not. Teach. aap on a work phone. Close with अगला कदम. No five-minute biography. Guided. Calls: vendor late, faculty not answering, teammate missing the slot. Free. Bad line / freeze: कहिए, आवाज़ कट रही है, दोबारा बोलिए. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Record one 25-second work call. Listen once. Re-do only the opening.

Slips. हू इज़ दिस → नमस्ते, मैं अदिति बोल रही हूँ, लैब से; आई विल मेल यू → मैं मेल पर लिख देती / देता हूँ

Lesson 08 · Meeting ki ek line 6w, 3m, 6m

Put one useful sentence into a meeting: update, ask, or block — then stop.

Say this: मेरी तरफ से अपडेट: ड्राफ्ट तैयार है। — Update from me: the draft is ready. · मुझे एक क्लैरिटी चाहिए। — I need one clarity. · यह शुक्रवार तक नहीं होगा। — This won’t happen by Friday. · मैं नोट कर लेता हूँ / लेती हूँ। — I’ll note that.

Warm-up. Stand-up circle. Each person, one sentence, sit. If they pad, they stand again. Teach. Kill office padding: ‘I just wanted to quickly kind of say’. Board the four verbs: तैयार, चाहिए, नहीं होगा, नोट. Guided. Fake meeting of five. Only one useful line each, then a 20-second version if asked. Free. Chair goes quiet. Someone must ask the date. Practise being that person. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. In a real group chat or meet this week, say one Hindi update out loud or on a voice note.

Slips. सो आई जस्ट वांटेड टु से → मेरी तरफ से अपडेट: ...; वी विल लुक इंटू इट → मैं शुक्रवार तक चेक करके बताता / बताती हूँ

Lesson 09 · Feedback lo, do 6w, 3m, 6m

Give and take feedback without disappearing or performing a roast.

Say this: एक चीज़ अच्छी थी: तुम समय पर आए। — One good thing: you came on time. · एक चीज़ बदलो: स्लाइड पर लिखकर मत पढ़ो। — Change one thing: don’t read off the slide. · यह सुनकर ठीक नहीं लगा, लेकिन समझ गया / गई। — That didn’t feel good to hear, but I got it. · मैं अगली बार यह आज़माऊँगा / आज़माऊँगी। — I’ll try this next time.

Warm-up. Last class’s talk in memory. Nobody names a person until they have one glow. Teach. एक अच्छी, एक बदलो. Formal aap if the person is senior. Tum for peers. Guided. Pairs on a 30-second retell. Feedback, then the speaker repeats the grow line. Free. Hard version: you disagree with the feedback. Stay in Hindi, stay adult. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Send one glow-and-grow voice note about your own last talk.

Slips. यू हैव टु बी मोर कॉन्फिडेंट → आवाज़ थोड़ी बड़ी करो, हम तक आनी चाहिए; ओके ओके सॉरी सॉरी → समझ गया / गई। अगली बार स्लाइड नहीं पढ़ूँगा / पढ़ूँगी

Lesson 10 · Jawani ke paise 3m, 6m

Talk money as a young worker or intern: stipend, split, send-home, wait for salary.

Say this: स्टाइपेंड दस तारीख को आता है। — The stipend comes on the 10th. · घर कुछ भेजता हूँ / भेजती हूँ। — I send something home. · इस महीने उधार नहीं लेना। — I don’t want to borrow this month. · सैलरी कट गई, ट्रैवल पर। — Salary got cut, on travel.

Warm-up. Optional numbers. Dignity first. Who supports whom is a fact, not a joke. Teach. आता है, भेजता हूँ, उधार, कट गई. Mix of hostel and home-living students. Guided. Pair: delayed stipend vs friends who want a trip. Free. You are the first earner in the house, or you still take money from home. Say it cleanly. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. One true money sentence you could say to a parent or a roommate, recorded once.

Slips. आई गेट टेन के → स्टाइपेंड दस हज़ार आता है; मनी टाइट यार ओनली → इस महीने पैसा टाइट है, ट्रिप नहीं कर पाऊँगा / पाऊँगी

Lesson 11 · News briefing 3m, 6m

Give a 60-second news briefing a teammate could use: fact, who is affected, what you don’t know.

Say this: दो मिनट में खबर यह है। — In two minutes, the news is this. · किसान / स्टूडेंट / ऑफिस — असर उन पर है। — The effect is on them. · सरकार कह रही है ___। — The government is saying ___. · पक्का नहीं, सोर्स एक है। — Not sure; there’s one source.

Warm-up. Three headlines the teacher brings (campus or city). No TV shouting. Teach. कह रही है / कह रहे हैं. Honour uncertainty. Kill copied English ledes. Guided. Brief a partner who will retell. If the retell dies, the briefing failed. Free. Panel of two: they brief the class, then take one hard ‘pata nahi kya?’ question. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Morning voice note: one story, fact then doubt.

Slips. ब्रेकिंग न्यूज़ फ्रेंड्स → दो मिनट में खबर यह है; आई रीड समवेयर → एक जगह पढ़ा, पक्का नहीं

Lesson 12 · Agar main hire karta 6m

Say who you would hire at 21, with proof, not vibes.

Say this: अगर मैं रखता / रखती, तो उसको रखता / रखती जो काम खत्म करता है। — If I were hiring, I’d take the one who finishes. · डिग्री से ज़्यादा मुझे समय पर आना चाहिए। — More than the degree, I need them on time. · एक सवाल पूछूँगा: पिछले महीने क्या बनाया? — I’d ask: what did you make last month? · मीठी बात से काम नहीं चलता। — Sweet talk doesn’t run the work.

Warm-up. Two imaginary CVs on the board: shiny intern vs messy-but-done project. Teach. अगर मैं + तो. चाहिए vs रखना. This is opinion practice, not HR training. Guided. Pairs hire for a fest stall or a lab helper. They must refuse one person kindly. Free. Hot-seat founder. Class pitches. Founder answers in Hindi only. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: three Hindi lines on who you would hire and one question you’d ask.

Slips. आई लुक फॉर पैशन → मुझे वह चाहिए जो काम खत्म करे; कल्चर फिट → टीम के साथ बिना लड़े काम करे

Lesson 13 · Mushkil log 3m, 6m

Talk about a difficult person at work or campus without a rant and without becoming sweet-fake.

Say this: एक साथी काम टालते हैं। — One teammate keeps putting work off. · मैंने साफ़ कहा, यह तेरा हिस्सा है। — I said clearly, this is your part. · चिल्लाने से बात नहीं बनी। — Shouting didn’t help. · अगली बार मैं लिखकर दूँगा / दूँगी। — Next time I’ll give it in writing.

Warm-up. No names of people in the room. Invent or blur. Teach. टालना, साफ़ कहा, हिस्सा. Past + next time. Not a therapy session; a speaking task. Guided. Pair: you are the difficult one, then you are the one who asks. Free. Mediation: three people, five minutes, one spoken agreement. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Rehearse one clean ask you still owe someone, in Hindi, not sent yet.

Slips. ही इज़ सो टॉक्सिक → वह काम टालते हैं और मीटिंग में नहीं आते; आई डोंट वांट ड्रामा → चिल्लाने से बात नहीं बनी, इसलिए मैं लिखकर दूँगा / दूँगी

Lesson 14 · Mini project present 3m, 6m

Present a small real project in three minutes: aim, what you did, what broke, what’s next.

Say this: यह प्रोजेक्ट मैंने दोस्तों के साथ किया। — I did this project with friends. · मकसद यह था कि फॉर्म आसान हो। — The aim was to make the form easier. · बीच में सर्वर गिर गया। — Midway the server went down. · अब हम टेस्ट और कर रहे हैं। — Now we are testing more.

Warm-up. Paper: four boxes. No slide deck required. Phone photo of work is enough. Teach. मकसद, बीच में, अब. Gender of प्रोजेक्ट often यह. Kill ‘today I am going to be talking about’. Guided. Three minutes, then two questions. Timer visible. Free. Second round: same project to a non-tech partner. If they glaze over, simplify live. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Cut the talk to eight Hindi sentences and record it once.

Slips. टूडे आई विल बी टॉकिंग → यह प्रोजेक्ट मैंने ___ के साथ किया; द सर्वर क्रैश्ड → बीच में सर्वर गिर गया

Lesson 15 · Small talk, cricket ke alawa 3m, 6m

Do small talk that is not only cricket, weather, or ‘kaha se ho’.

Say this: यह कैंटीन की चाय ठीक है आज। — The canteen tea is all right today. · तुम्हारी इंटर्नशिप किस इलाके में है? — Which area is your internship in? · मैंने वह शो अभी शुरू किया। — I just started that show. · चलो, मुझे लैब जाना है। — I should go; I have lab.

Warm-up. Walk-and-talk two laps of the room. First lap they may use cricket. Second lap they may not. Teach. Exit line is a skill: चलो, मुझे ___ जाना है. Hinglish show, series, internship stay. Guided. Stranger at a fest stall. 40 seconds, then a clean exit. Free. Lift with a manager: 20 seconds, no life story, no silence death. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Start one real small-talk turn this week that is not cricket. Report what you said.

Slips. व्हेयर आर यू फ्रॉम on loop → तुम किस इलाके में रहते / रहती हो?; ओके बाय suddenly → चलो, मुझे लैब जाना है, बाद में मिलते हैं

Lesson 16 · Do internship compare 3m, 6m

Compare two internships or two summers: money, learning, people, city — a choice you can say.

Say this: पहली इंटर्नशिप में पैसा था, सीखने को कम। — The first internship had money, little learning. · दूसरी में मैं अकेला / अकेली था / थी, लेकिन हाथ लगा। — In the second I was alone, but I put my hands on the work. · शहर महँगा था। — The city was expensive. · अगर फिर चुनूँ, तो दूसरी। — If I chose again, the second.

Warm-up. If they have only one internship, compare internship vs campus project vs home summer. Teach. में vs में, लेकिन, अगर फिर. No Excel-sheet recitation. Guided. Partner must guess which one you’d repeat. Then you confirm. Free. Panel of three: hire the speaker for next summer based only on the comparison. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: two columns, spoken, 70 seconds, end with the choice.

Slips. फर्स्ट वन वाज़ बेटर → पहली में पैसा था, सीखने को कम; आई ग्रो मोर → दूसरी में हाथ ज्यादा लगा

Lesson 17 · Ek failure jo bol sakun 3m, 6m

Tell a failure you can actually say: what broke, what you did after, no fake-humble twist.

Say this: मेरा इंटरनल खराब गया। — My internal went badly. · मैंने रात भर नहीं पढ़ा, यह सच है। — I didn’t study all night; that’s true. · अगले में मैं नोट्स रोज़ करता / करती थी। — For the next one I made notes daily. · छिपाने से कुछ नहीं होता। — Hiding it does nothing.

Warm-up. Teacher models a small failure, not a tragedy. Laugh once, then work. Teach. गया / गई, नहीं पढ़ा, अगले में. Ban ‘my weakness is I care too much’ as a failure story. Guided. Pairs: 80 seconds. Listener only asks फिर तुमने क्या किया? Free. Two volunteers. Room asks one kind follow-up. Protect the speaker if anyone mocks. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Record the failure once. If you cannot listen to it, shorten it and record again.

Slips. आई फेल्ड बट आई लर्न्ड अ लॉट empty → इंटरनल खराब गया, फिर मैं रोज़ नोट्स करने लगा / लगी; इट वाज़ नॉट माई फॉल्ट only → मेरी भी गलती थी: मैंने पूछा नहीं

Lesson 18 · Mid test 6w, 3m, 6m

Do the mid check: intro, one proof story, one opinion, a four-person GD.

Say this: Intro 60s. Story 60s. Opinion 40s. GD 4 minutes.

Warm-up. Names from a hat. Water. Timer on the table. Teach. No new language. Teacher scores keep-going and interaction, not shuddh endings. Guided. While one group tests, others whisper-prep keywords only. Free. Private glow/grow. Public clap for anyone who entered the GD once. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Re-record only the weakest of the three solos.

Slips. Freezing till the teacher fills silence → तीन सेकंड, फिर कोई हिंदी वाक्य; STAR chant → कब, मैंने क्या किया, फिर क्या हुआ

Lesson 19 · Lecture summarise 3m, 6m

Summarise a lecture or a long meeting in five spoken sentences someone else could use.

Say this: आज की क्लास की बात यह थी। — Today’s class was about this. · तीन पॉइंट थे। — There were three points. · मुझे एक चीज़ अटक गई। — One thing stuck for me. · अगला काम यह है। — The next task is this.

Warm-up. Teacher gives a two-minute messy talk on a city topic. Books closed. Teach. की बात यह थी / पॉइंट / अटक गई / अगला काम. Notes as arrows, not paragraphs. Guided. Pairs compare summaries. Add what you missed; don’t copy English headings. Free. One person briefs the room in 50 seconds. Class names one missing piece. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. After a real class this week, send a five-sentence Hindi summary to the group.

Slips. सो द लेक्चर वाज़ लाइक → आज की क्लास की बात यह थी कि...; नथिंग नथिंग आई गॉट इट → मुझे एक चीज़ अटक गई: ...

Lesson 20 · Culture, sheher 3m, 6m

Talk the city and the culture you actually live: language mix, rent, festival, who gets space.

Say this: इस शहर में हिंदी-अंग्रेज़ी दोनों चलती हैं। — In this city both Hindi and English run. · किराया कमरे से ज़्यादा बात है। — Rent is more than a room; it’s the whole talk. · त्योहार पर घर जाना पड़ता है, कभी नहीं भी। — For festivals you have to go home — sometimes you don’t. · मुझे वह गली पसंद है जहाँ शाम को भीड़ होती है। — I like the street that’s crowded in the evening.

Warm-up. One photo each: a street they walk. If no photo, describe from memory. Teach. चलती हैं, पड़ता है, भीड़. Allow critique: who is not safe, who is priced out. Keep it speakable. Guided. Partner city vs home town, two minutes, no tourism-board voice. Free. Hot-seat: ‘Yeh sheher tumhe rakhega?’ Answer with proof. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: this city in six sentences, including one thing that bothers you.

Slips. द सिटी इज़ वेरी वाइब्रंट → शाम को उस गली में भीड़ होती है, चाय सस्ती है; कल्चर इज़ रिच → यहाँ त्योहार पर दुकानें देर तक खुलती हैं

Lesson 21 · Ethics, ikkis saal 6m

Speak an ethics call at 21: copy, ghost intern, unpaid labour, a lie on a CV — your line.

Say this: कॉपी करके नंबर लाना मैं नहीं करूँगा / करूँगी। — I won’t take marks by copying. · किसी का काम अपने नाम पर नहीं चलता। — Someone’s work doesn’t run in your name. · अगर सीवी पर झूठ लिखा तो मैं फँसूँगा / फँसूँगी। — If I lie on the CV, I’ll get caught. · ना कहना भी नैतिकता है। — Saying no is also ethics.

Warm-up. Four dirty cases on slips. They draw one. No sermon from the teacher first. Teach. नहीं करूँगा, अपने नाम पर, फँसना, ना कहना. Disagreement is welcome; silence is not the goal. Guided. Pairs argue both sides of unpaid overtime ‘for exposure’. Free. Tiny tribunal: three judges, one speaker, two-minute verdict in Hindi. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Write no essay. Record your line on one case you might actually meet.

Slips. एवरीवन डज़ इट → बहुत लोग करते हैं, मैं नहीं करूँगा / करूँगी; इट्स नॉट अ बिग डील → सीवी पर झूठ बाद में फँसाता है

Lesson 22 · Freeze-maro workshop 6w, 3m, 6m

Kill the freeze with timed, messy, repeatable talk — the course’s engine room.

Say this: तीन सेकंड बाद बोलो। — Speak after three seconds. · अटको तो वही वाक्य दोहराओ। — If you stick, repeat the same sentence. · नया शब्द मत ढूँढो। — Don’t hunt a new word. · खत्म करो, फिर ठीक करो। — Finish, then fix.

Warm-up. Timer: 20–20–20 on canteen, commute, last failure. Teacher does not rescue. Teach. Workshop rules on the wall. Correction only in the last eight minutes, and only target frames. Guided. Stations: aunt-explain, meeting line, GD entry, proof story. Four minutes each. Free. Open mic: two minutes, any campus topic. Audience asks one hard question. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. First-take voice note on a new topic. Do not re-record. Send it.

Slips. Stopping to design the perfect sentence → अधूरा वाक्य, फिर अगला; Sorry sorry I forgot → वही बात दोबारा, धीरे

Lesson 23 · Showcase — teen minute 6w, 3m, 6m

Rehearse a three-minute talk: who you are, one proof, one opinion, close.

Say this: Who, proof story, opinion, one line on what’s next.

Warm-up. Teacher model that includes one stumble and a recovery. Teach. Keyword card, six words max. Standing. Eyes on a face, not on the fan. Guided. Pairs + two questions. Then swap partners and cut thirty seconds. Free. Dress rehearsal for half the room. Note freeze points, not every gender ending. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Full talk at home, standing, once to a person if you can.

Slips. Reading a paragraph → Six words, then talk; Corporate cartoon close → आगे मैं यह आज़माऊँगा / आज़माऊँगी

Lesson 24 · Finale — sawaal ke saath 6w, 3m, 6m

Give the showcase and survive one hard question; then place the next course.

Say this: Three-minute talk plus one hard question: failure, money, or aunt-explain.

Warm-up. Quiet waiting. Timer visible. Hard questions on slips in a cup. Teach. Score keep-going, proof, and the answer after the freeze — not accent. Guided. Talk, then the hard question. Ten seconds of thought is allowed; English drafting is not. Free. Circle: मैं अटक सकता / सकती हूँ, फिर बोल सकता / सकती हूँ. Point to Interview if placements are near. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. No homework. If a Hindi or mixed panel is coming, open Interview next.

Slips. Rescuing them with English → दोबारा पूछो, धीरे, हिंदी में; Marking shuddh Hindi → Mark a finished thought

Urban Spoken Hindi · Young adults · Interview aur pehli naukri

Young adults · Interview aur pehli naukri

Young adults 18–25 Interview speaking 20 lessons

Apne baare mein, proof stories, why this role, GD, and mocks. Crash option included.

Who: Final-year students and first-job hunters who can already hold a conversation and now face Hindi or mixed-language interviews.

Done when: They can do a 12-minute mock with intro, proof, one story, and two questions for the panel.

Place here when: Not for true beginners. Use after Fluency, or if they already speak at Intermediate.

Teacher note: Crash = lessons tagged crash, daily if possible. Never guarantee a job. Guarantee clearer speech. Formal aap stays on in the interview room.

Tests

StartCurrent intro, timed. Usually too long or too empty.
MidFull mock 1.
EndHarder mock 2 plus public intro.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Panel kya sunta hai 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Hear what a campus or first-job panel actually listens for: sense, proof, and a stop — not a speech.

Say this: पैनल कहानी नहीं, सबूत सुनता है। — The panel hears proof, not a saga. · लंबी बात से पहले एक वाक्य। — One sentence before the long talk. · नहीं पता, यह भी जवाब है। — ‘I don’t know’ is also an answer. · हिंदी माँगी हो तो हिंदी में रहो। — If they asked for Hindi, stay in Hindi.

Warm-up. Teacher plays two intros: one padded English, one short Hindi. Class says which they would hire. Teach. Board: शुरू, सबूत, रुको. Formal aap from this lesson to the finale. Hinglish canteen habits stay outside the mock room. Guided. Pairs rewrite a 90-second English dump into five Hindi sentences. Free. Hot-seat 40 seconds. Room votes: clear / empty / too long. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Record your current intro. Time it. Mark where the panel would stop listening.

Slips. सो बेसिकली आई एम अ पर्सन हू → मैं सिविल में हूँ, लैब का काम किया है; आई डोंट नो सर sorry freeze → पक्का नहीं सर, मैं यह चेक करके बताऊँगा / बताऊँगी

Lesson 02 · Apne baare mein 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Give a 60–90 second apne-baare-mein that a Hindi or mixed panel can use.

Say this: नमस्ते सर / मैम। मेरा नाम फातिमा है। — Namaste sir/ma’am. My name is Fatima. · मैं फाइनल ईयर में हूँ, इलेक्ट्रॉनिक्स। — I’m in final year, electronics. · इस साल मैंने एक प्रोजेक्ट और एक इंटर्नशिप की। — This year I did one project and one internship. · आज मैं इस रोल के बारे में बात करना चाहती हूँ / चाहता हूँ। — Today I want to talk about this role.

Warm-up. Listen to yesterday’s recordings. Cut the first 15 seconds of fog. Teach. Order: नाम, अब क्या, एक सबूत, क्यों यह कमरा. Gender: चाहता / चाहती. No childhood paragraph unless they ask. Guided. Pairs time 75 seconds. Listener raises a hand at ‘enough’. Free. Two public intros. Repair only aap ho and empty adjectives. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Re-record 80 seconds standing. First take if it is complete.

Slips. फ्रॉम चाइल्डहुड आई हैव ड्रीम्ड → फाइनल ईयर में हूँ, यह प्रोजेक्ट किया है; आई एम अ वेरी पैशनेट पर्सन → मैं समय पर काम खत्म करती / करता हूँ — एक उदाहरण है

Lesson 03 · Resume Hindi mein 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Walk a resume in Hindi: three lines the panel can interrupt.

Say this: ऊपर की लाइन: मैंने यह किया। — Top line: I did this. · इंटर्नशिप में मेरा काम ___ था। — In the internship my work was ___. · प्रोजेक्ट में मैंने ___ बनाया / बनाई। — In the project I built ___. · यह स्किल इसलिए लिखी है क्योंकि ___। — This skill is written because ___.

Warm-up. Paper resume or phone PDF. Teacher points at a line; they must speak it, not read the English. Teach. किया / की, बनाया / बनाई. If a line cannot be said in Hindi, it is not theirs yet — rewrite the speech, not necessarily the CV. Guided. Interrupt drill: panel says ‘rukie, yeh project?’ after 12 seconds. Free. Whole-class: three students, three lines each, then one follow-up. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Pick three resume lines. Say each in one Hindi sentence on a voice note.

Slips. आई वज़ रिस्पॉन्सिबल फॉर → मेरा काम यह था कि...; वेरियस एक्टिविटीज़ → फेस्ट में गेट संभाला, टीम के पाँच लोग थे

Lesson 04 · Yeh role kyun 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Say why this role without a brochure or a lie about ‘your esteemed organisation’.

Say this: यह रोल इसलिए चाहिए क्योंकि काम हाथ वाला है। — I want this role because the work is hands-on. · आपकी टीम ___ करती है, वह मुझे पता है। — Your team does ___; I know that. · मैं अभी सीख रहा हूँ / रही हूँ, लेकिन यह हिस्सा मैं कर चुका / चुकी हूँ। — I’m still learning, but I’ve already done this part. · सिर्फ नाम के लिए नहीं आ रहा / रही। — I’m not here just for the name.

Warm-up. Board a real JD in six words. Cross out ‘passionate about your vision’. Teach. क्योंकि + एक सच. Teach ‘मुझे पता है’ only when they did look. Honest ‘abhi seekh raha hoon’ beats fake expertise. Guided. Pairs: one is a role they want, one is a role they would refuse. Both get a Hindi why. Free. Panel: ‘Hum campus se kyun lein?’ Answer with proof, not hunger. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Write five Hindi keywords for this role. Speak 50 seconds. No English first draft.

Slips. आई वांट टु ग्रो विद योर कंपनी → यह रोल इसलिए चाहिए क्योंकि लैब वाला काम मुझे आता है; योर ऑर्गनाइज़ेशन इज़ प्रेस्टिजस → आपकी टीम यह प्रोडक्ट बनाती है, मैंने इस्तेमाल किया है

Lesson 05 · Strengths, proof ke saath 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Name two strengths only if each has a proof story you can finish.

Say this: मेरी एक ताकत यह है कि मैं काम खत्म करता हूँ / करती हूँ। — One strength: I finish the work. · उदाहरण: फेस्ट से एक रात पहले स्टॉल खड़ा किया। — Example: we put the stall up the night before the fest. · दूसरी ताकत: साफ़ पूछ लेता हूँ / लेती हूँ। — Second: I ask clearly. · खाली शब्द — मेहनती, ईमानदार — बिना कहानी नहीं। — Empty words — hardworking, honest — not without a story.

Warm-up. Ban list on the board: dedicated, team player, go-getter, मेहनती with no story. Teach. ताकत + उदाहरण. Two strengths max in a first interview. Guided. Partner may only say ‘proof?’ until a concrete noun appears. Free. Hot-seat: strength, 40-second proof, stop. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Two strengths, two proofs, keyword cards only. Record both.

Slips. आई एम अ टीम प्लेयर → ग्रुप में काम रुक गया था, मैंने बाँट दिया; माई स्ट्रेंथ इज़ कम्युनिकेशन → क्लाइंट को फोन पर साफ़ बताया, ऑर्डर बदला

Lesson 06 · Weakness, nakli nahi 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Give a real weakness and the repair, not a fake-humble strength in a wig.

Say this: एक कमज़ोरी: मैं बहुत बात सोचता हूँ / सोचती हूँ, बोलने में देर होती है। — One weakness: I think too long; I speak late. · पहले मैं डेडलाइन के दिन शुरू करता / करती था / थी। — I used to start on the deadline day. · अब मैं कल का काम आज लिख लेता हूँ / लेती हूँ। — Now I write tomorrow’s work today. · ‘बहुत पर्सफेक्शनिस्ट हूँ’ — यह जवाब यहाँ नहीं चलेगा। — ‘I’m too much of a perfectionist’ will not run here.

Warm-up. Teacher offers three fake weaknesses. Class kills them. Teach. कमज़ोरी + पहले + अब. Must be something they would actually fix. Gender on सोचता / सोचती, करता / करती. Guided. Pairs: if the listener could have guessed the ‘weakness’ as a strength, rewrite. Free. Panel: ‘Yeh weakness kaam ko kab kharab karti hai?’ They must give a scene. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: weakness, one scene, one repair. If it sounds cute, record again.

Slips. आई वर्क टू हार्ड → मैं ना नहीं कह पाता / पाती, फिर काम टूटता है; नो वीकनेस सर → एक कमज़ोरी है: ... और यह मैं बदल रहा / रही हूँ

Lesson 07 · Story bank 3m, 6m, crash

Build a bank of four speakable stories: team, fight, fail, a thing you made.

Say this: टीम वाली: काम बाँटा। — Team: we split the work. · लड़ाई वाली: मैंने साफ़ कहा। — Conflict: I said it clearly. · फेल वाली: इंटरनल / डिलीवरी गई। — Fail: the internal or the delivery went. · बनाई वाली: मैंने ___ बनाया। — Made: I built ___.

Warm-up. Four boxes on one page. Titles only, then one Hindi sentence each. Teach. Not a corporate cartoon. Stories may be fest, mess, lab, café shift, family shop. Tag them so they can be reached in five seconds. Guided. Speed: teacher calls ‘fail’ / ‘team’ / ‘made’. Twenty-five seconds. Sit. Free. Partner steals a story that is too thin and asks three क्यों. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Sleep with four titles under the pillow — say them once in the morning, no script.

Slips. जेनेरिक टीम वर्क स्पीच → फेस्ट से पहले स्टॉल अधूरा था, मैंने कील और टेप मँगवाई; आई ओवरकेम चैलेंजेज़ → सर्वर गिरा, हमने फोन से ऑर्डर लिया

Lesson 08 · Behavioural sawaal 3m, 6m, crash

Answer behavioural asks with one story from the bank, then stop for the next question.

Say this: जब टीम में टेंशन हुई... — When the team got tense... · मैंने यह किया। — I did this. · नतीजा यह रहा। — The result was this. · अगर फिर हो, तो पहले लिखकर बाँटूँगा / बाँटूँगी। — If it happens again, I’ll split it in writing first.

Warm-up. Question cards: deadline, conflict, mistake, someone lazy, you were wrong. Teach. Shape: जब / मैंने / नतीजा / फिर. Stop. Do not start a second story unless they ask. Guided. Interrupt after the first sentence: ‘example?’ They must land on a noun. Free. Two rounds: same question, tighter. Formal aap to the panel. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Pick two behavioural cards. One voice note each, under 70 seconds.

Slips. वी वर्क्ड अज़ अ टीम empty → जब स्लाइड्स नहीं बनीं, मैंने रात को बाँट दिया; आई एम अ लीडर → मैंने मीटिंग बुलाई, समय लिखा

Lesson 09 · HR classics 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Handle HR classics in Hindi: five-year, relocate, night shift, why should we take you.

Say this: पाँच साल बाद मैं यह काम अच्छी तरह करता / करती दिखूँ। — In five years I want to be seen doing this work well. · दूसरे शहर जा सकता हूँ / सकती हूँ, परिवार से बात करके। — I can go to another city after talking to family. · नाइट शिफ्ट एक सीमा तक चल जाएगी। — A night shift will run up to a point. · आप हमें इसलिए लें क्योंकि यह काम मैं कर चुका / चुकी हूँ। — Take me because I have already done this work.

Warm-up. Four classics on the board. No coaching them to lie about mobility. Teach. सकता / सकती, बात करके. ‘I don’t know my life at 28’ is allowed if it stays adult, not cute. Guided. Speed dating: 40 seconds per classic, rotate. Free. Nasty version: ‘Aapki family allow karegi?’ Answer with dignity, aap, no apology for being a woman or a first-gen student. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Record relocate and why-you, each 35 seconds. Listen for fake yes.

Slips. आई सी माइसेल्फ अज़ अ मैनेजर → पाँच साल बाद मैं यह काम खुद संभालता / संभालती दिखूँ; यस यस एनीव्हेयर → दूसरे शहर जा सकता / सकती हूँ, पहले घर से बात करूँगा / करूँगी

Lesson 10 · Interview GD 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Do a placement-style GD: enter early, stay human, close without eating the room.

Say this: मैं शुरुआत करूँ? विषय यह लगता है कि ___। — Shall I start? The topic seems to be ___. · एक आंकड़ा नहीं, एक उदाहरण है। — Not a statistic — an example. · जो अभी तक नहीं बोले, आएँ। — Those who haven’t spoken yet, come in. · निष्कर्ष: हम तीन बात मान सकते हैं। — Close: we can take three points.

Warm-up. Topic they might actually get: intern stipend, AI and jobs, city waste, campus politics. Teach. If the GD is marked in Hindi, stay in Hindi. One English noun is fine. Formal aap to the group if it is a judged room. Guided. Six minutes. Teacher notes first entry, invite-in, and who closed. Free. Second GD: silent student must open; last time’s closer may only invite. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise the open line and the invite-in line. Do not memorise a conclusion paragraph.

Slips. फ्रेंड्स टुडे वी आर गोइंग टु डिस्कस → विषय यह लगता है कि...; अज़ आई हैव ऑलरेडी सेड loop → एक नई बात: ...

Lesson 11 · Experience nahi to 3m, 6m, crash

Sit a no-experience case: project, fest, shop, care work, a course — still proof.

Say this: नौकरी नहीं की, यह सच है। — I haven’t done a job; that’s true. · प्रोजेक्ट में मैंने ___ किया। — In the project I did ___. · घर / दुकान / फेस्ट पर भी काम किया है। — I’ve also worked at home, a shop, or a fest. · सीखने की जगह चाहिए, कहानी बनाने की नहीं। — I need a place to learn, not a place to invent a story.

Warm-up. Shame phrases on the board get crossed: ‘mere paas kuchh nahi hai’, ‘sirf college’. Teach. नहीं की, लेकिन किया. Care work and family shop count if they can say what they did. Guided. Pairs turn ‘no experience’ into three proof nouns in two minutes. Free. Panel: ‘Aapke resume pe intern nahi.’ They answer without shrinking and without lying. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. List four things you have actually done. Say them in Hindi as if the panel just asked.

Slips. आई हैव नो एक्सपीरियंस सर → नौकरी नहीं की, प्रोजेक्ट और फेस्ट का काम किया है; जस्ट अ स्टूडेंट → मैं स्टूडेंट हूँ, और यह काम कर चुका / चुकी हूँ

Lesson 12 · Unse sawaal 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Ask the panel two questions that show you thought — not ‘no questions sir’.

Say this: टीम में नया आदमी पहले महीना क्या करता है? — What does a new person do in the first month? · सफलता यहाँ कैसे मापते हैं? — How do you measure success here? · यह रोल किस टीम के साथ बैठता है? — Which team does this role sit with? · मेरा एक सवाल और है। — I have one more question.

Warm-up. Kill list: salary first in a first-round Hindi oral (unless they opened it), and ‘no ma’am’. Teach. aap throughout. Questions about work, not flattery. One backup question if they already answered the first. Guided. Pairs: ask, hear a vague answer, ask a sharper second. Free. End of a fake interview: two questions, 40 seconds total, then thank-you and stand. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Write two questions for a real company or lab. Speak them; don’t read.

Slips. नो क्वेश्चंस सर → हाँ, एक सवाल: पहले महीना क्या काम मिलेगा?; व्हाट इज़ द वर्क लाइक empty → रोज़ का काम क्या रहेगा, और कौन रिव्यू करेगा?

Lesson 13 · Phone aur video 3m, 6m, crash

Handle a phone or video interview: sound, pause, ‘line gayi’, looking at the lens.

Say this: नमस्ते, मेरी आवाज़ आ रही है? — Namaste, can you hear me? · लाइन कट गई, मैं वापस आ गया / गई। — The line dropped; I’m back. · ज़रा दोबारा पूछिए, आवाज़ टूट रही थी। — Please ask again; the sound was breaking. · मैं नोट कर रहा हूँ / रही हूँ, एक सेकंड। — I’m noting that, one second.

Warm-up. Back-to-back phone, then one ‘camera’ (a phone on a bag). They see their own freeze face. Teach. कट गई / टूट रही थी. Don’t shout. Don’t say ‘hello? hello?’ five times. Formal aap. Guided. Phone intro 45 seconds. Video: same intro, then a proof, eyes on the lens not the self-view. Free. Chaos round: teacher mutes, then ‘sorry, continue’. They restart from a Hindi hinge, not from zero English. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Record a 50-second video intro. Watch once without sound, once with. Fix one thing.

Slips. हेलो हेलो हेलो → नमस्ते, मेरी आवाज़ आ रही है?; सॉरी नेटवर्क इज़ बैड only English → लाइन कट गई, मैं वापस आ गया / गई। आखिरी बात यह थी...

Lesson 14 · Body, awaaz 6m

Set body and voice for a Hindi oral: sit, hands, pace — not accent theatre.

Say this: पीठ लगाकर बैठिए। — Sit with your back on the chair. · आवाज़ कमरे तक जाए। — Let the voice reach the room. · जवाब के बाद दो सेकंड रुको। — After an answer, wait two seconds. · हँसना ठीक है, माफ़ी का शोर नहीं। — A laugh is fine; an apology-noise is not.

Warm-up. Stand, sit, walk to the chair. Class watches only the first three seconds. Teach. This is not elocution. Do not ‘fix’ home accents. Fix vanishing volume and apology-laughs. Guided. Pairs: one answers while the other only coaches volume and pause. Free. Two public answers. Teacher notes hands-in-pockets, racing, and looking at the floor. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise the intro sitting, then standing. Same words, better air.

Slips. सॉरी सॉरी सॉरी before every line → एक साँस, फिर जवाब; Mumbling at the table → आवाज़ कमरे तक जाए

Lesson 15 · Stress sawaal 3m, 6m, crash

Survive a stress question without shrinking, inventing, or attacking.

Say this: यह सवाल कठिन है, एक सेकंड सोचूँ। — That’s a hard question; I’ll think a second. · मुझे वह अनुभव नहीं, यह नज़दीकी अनुभव है। — I don’t have that experience; here is the nearest one. · अगर मैं गलत हूँ तो बताइए, मैं सुधारूँगा / सुधारूँगी। — If I’m wrong, tell me; I’ll correct it. · मैं अनुमान नहीं लगाऊँगा / लगाऊँगी। — I won’t guess.

Warm-up. Cold questions: gap, low pointer, job-hop of a parent, ‘why not IIT’, silence after they answer. Teach. सोचूँ, नज़दीकी, अनुमान नहीं. Three seconds of silence is strength. Formal aap even if the panel is rude in role-play. Guided. Pairs fire two stress Qs each. Listener may not soothe in English. Free. Hot-seat 90 seconds of pressure. Room protects the speaker after; we do not shame a freeze, we replay the hinge line. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Pick the stress Q you fear. Record the hinge + nearest-proof, not a perfect essay.

Slips. फ्रीज़ then English flood → यह सवाल कठिन है, एक सेकंड...; ब्लफ़ अ नंबर → पक्का नहीं, अनुमान नहीं लगाऊँगा / लगाऊँगी

Lesson 16 · Mock 1 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Sit a 12-minute mock: intro, resume line, one story, two questions for them.

Say this: 12 minutes: apne baare mein, one resume walk, one behavioural, your two questions.

Warm-up. Panel of two (teacher + student). Timer. Water. Formal aap from knock to thank-you. Teach. If they asked for Hindi, Hinglish only for nouns (UPI, intern, slide). Teacher notes freeze, padding, aap ho. Guided. Rest of class writes glow/grow, not grammar essays. Swap panel roles. Free. Debrief two minutes: one glow, one grow. No new teaching. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Same night: re-record only the intro and the weakest story.

Slips. आप कैसे हो to the panel → आप कैसे हैं?; Ending with no questions → हाँ, एक सवाल: पहले महीना क्या काम मिलेगा?

Lesson 17 · Repair clinic 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Repair what Mock 1 showed: padding, freeze, gender, register, empty strengths.

Say this: यह लाइन काटो। — Cut this line. · यह लाइन रखो। — Keep this line. · यहाँ सबूत डालो। — Put proof here. · यहीं रुको। — Stop here.

Warm-up. Teacher pad from Mock 1 on the board, anonymised. Teach. Clinic stations: aap/hain, gender on last story, 20-second start, kill-the-padding. No new topics. Guided. Rewrite one answer on the feet, not on a laptop. Free. Re-run the worst 90 seconds of Mock 1 for anyone who wants it. Clap for the cut, not for length. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Send a repaired 60-second clip of the worst answer. First clean take.

Slips. Adding more English to sound smart → छोटी हिंदी, एक संज्ञा, एक काम; Sorry I was nervous as the whole answer → एक सेकंड... फिर वही सबूत

Lesson 18 · Offer, wait, reject 6m

Speak the week after: offer, waitlist, reject, a hold — adult, not a film scene.

Say this: मुझे ऑफर आया है, दो दिन सोचना है। — I have an offer; I need two days to think. · अभी वेटलिस्ट पर हूँ। — I’m on the waitlist right now. · नहीं हुआ, यह भी जानकारी है। — It didn’t happen; that’s information too. · मैम, क्या डेडलाइन बढ़ा सकते हैं? — Ma’am, can you extend the deadline?

Warm-up. Three slips: offer, reject, silence. They speak the first call home in Hindi. Teach. आया है, सोचना है, नहीं हुआ. No guarantee talk. Formal aap with HR on the phone. Guided. Pairs: two offers, different cities. Think aloud. Then the HR call. Free. Friend got the job you wanted. Congrats plus one true line, no poison. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Rehearse one HR line and one family line. Do not send until you mean them.

Slips. आई एम गॉस्टेड → अभी जवाब नहीं आया, मैं फॉलो-अप करूँगा / करूँगी; आई विल जॉइन फॉर शोर without thinking → दो दिन सोचना है, घर से बात करके बताता / बताती हूँ

Lesson 19 · Mock 2 6m

Sit a harder 15-minute mock: stress Q, no-experience push, and a Hindi-only stretch.

Say this: 15 minutes: intro, why this role, fail story, stress Q, two questions, close.

Warm-up. New panel face if you can. Harder JD. If the course promised Hindi, no English rescue from the teacher. Teach. Push on empty adjectives. Push on aap/tum. Allow ‘nahi pata’ as a full move. Guided. Two mocks run in parallel if the group is large. Listeners score keep-going and proof. Free. Short debrief. Save energy for the public finale. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Repair one answer only. Sleep. Do not rewrite the whole personality.

Slips. Switching to English when stressed → हिंदी में रुको: यह सवाल कठिन है, एक सेकंड; Longer than Mock 1 with more fog → छोटा, सबूत, रुको

Lesson 20 · Finale — public intro 6w, 3m, 6m, crash

Give a public intro and close the course: clearer speech, not a promised job.

Say this: Public 90-second intro plus one proof. Then a thank-you to the panel that is not a speech.

Warm-up. Chairs as a corridor outside the room. Walk in, sit, speak, thank, leave. Teach. Score the walk-in, the stop, and the two questions they prepared — never ‘you will get placed’. Guided. If time: 60-second stress Q for volunteers. Protect anyone who shakes. Free. Circle: मैं पैनल के सामने हिंदी में रुक सकता / सकती हूँ और फिर बोल सकता / सकती हूँ. No certificates that lie about jobs. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. No homework. Send the public intro to yourself in a year. Go to work or back to Fluency if the freeze returned.

Slips. Guaranteeing a placement → Guarantee clearer speech only; Correcting accent on the last day → One glow, one grow, then stop

Urban Spoken Hindi · Adults · Zero se, izzat ke saath

Adults · Zero se, izzat ke saath

Adults 26–50 · also Seniors 50+ Absolute Beginner 30 lessons

Naam, dukaan, doctor, phone, building, school gate. Seniors use the same book, slower.

Who: Urban adults who have little or no spoken Hindi. Homemakers, workers, and seniors are all welcome.

Done when: They can greet, shop, ask for help, tell a simple day, and speak for one minute about their life.

Place here when: Place here if they need English or another language for almost every instruction.

Teacher note: If you are teaching seniors (50+), use this same course at a slower pace. Skip job-interview energy. Keep everyday city life. Repeat more. Sit more. Praise effort every time. Never rush a person who is thinking.

Tests

StartName and hello only. Smile.
MidHome, shop, help, Sunday.
EndShowcase plus shop or help.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Zero se, izzat ke saath 6w, 3m, 6m

Greet an adult stranger with namaste and aap, say your name, and say you are learning Hindi — no baby talk.

Say this: नमस्ते। मेरा नाम ___ है। — Namaste. My name is ___. · आपसे मिलकर अच्छा लगा। — Glad to meet you. · मैं हिंदी सीख रहा हूँ / सीख रही हूँ। — I am learning Hindi.

Warm-up. Chairs stay put. Teacher walks to each person, namaste, aap. No songs, no clapping games. Seniors remain seated. Teach. Board: मेरा नाम, never मैं नाम. Aap with the teacher and anyone new. Learning as an adult is ordinary, not cute. Guided. Pairs: namaste, name, milkar accha laga. Swap seats only if bodies allow; otherwise turn chairs. Free. Three introductions around the room. Sit between turns. Praise the attempt, not the accent. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: नमस्ते। मेरा नाम ___ है। मैं हिंदी सीख रहा/रही हूँ।

Slips. मैं नाम रीना → मेरा नाम रीना है; आपका नाम क्या हो? → आपका नाम क्या है?; मैं हिंदी सीखता है → मैं हिंदी सीख रहा/रही हूँ

Lesson 02 · Aap kaise hain — asli jawab 6w, 3m, 6m

Ask how someone is and answer with a real state, not only theek hoon.

Say this: आप कैसे हैं? / आप कैसी हैं? — How are you? (aap) · मैं ठीक हूँ। — I am okay. · आज थोड़ा थक गया हूँ / थक गई हूँ। — I am a bit tired today. · दिन अच्छा चल रहा है। — The day is going well.

Warm-up. Three faces on the board: theek, thaka, accha din. Adults point; no acting like children. Teach. Aap + हैं/हैं. Gender: थका / थकी, कैसी for a woman you address with aap. Tum only for a same-age classmate who agrees. Guided. Two lines: ‘building guard’ gets aap; ‘class partner’ may get tum. Real answers, one extra word. Free. Door chat: enter, namaste, aap kaise hain, sit, one true sentence. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Ask one adult at home आप कैसे हैं? Report their real answer, not only ठीक.

Slips. आप कैसे हो? (to teacher) → आप कैसे हैं?; मैं ठीक है → मैं ठीक हूँ; मैं टायर्ड → मैं थक गया/गई हूँ

Lesson 03 · Sheher ki ginti 3m, 6m

Use numbers for an auto fare, a kilo, and a floor.

Say this: ऑटो अस्सी का होगा। — The auto will be about eighty. · दो किलो टमाटर दीजिए। — Two kilos of tomatoes, please. · हम पाँचवीं मंज़िल पर रहते हैं। — We live on the fifth floor.

Warm-up. Sit and count 1–20 together, slowly. Repeat. Seniors set the pace. Teach. City trio: रुपये, किलो, मंज़िल / फ्लोर. Both मंज़िल and फ्लोर are fine in a city. Guided. Pair cards: fare, weight, floor. Say the full sentence, not the number alone. Free. Mini city: one is driver, one is kirana, one is a neighbour asking the floor. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Say one real number from your building or last auto ride.

Slips. ऑटो एटी रुपये → ऑटो अस्सी रुपये का होगा; टू किलो टमाटर → दो किलो टमाटर दीजिए; फिफ्थ फ्लोर रहते → हम पाँचवीं मंज़िल पर रहते हैं

Lesson 04 · Ghar mein kaun rehta hai 6w, 3m, 6m

Name the people you actually live with, including living alone.

Say this: मैं पति / पत्नी के साथ रहती हूँ / रहता हूँ। — I live with my spouse. · माँ-बाप साथ रहते हैं। — My parents live with us. · मैं अकेला रहता हूँ / अकेली रहती हूँ। — I live alone. · यह मेरे बेटे हैं / मेरी बेटी है। — This is my son / daughter.

Warm-up. Optional family photo, face down until they choose to show it. No one is forced. Teach. हैं for elders and for respect. Living alone is a full, dignified sentence. Homemakers name the household they run. Guided. Partner: three sentences. Listener only asks एक और बात? Free. Gallery of spoken homes — no drawings required. Seniors stay seated and speak. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: घर में कौन रहता है, three lines. No shame words.

Slips. मैं अकेला है → मैं अकेला रहता हूँ / अकेली रहती हूँ; मेरी मम्मी है (elder) → मेरी माँ हैं; मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ → मैं घर सँभालती/सँभालता हूँ — if that is the work

Lesson 05 · Sheher ka ghar 3m, 6m

Describe a flat, floor, lift or stairs, and one room you use most.

Say this: मैं सोसाइटी के फ्लैट में रहती हूँ / रहता हूँ। — I live in a society flat. · लिफ्ट है, कभी सीढ़ी भी चढ़ते हैं। — There is a lift; sometimes we take the stairs. · किचन मेरी सबसे ज़रूरी जगह है। — The kitchen is my most needed place.

Warm-up. Name the area of the city only if people want. No map quiz. Teach. फ्लैट, मकान, सोसाइटी, मंज़िल, बालकनी. Kitchen as serious workspace, not a joke. Guided. Partner tour: gate → lift → door → one room. Free. A relative is visiting from out of town. Explain the house in one minute, sitting. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Walk or sit at home and name three rooms in Hindi.

Slips. मैं फ्लैट रहता → मैं फ्लैट में रहता/रहती हूँ; किचन है इंपॉर्टेंट → किचन मेरी ज़रूरी जगह है; हम थर्ड फ्लोर → हम तीसरी मंज़िल पर रहते हैं

Lesson 06 · Subah, dopahar, shaam 3m, 6m

Name parts of the day and clock time you actually use.

Say this: सुबह सात बजे मैं उठती हूँ / उठता हूँ। — I get up at seven in the morning. · दोपहर में खाना होता है। — Lunch is in the afternoon. · शाम को बाज़ार जाती हूँ / जाता हूँ। — I go to the market in the evening. · कितने बजे हैं? — What time is it?

Warm-up. A simple clock or phone clock. Count hours once, slowly. Teach. सुबह / दोपहर / शाम / रात + बजे. साढ़े only if the room is ready. Guided. Fill a spoken day strip: four times, four actions. Free. Wrong-time comedy: partner says the time; you correct kindly. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tomorrow morning: आज सुबह ___ बजे उठूँगा/उठूँगी।

Slips. मैं उठता है सात → मैं सुबह सात बजे उठता/उठती हूँ; आठ बजा है → आठ बजे हैं; ईवनिंग मैं मार्केट → शाम को मैं बाज़ार जाता/जाती हूँ

Lesson 07 · Mera aam din 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell a simple real day: home, work, errands — homemaking counts as the day.

Say this: सुबह चाय बनाती हूँ / बनाता हूँ। — I make tea in the morning. · फिर दफ्तर / घर का काम होता है। — Then there is office / housework. · रात को दस बजे सोती हूँ / सोता हूँ। — I sleep at ten at night.

Warm-up. Four board words only: उठना, काम, खाना, सोना. Adults supply their own facts. Teach. फिर / उसके बाद. Gender on उठती / उठता. Never upgrade ‘ghar ka kaam’ into a joke. Guided. Partner day in 45 seconds. Listener writes nothing; only listens. Free. Class chain: each person adds one true step. Sit between turns. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: your real day, four Hindi sentences.

Slips. मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ → सुबह घर का काम करती/करता हूँ, फिर...; मैं उठता है → मैं उठता/उठती हूँ; फिर मैं ऑफिस → फिर मैं दफ्तर जाता/जाती हूँ

Lesson 08 · Khana: banana ya order 6w, 3m, 6m

Talk about food you cook or order, with likes and a polite order.

Say this: मैं रोज़ दाल-चावल बनाती हूँ / बनाता हूँ। — I cook dal-rice most days. · आज ऑर्डर कर लिया। — I ordered today. · मुझे कम तेल चाहिए। — I want less oil. · एक प्लेट चाट देना। — One plate of chaat, please.

Warm-up. No tasting games. Name last night’s food, sitting. Teach. मुझे पसंद है, not मैं पसंद. Cooking is skill. Ordering is city life. Both are adult. Guided. Pair: cook story or delivery call. Swap. Free. Stall or kitchen: order, change one thing (कम मिर्च / बिना प्याज़). Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. At one meal, say two Hindi food sentences aloud.

Slips. मैं इडली पसंद है → मुझे इडली पसंद है; मैं भूख हूँ → मुझे भूख लगी है; एक चाट दे → एक प्लेट चाट दीजिए

Lesson 09 · Kirana par aap 6w, 3m, 6m

Buy two things at a kirana with aap, price, and thanks.

Say this: भैया, यह कितने का है? — Bhaiya, how much is this? · आधा किलो चीनी दीजिए। — Half a kilo of sugar, please. · थोड़ा कम हो जाएगा? — Can it be a little less? · शुक्रिया। पैसे लीजिए। — Thanks. Please take the money.

Warm-up. Desk kirana: packets, a notebook ‘khata’. Adults stand only if they wish. Teach. कितने का / कितनी की. Shopkeeper gets aap even if you say भैया. No तुम to a stranger behind a counter. Guided. Pair shop. Then swap roles. Seniors may keep the same role and repeat. Free. Whole-class shop: each person buys two items and hears the total. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. At a real kirana this week: यह कितने का है?

Slips. यह कितना है पैकेट → यह पैकेट कितने का है?; कम करो तुम → थोड़ा कम हो जाएगा?; थैंक यू ओनली → शुक्रिया

Lesson 10 · Madad chahiye 6w, 3m, 6m

Ask for help from a guard, neighbour, or stranger without shrinking.

Say this: ज़रा मदद कीजिए। — Please help me a little. · यह बैग भारी है। — This bag is heavy. · लिफ्ट कौन से फ्लोर पे जाएगी? — Which floor is the lift going to? · धन्यवाद, बड़ी मदद हुई। — Thank you, that helped a lot.

Warm-up. Mime a heavy bag and a stuck gate. Freeze. Choose the line. Teach. मदद कीजिए (aap) vs मदद करो (close person). Dignity: you may ask. You are not a burden. Guided. Three cards: heavy bag, wrong floor, can’t read a notice. Partner helps. Free. Lobby role-play: guard, neighbour, delivery person. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Ask for one real small help this week in Hindi.

Slips. मेरी मदद करो सर → ज़रा मदद कीजिए; बैग हैवी है → बैग भारी है; थैंक्स ब्रदर → धन्यवाद, मदद हुई

Lesson 11 · Doctor ke kamre mein 3m, 6m

Tell a doctor what hurts, since when, and ask about medicine.

Say this: डॉक्टर साहब, तीन दिन से बुखार है। — Doctor, I have had fever for three days. · पेट में दर्द है। — There is pain in the stomach. · दवाई कब खानी है? — When should I take the medicine? · मुझे एलर्जी है। — I have an allergy.

Warm-up. Two chairs: doctor and patient. No comedy voices. Teach. से for since. खानी with दवाई (f). Aap stays on. Seniors may bring a real health word they need. Guided. Pairs: cough, blood pressure, can’t sleep. Listener writes the ‘prescription’ as speech. Free. Clinic + chemist: same complaint, then ‘din mein do baar’. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: one real or practice symptom, three lines.

Slips. मुझे कफ़ है तीन दिन → तीन दिन से खांसी है; दवाई कब खाना → दवाई कब खानी है?; डॉक्टर तुम देखो → डॉक्टर साहब, देखिए

Lesson 12 · Phone: main bol raha / rahi hoon 6w, 3m, 6m

Open a call with your name and one clear reason.

Say this: हेलो, मैं मीरा बोल रही हूँ। — Hello, this is Meera speaking. · हेलो, मैं अरुण बोल रहा हूँ। — Hello, this is Arun speaking. · क्या राजू जी घर पर हैं? — Is Raju-ji at home? · पाँच मिनट में आ रही हूँ / आ रहा हूँ। — I will come in five minutes.

Warm-up. Back-to-back chairs. Phones stay imaginary. Sit. Teach. बोल रहा / रही agrees with the speaker, not the listener. Start with name. Don’t teach a whole telecom syllabus. Guided. Three 20-second calls: home, shop, wrong number + माफ़ कीजिए. Free. Missed-call return: मैंने मिस कॉल किया था, अब बात करूँ? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note that sounds like a real 15-second call.

Slips. मैं मीरा है → मैं मीरा बोल रही हूँ; मैं अरुण बोल रहा है → मैं अरुण बोल रहा हूँ; राजू घर है क्या ओनली → क्या राजू जी घर पर हैं?

Lesson 13 · Ilaake ki jagahen 3m, 6m

Name park, kirana, clinic, school, bus stop near you.

Say this: घर के पास पार्क है। — There is a park near home. · किराना दूकान नीचे है। — The kirana is downstairs. · बस स्टॉप थोड़ा दूर है। — The bus stop is a little far. · क्लिनिक सोसाइटी के गेट के बाहर है। — The clinic is outside the society gate.

Warm-up. Four place words on the board. People add their real names. Teach. के पास / के नीचे / थोड़ा दूर / के बाहर. Temple, mosque, church, gurdwara — whoever they actually visit, or none. Guided. Partner guesses which area from three clues. Free. A new tenant asks what is near. Answer sitting, slowly. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Name three nearby places to someone at home.

Slips. घर पास पार्क → घर के पास पार्क है; दूकान है नीचे ओनली → किराना दूकान नीचे है; क्लिनिक आउटसाइड → क्लिनिक गेट के बाहर है

Lesson 14 · Dheere dheere raasta 3m, 6m

Give slow directions: seedhe, baaye, daaye, and a landmark.

Say this: सीधे जाइए। — Please go straight. · बाएँ मुड़िए। — Please turn left. · दूसरी गली में मंदिर है। — There is a temple in the second lane. · गेट के पास रुकिए। — Please stop near the gate.

Warm-up. Tape a small floor path, or point on a paper. Seniors direct from a chair. Teach. इए with aap. Friends may hear जाओ, मुड़ो. Landmark before the turn. Guided. Partner guides a ‘visitor’ to the window, then the door. Free. From the main road to your building: four sentences, no rush. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Give a family member the lift-to-door route in Hindi.

Slips. लेफ्ट जाओ आप → बाएँ मुड़िए; सीधे जा तुम अंकल → सीधे जाइए; पार्क है दूसरी → दूसरी गली में पार्क है
