Lesson 15 · Kapde aur mausam 3m, 6m

Say what you wear for heat, rain, and a function.

Say this: आज गर्मी है, सूती कपड़े पहने हैं। — It is hot; I am wearing cotton. · बारिश हो रही है, छाता चाहिए। — It is raining; I need an umbrella. · शादी है, अच्छे कपड़े पहनूँगा / पहनूँगी। — There is a wedding; I will wear good clothes.

Warm-up. Window or phone weather photo. No fashion show. Teach. गर्मी / सर्दी / बारिश. पहन रहा / रही. Function clothes are adult, not costume. Guided. If-then pairs: गर्मी → सूती; बारिश → छाता; सर्दी → शॉल या स्वेटर. Free. Pack a small bag aloud for a wet evening plus a puja or dinner. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tomorrow’s weather + one clothing line on a voice note.

Slips. आज गरम है बहुत ओनली → आज बहुत गर्मी है; मैं ब्लू शर्ट पहनता → मैं नीली शर्ट पहन रहा/रही हूँ; बारिश है हो → बारिश हो रही है

Lesson 16 · Darwaze kholne wali baatein 3m, 6m

Use please, thanks, sorry, and one-minute so the room opens.

Say this: ज़रा एक मिनट। — Just a minute, please. · कृपया बैठिए। — Please sit. · माफ़ कीजिए, लेट हो गया / गई। — Sorry I am late. · शुक्रिया, बड़ी मेहरबानी। — Thank you, that’s kind.

Warm-up. Mime: bump in a corridor, late to class, someone holding a door. Teach. Teacher/elder/stranger = माफ़ कीजिए, बैठिए. Close friend = सॉरी, बैठो. Both are real city Hindi. Guided. Card sort: who hears which line? Then say it, don’t only sort. Free. Three doors: society office, clinic desk, neighbour. Open each with one line. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use शुक्रिया or माफ़ कीजिए once today with a real adult.

Slips. माफ़ करो सर → माफ़ कीजिए; बैठो आप → आप बैठिए; थैंक यू सो मच ओनली → शुक्रिया, मेहरबानी

Lesson 17 · Pichhla Itvaar 3m, 6m

Tell last Sunday with gaya/gayi and kiya/ki — a real adult Sunday.

Say this: रविवार को मैं बाज़ार गया / गई। — On Sunday I went to the market. · हमने फिल्म देखी। — We watched a film. · मैं घर पर ही था / थी, आराम किया। — I was at home and rested.

Warm-up. Three prompts: market, rest, relatives. Rest is a valid Sunday. Teach. गया / गई with the speaker. हमने + देखा/देखी. Staying home is not laziness language. Guided. Pair interview: two true sentences, one question. Free. Lie detector: two true, one false. Class guesses, kindly. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: last Sunday, four sentences.

Slips. मैं गया मार्केट → मैं बाज़ार गया/गई; हम देखा फिल्म → हमने फिल्म देखी; कुछ नहीं किया, बेकार दिन → मैं घर पर था/थी, आराम किया

Lesson 18 · Kal kya hoga 3m, 6m

Talk about tomorrow with a plan, a maybe, and a can’t.

Say this: कल सुबह मार्केट जाऊँगी / जाऊँगा। — Tomorrow morning I will go to the market. · शायद बारिश होगी। — It might rain. · कल नहीं आ पाऊँगा / पाऊँगी। — I won’t be able to come tomorrow.

Warm-up. A tiny diary square: कल. People write nothing; they speak. Teach. गा/गी, शायद, नहीं आ पाऊँगा/गी. पता नहीं is a complete adult answer. Guided. Plans in pairs, then change one plan. Free. Phone cancel for tomorrow: polite, short, aap or tum as needed. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell someone at home one real कल plan in Hindi.

Slips. कल मैं जाना मार्केट → कल मैं मार्केट जाऊँगा/जाऊँगी; मईबी रेन → शायद बारिश होगी; नहीं आ सकता कल ओनली → कल नहीं आ पाऊँगा/पाऊँगी

Lesson 19 · School gate ki Hindi 6w, 3m, 6m

Speak at the school gate: child name, class, a short message for the teacher.

Say this: मैम, मैं आरव की माँ / पापा हूँ। — Ma’am, I am Aarav’s mother / father. · क्लास थ्री बी। — Class 3B. · आज थोड़ा लेट होंगे। — They will be a little late today. · होमवर्क बैग में है। — The homework is in the bag.

Warm-up. A ‘gate’ of two chairs. Adults without school children play aunt, uncle, or neighbour — still dignified. Teach. Aap + मैम/सर. Child’s name first. No shouting across a road in class; practise volume for a real gate. Guided. Three gates: late, sick, forgot tiffin. Free. Guard + teacher: two different aap conversations. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. If you have a school gate this week, use one line. If not, voice-note the intro.

Slips. मैम तुम सुनो → मैम, एक मिनट सुनिए; मैं आरव मम्मी → मैं आरव की माँ हूँ; लेट है बच्चा → आज थोड़ा लेट होंगे

Lesson 20 · Review aur test 1 6w, 3m, 6m

Reuse name, home, shop, help, and how-you-are in a kind mid test.

Say this: नमस्ते। मेरा नाम ___ है। मैं ___ में रहती/रहता हूँ। आज ___ हूँ। यह कितने का है? ज़रा मदद कीजिए।

Warm-up. Station rotation, sitting: name, home, kirana, help. Two minutes each. Teach. Teacher notes aap repairs and shame phrases. Do not announce scores aloud. Guided. Pairs run the five lines without reading. Free. Mini test: 90 seconds + one kirana or help role-play. Smile. Seniors get extra wait time. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: the five lines, sitting or standing, as the body allows.

Slips. Only English frames → Keep the Hindi frames; one English noun is fine; मैं ठीक, स्टॉप → आज ___ हूँ — add one real word

Lesson 21 · Paise, wapas, UPI 3m, 6m

Talk change, a total, and everyday UPI words.

Say this: कुल एक सौ बीस रुपये। — The total is one hundred twenty rupees. · वापस बीस दीजिए। — Please give twenty back. · UPI कर दूँ? — Shall I do UPI? · स्कैन कीजिए। पेमेंट हो गया। — Please scan. The payment is done.

Warm-up. Paper notes and a drawn QR. Count change slowly once as a class. Teach. वापस, कुल, स्कैन, पेमेंट. UPI is city Hindi. Practise both cash and scan. Guided. Pair shop: cash change, then UPI fail and retry. Free. Split a 200-rupee snack bill two ways, spoken. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Next real payment: one Hindi line before you scan or pay.

Slips. वापस पैसे दो तुम → वापस बीस रुपये दीजिए; आई विल यूपीआई → मैं UPI कर देती/देता हूँ; पेमेंट हो गया क्या ओनली → पेमेंट हो गया। चेक कर लीजिए

Lesson 22 · Bus, auto, metro 3m, 6m

Ride the city: stop, ticket or card, seat, get down.

Say this: यहीं रुकिए। — Please stop here. · एक टिकट, राजेन्द्र नगर। — One ticket, Rajendra Nagar. · यह सीट खाली है क्या? — Is this seat free? · मुझे अगले स्टॉप पर उतरना है। — I have to get down at the next stop.

Warm-up. Chairs as auto. Driver in front. Seniors sit; they do not bounce. Teach. Public speech stays aap. Metro words: कार्ड, गेट, स्टेशन — keep them. Guided. Pairs: wrong stop, crowded seat, auto fare check. Free. Whole-class route: each person names one real stop from their city. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use one line on a real ride this week if you travel.

Slips. यहाँ रुक तुम → यहीं रुकिए; सीट मैं लेता → यह सीट खाली है क्या?; अगला स्टॉप गेट डाउन → मुझे अगले स्टॉप पर उतरना है

Lesson 23 · Bank ya post office 6m

Do one window task: token, form, or a parcel, with aap.

Say this: टोकन नंबर कितना है? — What is the token number? · फॉर्म कहाँ भरें? — Where should we fill the form? · यह पार्सल भेजना है। — I need to send this parcel. · पासबुक अपडेट कर दीजिए। — Please update the passbook.

Warm-up. A window of two desks. Waiting chairs. No rushing the queue. Teach. Window Hindi is slow and loud-enough, not angry. Seniors repeat the token question twice. Guided. Three tasks: deposit line, parcel, missed number. Free. Officer is tired; you stay polite and clear. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: one bank or post line you might actually need.

Slips. फॉर्म कहाँ → फॉर्म कहाँ भरें?; पार्सल सेंड करना → यह पार्सल भेजना है; तुम अपडेट करो → पासबुक अपडेट कर दीजिए

Lesson 24 · Ghar pe mehman 3m, 6m

Receive guests: come in, sit, tea, and a polite short visit.

Say this: अंदर आइए, बैठिए। — Please come in, please sit. · चाय लेंगी / लेंगे? — Will you take tea? · ज़रा देर ठहरिए। — Please stay a little while. · अभी निकलना पड़ेगा, माफ़ कीजिए। — I will have to leave now, sorry.

Warm-up. Door of the classroom is the flat door. Host stays calm. Teach. आइए / बैठिए. Tea offer is culture, not compulsory; practise a kind no: अभी नहीं, धन्यवाद. Guided. Pairs: unexpected neighbour, planned aunt, guest who must leave. Free. Two hosts, one guest. Seniors host from a chair. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise the door lines once at home, even alone.

Slips. आओ अंदर आप → अंदर आइए; चाय लो → चाय लीजिए; नाउ आई हैव टू गो → अभी निकलना पड़ेगा, माफ़ कीजिए

Lesson 25 · Bacche, ghar, agla saal 6m

Talk about children or family plans — including no children, and grandchildren.

Say this: मेरे दो बच्चे हैं। — I have two children. · अभी प्लान नहीं है। — There is no plan right now. · पोती स्कूल जाती है। — My granddaughter goes to school. · अगले साल माँ के पास जा सकते हैं। — Next year we might go to my mother’s.

Warm-up. Warn the room: no advice, no ‘when will you’. Only language. Teach. हैं with बच्चे. नहीं है as a full answer. Seniors may choose grandchildren. Never pry. Guided. Partner shares only what they want. Listener says अच्छा, और क्या बताना है? Free. Three dignified lives: children, no children, care for parents. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Optional voice note: one true family sentence. Skip if private.

Slips. मैं दो बच्चे है → मेरे दो बच्चे हैं; अभी नो प्लान → अभी प्लान नहीं है; मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ, कुछ नहीं → मैं घर सँभालती/सँभालता हूँ; अगले साल...

Lesson 26 · Samajh nahi aaya — izzat ke saath 3m, 6m

Say you don’t understand and ask for a slower repeat, without shrinking.

Say this: माफ़ कीजिए, समझ नहीं आया / आई। — Sorry, I didn’t understand. · ज़रा धीरे बोलिए। — Please speak a little slowly. · एक बार फिर बताइए। — Please tell me once more. · मेरा मतलब यह था... — What I meant was...

Warm-up. Teacher speaks too fast on purpose, then repairs. Smile. No mock. Teach. आया / आई with the speaker. This is strength, not failure. Seniors get a second silence after the line. Guided. Repair clinic: shop, doctor, building secretary — you missed a word. Free. Hot seat: teacher uses one new word; student uses the four lines. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use ज़रा धीरे बोलिए once this week in a real conversation.

Slips. मैं नहीं समझ → समझ नहीं आया/आई; रिपीट करो आप → एक बार फिर बताइए; सॉरी आई एम डम → माफ़ कीजिए, धीरे बोलिए

Lesson 27 · Thaila kho gaya 6m

Tell a short story: I lost my bag, I asked, I found it or I didn’t.

Say this: मेरा थैला ऑटो में रह गया। — My bag was left in the auto. · मैंने गार्ड से पूछा। — I asked the guard. · वह गेट के पास मिला। — It was found near the gate. · नहीं मिला, पुलिस में नाम लिखाया। — It wasn’t found; I filed a name at the police post.

Warm-up. Four picture cards in order. Sit and point. Teach. रह गया / गई. मैंने + पूछा. Two endings: found / not found. Both are adult stories. Guided. Retell with a partner, change the place: metro, market, clinic. Free. New detail: what was inside (without valuables theatre). Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell the story to someone at home, four lines.

Slips. बैग लॉस्ट हो गया → थैला खो गया / ऑटो में रह गया; मैं पूछा गार्ड → मैंने गार्ड से पूछा; पुलिस में कंप्लेंट → पुलिस चौकी पर नाम लिखाया

Lesson 28 · Pasand ki jagah 3m, 6m

Talk about a favourite city place with kyunki and one honest problem.

Say this: मुझे सब्ज़ी मंडी पसंद है क्योंकि ताज़ी सब्ज़ी मिलती है। — I like the sabzi mandi because the vegetables are fresh. · पार्क में हवा है, शाम को भीड़ भी है। — The park has air; in the evening there is also a crowd. · घर की छत शांत है। — The terrace at home is quiet.

Warm-up. Phone photos welcome: park, mandi, temple, tea stall, terrace. Teach. पसंद + क्योंकि + one problem. Terrace and kitchen window count as places. Guided. Pairs convince each other to visit. Seniors may choose a bench, not a trek. Free. Class hears three places. No vote that ranks lives. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: favourite place, 40 seconds.

Slips. मुझे पार्क लाइक → मुझे पार्क पसंद है; क्योंकि ताज़ा → क्योंकि वहाँ ताज़ी सब्ज़ी मिलती है; भीड़ है ना, नो गो → पसंद है, लेकिन शाम को भीड़ है

Lesson 29 · Meri zindagi — taiyari 6w, 3m, 6m

Prepare a one-minute talk: name, home, day, one place, one people sentence.

Say this: नमस्ते। मेरा नाम ___ है। मैं ___ में रहती/रहता हूँ। सुबह ___। मुझे ___ पसंद है। घर में ___।

Warm-up. Teacher models one minute, sitting, adult voice. Then a slow repeat-after for seniors. Teach. Keywords on a card, not a paragraph to read. Homemaking is content. Delete any shame line you hear in rehearsal. Guided. Rehearse with a partner. Partner asks one kind question. Free. Two volunteers. Clap is optional; a quiet शुक्रिया from the class is enough. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise twice at home. Sit if you wish. Do not rush.

Slips. Reading in a tiny voice → Look at one face and speak; मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ → मैं घर का काम करती/करता हूँ, फिर...; Only name and stop → Add home, day, and one pasand

Lesson 30 · Aakhri bolne ka test 6w, 3m, 6m

Give the one-minute life talk and one shop or help role-play. Feel proud.

Say this: Teacher questions in full sentences. Student answers sitting or standing.

Warm-up. Quiet pairs practise while you test one-to-one. No public ranking. Teach. Test: namaste, how-are-you (real), home, kirana or help, 60-second life talk. Guided. Under five minutes. Warm voice. Extra silence for seniors. Free. Circle, still seated: मैं हिंदी बोल सकता / सकती हूँ — slowly, together. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. No homework. Start Everyday when they can do this without panic.

Slips. Failing an adult for accent → Score courage, sentences, and being understood; Correcting on stage → Note two strengths and one next sentence privately

Urban Spoken Hindi · Adults · Rozmarra sheher

Adults · Rozmarra sheher

Adults 26–50 · also Seniors 50+ Elementary / Everyday 24 lessons

PTA, doctor details, appointments, society, city travel, a week in the building.

Who: Adults who can already do the Absolute Beginner test, or who survive a shop but freeze at school gate or the clinic.

Done when: They can handle PTA, a clinic story, a complaint, a neighbour chat, and a two-minute week talk.

Place here when: Place here after Absolute, or if they can buy vegetables but cannot talk to a class teacher.

Teacher note: If you are teaching seniors (50+), use this same course at a slower pace. Skip job-interview energy. Keep everyday city life. Repeat more. Sit more. Praise effort every time. Never rush a person who is thinking. Seniors may skip workplace-like society fights if it drains them; keep guests, park, and doctor.

Tests

StartNeighbour hello, shop, one home sentence.
MidPTA or doctor, appointment call, complaint.
EndWeek-in-my-city plus one role-play.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Building ka lamba hello 6w, 3m, 6m

Keep a neighbour chat going past namaste: day, family, one building detail.

Say this: नमस्ते आंटी, आज कैसा रहा? — Namaste aunty, how was today? · लिफ्ट फिर खराब है। — The lift is out of order again. · बच्चे स्कूल टाइम पर निकल गए। — The children left on school time. · आप चाय पीने आएँगे / आएँगी? — Will you come for tea?

Warm-up. Stand or sit in a ‘corridor’. 20-second hellos that die on अच्छा get a redo. Teach. One-word अच्छा stops the chat. Add one fact. Aap with older neighbours; tum only if that is truly your relation. Guided. Speed meetings: 40 seconds, rotate three partners. Free. Keep a Hindi chat for one minute with no teacher in the pair. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Start a two-line neighbour chat this week, even at the gate.

Slips. अच्छा। बाय → आज ठीक रहा। लिफ्ट बंद थी, सीढ़ी चढ़ी; तुम कैसे हो आंटी → आप कैसे हैं आंटी?; लिफ्ट नॉट वर्किंग → लिफ्ट खराब है

Lesson 02 · Poora weekday 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell a full weekday with pehle, phir, uske baad — home or office.

Say this: पहले मैं नाश्ता लगाती हूँ / लगाता हूँ। — First I set out breakfast. · फिर स्कूल्स की ड्रॉपिंग / दफ्तर। — Then the school drop / the office. · उसके बाद दोपहर का काम। — After that, the afternoon work. · शाम को ट्यूशन या बाज़ार। — In the evening, tuition or the market.

Warm-up. Five clock times on the board. People fill them by speaking. Teach. Linkers, not a grammar lecture. Homemakers’ weekdays are full. Seniors may tell a slower day. Guided. Retell your partner’s Wednesday in their shoes. Free. A day that slipped: rain, late driver, guest. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: your real Thursday, five lines with pehle/phir.

Slips. पहले नाश्ता → पहले मैं नाश्ता लगाती/लगाता हूँ; फिर ड्रॉपिंग करती → फिर बच्चों को स्कूल छोड़ती/छोड़ता हूँ; मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ पूरा दिन → सुबह से शाम तक घर का काम चलता है

Lesson 03 · Market mein choice 6w, 3m, 6m

Choose in a market: this or that, fresher, cheaper, not this one.

Say this: यह ताज़ा है, वह नरम है। — This is fresh; that is soft. · थोड़ा और दिखाइए। — Please show a little more. · सस्ता मिलेगा क्या? — Will I get it cheaper? · यह नहीं, बिल्कुल वाली दीजिए। — Not this; please give the firm ones.

Warm-up. Two piles of (real or pictured) sabzi or fruit. Adults choose, they do not ‘play shop’. Teach. यह / वह, और दिखाइए, सस्ता, नहीं यह वाला. Aap with the seller. Guided. Pairs bargain without fighting. Practise अभी नहीं, धन्यवाद. Free. Family market: one speaks, one is silent wallet. Then swap. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use थोड़ा और दिखाइए once at a real stall.

Slips. यह फ्रेश है → यह ताज़ा है; और दिखा तुम → थोड़ा और दिखाइए; नहीं चाहिए रुक → अभी नहीं, धन्यवाद

Lesson 04 · PTA aur homework 6w, 3m, 6m

Talk to a class teacher about homework, marks, and how the child is at home.

Say this: मैम, होमवर्क रोज़ कितना होता है? — Ma’am, how much homework is there each day? · घर पर वह खुद बैठता है / बैठती है। — At home he/she sits on their own. · गणित में थोड़ी मदद चाहिए। — They need a little help in maths. · कल मीटिंग में आऊँगी / आऊँगा। — I will come to the meeting tomorrow.

Warm-up. Desk + two chairs. No scolding-the-child theatre. Teach. Aap + मैम/सर. Child’s name and class first. Adults without school-age children play a grandparent or a neighbour helping. Guided. Three PTAs: homework load, friendship worry, missed notebook. Free. Teacher talks fast; parent uses धीरे बोलिए and repeats the action. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Write no essay. Voice-note a 30-second PTA opening.

Slips. मैम तुम बताओ → मैम, ज़रा बताइए; होमवर्क बहुत है ना → होमवर्क रोज़ कितना होता है?; बच्चा डल है → गणित में थोड़ी मदद चाहिए — no shame labels

Lesson 05 · Doctor: poori baat 6w, 3m, 6m

Give a clinic story: since when, worse when, medicine names you actually use.

Say this: दो हफ्ते से खांसी है, रात को बढ़ती है। — Cough for two weeks, worse at night. · बीपी की दवाई चल रही है। — I am on BP medicine. · टेस्ट करवाना है क्या? — Do I need a test? · पहले वाली दवाई से आराम था। — The earlier medicine had given relief.

Warm-up. A small timeline on the board: pehle, ab, raat ko. Teach. से, बढ़ती है, आराम. Seniors may bring one real medicine word. No diagnosis from the teacher. Guided. Patient + attendant + doctor. Attendant speaks only if asked, then with aap. Free. Chemist follow-up: khali pet, khane ke baad, kitni goli. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: one detailed symptom paragraph, 40 seconds.

Slips. मुझे कफ़ है लॉन्ग टाइम → दो हफ्ते से खांसी है; रात को बढ़ता है खांसी → रात को खांसी बढ़ती है; दवाई पुराना गुड था → पहले वाली दवाई से आराम था

Lesson 06 · Appointment aur callback 6w, 3m, 6m

Book, shift, or return a call for clinic, salon, or society office.

Say this: क्या शनिवार को स्लॉट मिलेगा? — Will a Saturday slot be available? · मैं सुबह दस बजे आ सकती हूँ / सकता हूँ। — I can come at ten in the morning. · आप वापस कॉल कर देंगी / देंगे? — Will you call back? · मैं मीरा बोल रही हूँ, कल वाली अपॉइंटमेंट। — This is Meera; about yesterday’s appointment.

Warm-up. Back-to-back phones. Write the time only after they say it. Teach. मिल जाएगा / मिलेगा, वापस कॉल, बोल रही/रहा हूँ. Reception gets aap. Guided. Three calls: book, postpone, they missed your call. Free. Hold music, then you repeat name + reason slowly. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. If you book anything this week, open in Hindi.

Slips. आई वांट स्लॉट → क्या शनिवार को स्लॉट मिलेगा?; कॉल बैक करना → आप वापस कॉल कर दीजिएगा; मैं मीरा है, अपॉइंटमेंट → मैं मीरा बोल रही हूँ, अपॉइंटमेंट के बारे में

Lesson 07 · Complex ke andar raasta 3m, 6m

Give directions inside a society: tower, wing, parking, clubhouse.

Say this: बी विंग, तीसरी मंज़िल, लेफ्ट साइड। — B wing, third floor, left side. · क्लबहाउस गेट के पीछे है। — The clubhouse is behind the gate. · पार्किंग बेसमेंट में है, स्लोप से जाइए। — Parking is in the basement; take the slope. · गार्ड से पूछिए, वह बता देंगे। — Ask the guard; he will tell you.

Warm-up. Paper map of a fake society. Seniors keep the map on the table. Teach. विंग, टॉवर, बेसमेंट are city words. Mix is fine. Imperatives stay आइए/जाइए with strangers. Guided. Visitor from another tower. Partner guides without pointing first, then may point. Free. Night-time: lights failed, you guide someone to their lobby. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Explain your real wing/floor to a partner in four lines.

Slips. बी विंग लेफ्ट जाना → बी विंग, बाएँ मुड़िए; पार्किंग इज़ इन बेसमेंट → पार्किंग बेसमेंट में है; गार्ड से पूछ तुम → गार्ड से पूछिए

Lesson 08 · Pichhla mahina, agla mahina 3m, 6m

Talk last month and next month: bill, trip, school, health.

Say this: पिछले महीने बिजली का बिल ज़्यादा आया। — Last month the electricity bill was high. · अगले महीने दीपावली है। — Next month is Diwali. · हम अगले महीने गाँव जा सकते हैं। — We might go to the village next month. · पिछले महीने डॉक्टर के पास गई / गया था। — I had gone to the doctor last month.

Warm-up. A 60-day strip: पिछला / अगला. People add one true event. Teach. पिछले महीने, अगले महीने. था/थी/थे light touch. Festivals of any faith, or none. Guided. Pair: one last-month fact, one next-month plan, one शायद. Free. Society notice: water cut next month — tell a neighbour. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: one पिछला, one अगला.

Slips. लास्ट मंथ बिल हाई → पिछले महीने बिल ज़्यादा आया; नेक्स्ट मंथ विलेज → अगले महीने गाँव जा सकते हैं; मैं गया था डॉक्टर लास्ट → पिछले महीने डॉक्टर के पास गया/गई था/थी

Lesson 09 · Complaint jo kaam kare 6w, 3m, 6m

Make a complaint that is specific, polite, and asks for a time.

Say this: दो दिन से पानी नहीं आ रहा। — There has been no water for two days. · कृपया आज शाम तक देखिए। — Please look at it by this evening. · टिकट नंबर दे दीजिए। — Please give me a ticket number. · यह तीसरी बार बोल रही हूँ / रहा हूँ। — This is the third time I am saying it.

Warm-up. Board: fact + ask + when. Anger mime, then lower the voice. Teach. Aap stays even when you are right. Specific beats loud. Seniors practise the same lines twice. Guided. Water, parking, school bus, wrong grocery order — pairs pick one. Free. Secretary is vague; you ask again for a time and a name. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. If nothing is broken, voice-note a practice water complaint.

Slips. पानी नहीं है यार फिक्स करो → दो दिन से पानी नहीं आ रहा, आज शाम तक देखिए; आई एम फर्स्ट टाइम कंप्लेन → यह तीसरी बार बता रही/रहा हूँ; टिकट नंबर दे तुम → टिकट नंबर दे दीजिए

Lesson 10 · Padosiyon ko bulao, naa bhi bolo 3m, 6m

Invite a neighbour, accept, or say no without a story that wounds.

Say this: रविवार को चाय पर आइए। — Please come for tea on Sunday. · ज़रूर आएँगे। — We will surely come. · उस दिन नहीं हो पाएगा, धन्यवाद। — That day won’t be possible, thank you. · बच्चों की छुट्टी है, घर पर रहना है। — The children have a holiday; we need to stay home.

Warm-up. Two invitations on slips. People may refuse; that is the skill. Teach. आइए for most neighbours. A clean no: नहीं हो पाएगा + one short reason or none. Guided. Invite, accept, postpone, refuse. Rotate. Free. Festival tray at the door: take, refuse sugar, thank. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Invite or thank one neighbour in Hindi this week.

Slips. आओ चाय तुम आंटी → रविवार को चाय पर आइए; नो आई कैनॉट → उस दिन नहीं हो पाएगा, धन्यवाद; मैं बिज़ी हूँ ओनली → उस दिन निकलना मुश्किल है

Lesson 11 · Fees, kiraya, EMI 3m, 6m

Say school fees, rent, and EMI words without panic or shame.

Say this: इस महीने फीस जमा करनी है। — Fees have to be paid this month. · किराया पाँच तारीख तक देना है। — Rent has to be given by the fifth. · EMI ऑटो-डेबिट हो जाती है। — The EMI is auto-debited. · थोड़ा इंतज़ार करवाइए, पैसे आ जाएँगे। — Please wait a little; the money will come.

Warm-up. Three envelopes: फीस, किराया, EMI. No one announces real numbers unless they want. Teach. जमा करनी है, देना है, हो जाती है. Money talk is adult. Teacher does not ask salaries. Guided. Pairs: school counter, landlord, bank SMS confusion. Free. Kind delay: one sentence, no autobiography. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Read one real SMS or slip at home and say it in one Hindi line.

Slips. फीस पे करना → फीस जमा करनी है; रेंट बाय फिफ्थ → किराया पाँच तारीख तक देना है; मैं पैसे नहीं, शर्म → थोड़ा समय दीजिए, पैसे आ जाएँगे — no shame speech

Lesson 12 · Hindustan ke andar safar 3m, 6m

Handle a short India trip: ticket, platform, relative’s house — not a full travel course.

Say this: ट्रेन सुबह छह बजे है। — The train is at six in the morning. · कौन सा प्लेटफ़ॉर्म है? — Which platform is it? · हम दो दिन नानी के घर रुकेंगे। — We will stay two days at nani’s house. · सामान ऊपर रख दीजिए। — Please put the luggage up.

Warm-up. One suitcase (or bag) in the room. Sit on the ‘berth’ chairs. Teach. प्लेटफ़ॉर्म, बर्थ, सामान. Aap with coolies and TTE. This is a weekend add-on, not tourism class. Guided. Pairs: late taxi to station, wrong coach, call the person picking you up. Free. Two-minute trip: city A to city B, one problem, arrival line. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. If you have a trip this term, practise the opening call in Hindi.

Slips. कौन प्लेटफ़ॉर्म → कौन सा प्लेटफ़ॉर्म है?; लगेज ऊपर रख तुम → सामान ऊपर रख दीजिए; वी विल स्टे टू डेज → हम दो दिन रुकेंगे

Lesson 13 · Agar baarish, agar late 6m

Use agar for rain, late school, late guest, late office.

Say this: अगर बारिश हुई तो कैब लेंगे। — If it rains we will take a cab. · अगर बस लेट हुई तो मैम को मैसेज करूँगी / करूँगा। — If the bus is late I will message ma’am. · अगर तुम लेट हो तो खाना रख दूँगी / दूँगा। — If you are late I will keep the food.

Warm-up. Three picture ifs: rain, clock, guest. No grammar tree. Teach. अगर + तो. One clean pair is enough. Tum inside the family if that is their home; aap for the teacher message. Guided. Build four if-then lives: rain, late child, late husband/wife, late driver. Free. Society function: if the power cuts, what do you say to guests? Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: two real अगर lines for this week.

Slips. इफ रेन तो कैब → अगर बारिश हुई तो कैब लेंगे; अगर लेट तो मैसेज → अगर बस लेट हुई तो मैसेज करूँगी/गा; खाना रख दूँ तुम लेट → अगर तुम लेट हो तो खाना रख दूँगी/गा

Lesson 14 · Film, match, serial 6m

Talk about a film, a match, or a serial without a review essay.

Say this: कल मैच देखा, बहुत टाइट था। — I watched the match yesterday; it was very tight. · यह सीरियल लंबा है, फिर भी देखती हूँ / देखता हूँ। — This serial is long, still I watch it. · फिल्म अच्छी लगी, कहानी कमज़ोर थी। — I liked the film; the story was weak.

Warm-up. One still on a phone. People who don’t watch TV say so — full answer. Teach. देखी / देखा, अच्छी लगी, फिर भी. Opinions stay short. No exam on cricket rules. Guided. Pairs: recommend, refuse, agree halfway. Free. Family fight over the remote — kind Hindi, 40 seconds. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Tell someone one line about the last thing you watched — or that you don’t watch.

Slips. मैच वॉच किया → मैंने मैच देखा; फिल्म वाज़ गुड → फिल्म अच्छी लगी; सीरियल बोरिंग बट आई वॉच → सीरियल लंबा है, फिर भी देखती/देखता हूँ

Lesson 15 · Kam Hindi wale ki madad 6m

Help someone with less Hindi: slow, short, no baby voice.

Say this: धीरे-धीरे बोलिए, वह नई हैं। — Please speak slowly; she/he is new. · आप बैठिए, मैं पूछ कर बताती हूँ / बताता हूँ। — Please sit; I will ask and tell you. · यह फॉर्म यहाँ भरना है। — This form has to be filled here. · समझ आ रहा है? — Is it making sense?

Warm-up. One student is the new neighbour. The helper does not raise pitch like a child song. Teach. Short sentences. Aap to the new adult. Never laugh at their Hindi. Seniors are often the patient helpers. Guided. Clinic desk, society office, school gate — help without taking over. Free. The new person says समझ नहीं आया; you repair, you don’t shout. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Help one person this week with a slow Hindi line, or practise with family.

Slips. आप हो नई, सिंपल बोलो → वह नई हैं, धीरे बोलिए; फॉर्म यहाँ, क्विक → यह फॉर्म यहाँ भरना है; अंडरस्टैंड? (sharp) → समझ आ रहा है?

Lesson 16 · Review aur test 2 6w, 3m, 6m

Reuse building hello, PTA or doctor, a complaint, and an appointment call.

Say this: आंटी, आज कैसा रहा? मैम, होमवर्क... दो दिन से पानी नहीं। शनिवार को स्लॉट मिलेगा?

Warm-up. Four stations, sitting. Teacher times 90 seconds each. Teach. Listen for aap at the gate and the clinic. Note any shame line and rewrite privately. Guided. Pairs choose PTA or doctor, then one complaint. Free. Mid test: neighbour + one long role-play. Extra wait for seniors. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: neighbour hello plus one complaint, 50 seconds.

Slips. Jumping to English when stuck → Use धीरे बोलिए and stay in the frame; Complaint as shouting → Fact + time + aap

Lesson 17 · Society meeting, chhoti 6w, 3m, 6m

Speak once in a miniature society meeting: point, agree, ask a cost.

Say this: मेरा एक पॉइंट है। — I have one point. · मुझे सहमति है, लेकिन समय कम है। — I agree, but time is short. · यह काम कितने का होगा? — About how much will this work cost? · मिनट्स में लिख दीजिए। — Please put it in the minutes.

Warm-up. A circle of chairs. One ‘secretary’. Seniors speak seated, once is enough. Teach. Phrases that buy a turn. No speech about national politics. Parking, water, festival fund only. Guided. Each person one point. Others only सहमति / एक सवाल. Free. Meeting runs eight minutes. You stop the over-talker kindly: समय कम है. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Prepare one real building point, even if you never stand to speak it.

Slips. आई हैव अ पॉइंट → मेरा एक पॉइंट है; येस बट → मुझे सहमति है, लेकिन...; कॉस्ट कितना → यह काम कितने का होगा?

Lesson 18 · Darwaze par kaam wale 3m, 6m

Talk to service people at the door: maid, plumber, delivery, cook on leave.

Say this: आइए, जूते बाहर। — Please come; shoes outside. · आज छुट्टी है क्या? — Is it a holiday today? · यह नल टपक रहा है, देखिए। — This tap is dripping; please look. · पार्सल नीचे रख दीजिए। — Please put the parcel down.

Warm-up. Door knock. Adults decide their own household words. No mock of anyone’s work. Teach. Aap is default at the door unless the house truly uses tum with a long-time worker — discuss, don’t police families. Clear task + time. Guided. Four knocks: delivery, plumber, child’s friend, society notice. Free. Person is late; you ask, you do not humiliate. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use one door line this week with a real delivery or worker.

Slips. अंदर आ, जूता → आइए, जूते बाहर उतारिए; नल लीकिंग → नल टपक रहा है; पार्सल दे → पार्सल नीचे रख दीजिए

Lesson 19 · Achhi khabar, buri khabar 6m

Give good news and hard news in short, human Hindi.

Say this: बड़ी खुशी की बात है, नौकरी लग गई। — Happy news: the job came through. · माँ की तबीयत ठीक नहीं है। — Mother’s health is not good. · चिंता मत कीजिए, हम साथ हैं। — Please don’t worry; we are with you. · अभी कुछ मत पूछिए, बाद में बताऊँगी / बताऊँगा। — Please don’t ask more now; I will tell later.

Warm-up. Warn: no real trauma required. Invent or use old, settled news. Teach. खुशी की बात, तबीयत, चिंता मत कीजिए. Soft voice. Seniors may speak of health with dignity. Guided. Pairs: job, baby, transfer, hospital — listener only supports. Free. Phone version of hard news: slower, name first. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Optional: one kind support line to a friend this week.

Slips. गुड न्यूज़, जॉब हो गया → खुशी की बात है, नौकरी लग गई; मम्मी इज़ नॉट वेल ओनली → माँ की तबीयत ठीक नहीं है; डोंट वरी यार (to elder) → चिंता मत कीजिए

Lesson 20 · Safar ki kahani 3m, 6m

Tell a trip story with pehle, phir, a problem, and arrival.

Say this: पहले स्टेशन देर से पहुँचे। — First we reached the station late. · फिर ट्रेन मिल गई। — Then we caught the train. · रास्ते में खाना ठंडा था। — The food on the way was cold. · आखिर में नानी घर पर खड़ी थीं। — In the end nani was standing at the house.

Warm-up. Three photos or three board boxes. Teach. पहला / फिर / आखिर में. गया/गई if they can. A local day-trip counts. Guided. Tell, then change the problem: rain, lost ticket, child sleeping. Free. Audience asks one क्यों? Speaker answers one sentence. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: a real small trip, 50 seconds.

Slips. पहले हम लेट → पहले हम स्टेशन देर से पहुँचे; फाइनली नानी वाज़ देयर → आखिर में नानी घर पर थीं; फूड वाज़ कोल्ड → खाना ठंडा था

Lesson 21 · Sheher par raay 6w, 3m, 6m

Give an opinion on city life: traffic, parks, safety, rent — with kyunki.

Say this: इस शहर में मेट्रो से आसानी है, लेकिन किराया तेज़ है। — Metro makes this city easier, but rent is high. · पार्क कम हैं, बच्चों को जगह चाहिए। — There are few parks; children need space. · मुझे लगता है दुकानें देर तक खुलनी चाहिए। — I think shops should stay open later.

Warm-up. Two photos: jammed road, evening park. No party politics. Teach. मुझे लगता है + क्योंकि + लेकिन. Disagree without a fight. Guided. Pairs take opposite sides for two minutes, then swap. Free. Tiny GD: four people, one topic, one person keeps time. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: one city opinion, 40 seconds, kyunki included.

Slips. ट्रैफिक इज़ बैड → ट्रैफिक बहुत है क्योंकि सड़कें कम हैं; आई थिंक शॉप्स → मुझे लगता है दुकानें...; यू आर रॉन्ग → मेरी बात अलग है, क्योंकि...

Lesson 22 · Class teacher ko call 6m

Call the class teacher: name the child, the issue, and a time to talk.

Say this: मैम, मैं कबीर की माँ बोल रही हूँ, क्लास फाइव ए। — Ma’am, this is Kabir’s mother, class 5A. · दो मिनट मिल जाएँगे? — Can I have two minutes? · कल गेट पर बात कर लें? — Shall we talk at the gate tomorrow? · वॉट्सऐप पर फोटो भेज दूँ? — Shall I send a photo on WhatsApp?

Warm-up. Back-to-back. Teacher is busy; you stay short. Teach. Name + class + one issue + ask for a slot. Aap. Not a speech about the child’s whole life. Guided. Three calls: fever, fight, project. Grandparent callers welcome. Free. Teacher calls you back; you pick up with मैं ___ बोल रही/रहा हूँ. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. If you must call a teacher this week, open in Hindi.

Slips. मैम, कबीर प्रॉब्लम → मैम, मैं कबीर की माँ/पापा बोल रही/रहा हूँ; यू हैव टू मिनट? → क्या दो मिनट मिल जाएँगे?; व्हाट्सऐप फोटो → वॉट्सऐप पर फोटो भेज दूँ?

Lesson 23 · Ek hafte ki taiyari 6w, 3m, 6m

Workshop a two-minute week-in-my-city talk: five days, one problem, one joy.

Say this: सोमवार को... मंगलवार को... एक दिक्कत यह थी... रविवार को आराम मिला।

Warm-up. Teacher models two minutes, sitting. Board: five day names and दिक्कत / राहत. Teach. Keywords only. Homemaking weeks are rich. Seniors may tell three days, not five. Guided. Rehearse twice with a new partner. Partner asks क्यों? once. Free. Two or three volunteers. Class notes one clear sentence, not errors. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise the week talk once at home, timer on.

Slips. Listing English days only → सोमवार को, मंगलवार को; मैं तो घर पे ही थी पूरा हफ्ता → हफ्ते में घर, बाज़ार, और एक डॉक्टर; Reading a paragraph → Five keywords, then speak

Lesson 24 · Hafte ka manch aur aakhri test 6w, 3m, 6m

Give the week talk and one PTA, clinic, or complaint role-play.

Say this: Two-minute week. Then teacher picks one everyday role-play.

Warm-up. Breathing. Sit if you wish. Audience is kind and quiet. Teach. End test: week talk + PTA or doctor or complaint. Score clarity and keep-going, not accent. Guided. Half the room performs; half listens, then swap if time. Free. Circle: इस शहर में मैं हिंदी में काम चला सकती / सकता हूँ. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. No homework. Fluency + workplace if they freeze in rooms; stay here if the city still feels new.

Slips. Public ranking → Private glow and one grow; Fixing gender on stage → Fix later, thank them now

Urban Spoken Hindi · Adults · Meeting room ki Hindi

Adults · Meeting room ki Hindi

Adults 26–50 Intermediate fluency + workplace 23 lessons

Stand-ups, disagreement, client calls, briefings. Return-to-work lives are included.

Who: Working adults and return-to-work adults who understand office Hindi but stay quiet in rooms, or who only speak English at work and now need Hindi with teams or clients.

Done when: They can give a 30-second update, disagree politely, run a short meeting point, and brief a senior for four minutes.

Place here when: Place here if they can do Everyday Hindi but go silent in meetings — or if they are returning to work.

Teacher note: Replace empty office padding with a clear ask. Homemakers returning to work use home operations as proof.

Tests

StartJob talk and one meeting line.
MidMeeting mock.
EndBriefing plus hard question.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Office freeze todna 6w, 3m, 6m

Unfreeze in a Hindi-using room: one seat-taking line and a rescue line.

Say this: एक मिनट, मैं हिंदी में कहती हूँ / कहता हूँ। — One minute, I will say it in Hindi. · मेरा पॉइंट यह है... — My point is... · समझ नहीं आया, दोबारा बोलिए। — I didn’t get that; please say it again. · मैं नोट करके वापस आती हूँ / आता हूँ। — I will note it and come back.

Warm-up. Silent circle, then each person says only मेरा पॉइंट यह है plus one word. Sit. No ice-breaker games. Teach. Freeze is normal. Rescue lines are professional, not weak. Return-to-work adults belong in this room on day one. Guided. Partner throws a fast English meeting line; you answer with one Hindi seat-taker. Free. Tiny stand-up: name, one point, stuck. Sixty seconds total. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: मेरा पॉइंट यह है + one real work or home-ops fact.

Slips. बेसिकली आई वॉज़ जस्ट → मेरा पॉइंट यह है; सॉरी सॉरी समझ नहीं (shrink) → समझ नहीं आया, दोबारा बोलिए; मैं बाद में बोलूँगी, क्वाइट → मैं नोट करके वापस आती/आता हूँ

Lesson 02 · Apna kaam batao 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell what you do in plain Hindi — job, shop, home operations, or a break.

Say this: मैं अकाउंट्स में काम करती हूँ / करता हूँ। — I work in accounts. · मैं घर का पूरा ऑपरेशन सँभालती हूँ / सँभालता हूँ। — I run the whole home operation. · अभी ब्रेक पर हूँ, वापस आना है। — I am on a break; I will return. · मेरा काम टीम को सपोर्ट करना है। — My work is to support the team.

Warm-up. Teacher models a homemaker version and an office version with equal weight. Teach. करती/करता हूँ, सँभालती/ता हूँ. Delete मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ in the air if it appears. Break is a chapter, not an apology. Guided. 90-second work talk. Partner asks एक उदाहरण? Free. Hot seat: say the work to a ‘client uncle’ and to a ‘HR’ — same facts, slightly more aap. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: 40 seconds, no shame phrase, one example.

Slips. मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ → मैं घर का ऑपरेशन सँभालती/सँभालता हूँ; आई एम इन अकाउंट्स ओनली → मैं अकाउंट्स में काम करती/करता हूँ; अभी कुछ नहीं करती → अभी ब्रेक पर हूँ, स्किल्स चल रही हैं

Lesson 03 · Meeting mein seat 6w, 3m, 6m

Use phrases that buy a seat: join late, add, park, and close your bit.

Say this: थोड़ा लेट आई / आया, माफ़ कीजिए। — I came a bit late, sorry. · मैं एक लाइन जोड़ूँ? — May I add one line? · यह पॉइंट पार्क कर दें, बाद में। — Let’s park this point for later. · मेरी तरफ़ से इतना ही। — That’s all from my side.

Warm-up. A messy meeting for 60 seconds. Then replay with the four lines. Teach. जोड़ूँ, पार्क, तरफ़ से. Hinglish park/point is office-normal. Aap with seniors in the room. Guided. Fishbowl: four people, one observer notes who never got a seat. Free. You arrive late; you still take one seat without a speech. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. In any real meeting this week, use one seat-taking line — even in a family money talk.

Slips. सॉरी सॉरी लेट (30 seconds) → थोड़ा लेट आई/आया, माफ़ कीजिए; कैन आई ऐड → मैं एक लाइन जोड़ूँ?; फ्रॉम माई साइड दैट्स इट → मेरी तरफ़ से इतना ही

Lesson 04 · Narmi se asahmati 6w, 3m, 6m

Disagree without a fight and without disappearing.

Say this: मैं समझ गई / गया, मेरी बात थोड़ी अलग है। — I understand; my view is a bit different. · डेटा यह दिखा रहा है... — The data is showing... · क्या हम एक हफ़्ता और देखें? — Shall we watch it one more week? · आप सही कह रहे हैं, बस रिस्क यह है। — You are right; only the risk is this.

Warm-up. Two cards: हाँ and रुकिए. Practise the pause before नहीं. Teach. अलग है, रिस्क, एक हफ़्ता और. Soft face, clear noun. Not आप गलत हैं. Guided. Pairs: deadline, school fee extra class, vendor price. One disagrees. Free. Manager loves a bad idea; you disagree once, then offer a test. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: one disagreement + one alternative, 30 seconds.

Slips. यू आर रॉन्ग → मेरी बात थोड़ी अलग है; आई डोंट थिंक सो ओनली → मुझे लगता है रिस्क यह है; जैसा आप कहें (disappear) → आप सही कह रहे हैं, बस एक शर्त है

Lesson 05 · Client ya vendor phone 6w, 3m, 6m

Run a short client or vendor call: name, issue, next date.

Say this: मैं प्रिया बोल रही हूँ, ___ कंपनी से। — This is Priya, from ___ company. · ऑर्डर नंबर बताइए। — Please tell me the order number. · यह शुक्र तक आ जाएगा। — This will arrive by Friday. · मैं मेल पर लिख देती हूँ / देता हूँ। — I will put it on mail.

Warm-up. Back-to-back. Write the promise only after they say a day. Teach. बोल रही/रहा हूँ + company. Promise + date. Aap even if the vendor is younger. Guided. Three calls: delay, wrong item, extra quantity. Homemakers may be the ‘school vendor’ or tiffin client. Free. Angry client; you stay slow and take a callback time. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: a 25-second client opening plus one promise.

Slips. मैं प्रिया फ्रॉम कंपनी → मैं प्रिया बोल रही हूँ, कंपनी से; इट विल कम बाय फ्राइडे → यह शुक्र तक आ जाएगा; आई विल मेल → मैं मेल पर लिख देती/देता हूँ

Lesson 06 · Stand-up: kal, aaj, atka 6w, 3m, 6m

Give a 30-second stand-up: yesterday, today, stuck.

Say this: कल मैंने बिल भेजा। — Yesterday I sent the bill. · आज क्लाइंट को कॉल है। — Today I have a client call. · एक चीज़ अटकी है, पासवर्ड नहीं खुल रहा। — One thing is stuck; the password isn’t opening. · मदद चाहिए ___ से। — I need help from ___.

Warm-up. Timer visible. First round may be home operations: कल राशन, आज स्कूल, अटकी हुई किताब. Teach. कल मैंने, आज, अटकी है. Short verbs. No novel. Return-to-work uses home proof with the same frame. Guided. Rounds of four. If you finish early, sit. If you ramble, teacher claps once. Free. Second pass: cut 20%. Clearer, not fancier. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Send a 30-second stand-up voice note about a real yesterday/today.

Slips. यैस्टर्डे आई हैव सेंट → कल मैंने बिल भेजा; टूडे आई एम डूइंग → आज क्लाइंट को कॉल है; आई एम स्टक → एक चीज़ अटकी है

Lesson 07 · Meeting se pehle small talk 3m, 6m

Do 40 seconds of human talk before the agenda — then switch.

Say this: ट्रैफिक कैसा था? — How was the traffic? · बच्चों की छुट्टी चल रही है। — The children’s holiday is on. · चाय आ जाए, फिर शुरू करें। — Let tea come, then we’ll start. · अच्छा, एजेंडे पर आते हैं। — Right, let’s come to the agenda.

Warm-up. Two minutes of fake corridor. Then a bell: agenda. Teach. Small talk is short. No medical deep-dive. एजेंडे पर आते हैं is a skill. Guided. Pairs: weather, commute, cricket, then switch on a clap. Free. Senior walks in; you greet with aap, one human line, then the file. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Use एजेंडे पर आते हैं (or घर की बात पर आते हैं) once this week.

Slips. हाउ वाज़ ट्रैफिक ओनली → ट्रैफिक कैसा था?; लेट्स स्टार्ट → एजेंडे पर आते हैं; लॉन्ग फैमिली स्टोरी → एक लाइन, फिर काम

Lesson 08 · Manager se request 6w, 3m, 6m

Ask a manager or a senior at home-office for time, leave, or a tool.

Say this: मुझे आधा घंटा चाहिए, एक फाइल के लिए। — I need half an hour for one file. · शुक्रवार को छुट्टी चाहिए, घर में मेहमान हैं। — I need Friday off; we have guests. · क्या आप रिव्यू कर देंगी / देंगे? — Could you please review? · डेडलाइन कठिन है, एक दिन और मिलेगा? — The deadline is tight; can I have one more day?

Warm-up. One ‘manager’ chair. Requests, not confessions. Teach. चाहिए + reason in one clause. Aap. Homemakers returning to work practise the same ask with a coordinator. Guided. Three asks: WFH, software access, school emergency. Free. Manager says no; you offer a smaller ask. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Write nothing. Voice-note one real request you might make.

Slips. प्लीज़ गिव मी हाफ आवर → मुझे आधा घंटा चाहिए; आई नीड लीव → शुक्रवार को छुट्टी चाहिए; डेडलाइन इज़ हार्ड → डेडलाइन कठिन है, एक दिन और मिलेगा?

Lesson 09 · Appraisal aur feedback 3m, 6m

Receive and give feedback: one glow, one grow, one example.

Say this: जो काम समय पर आया, वह अच्छा रहा। — The work that came on time was good. · एक जगह और स्पष्टता चाहिए। — One place needs more clarity. · उदाहरण दूँ? — Shall I give an example? · मैं यह बदल सकती हूँ / सकता हूँ। — I can change this.

Warm-up. Paper with two boxes: अच्छा, आगे. No HR lecture. Teach. स्पष्टता, उदाहरण, बदल. Hear first. Don’t argue the first ten seconds. Guided. Pairs: give feedback on a fake delayed report. Then receive. Free. Self-appraisal 40 seconds: two wins, one next skill. Home ops count as wins. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: one glow and one grow about your own week.

Slips. यू डिड गुड बट → समय पर आया, वह अच्छा रहा। एक जगह...; आई विल ट्राइ → मैं यह बदल सकती/सकता हूँ; मैं तो कुछ खास नहीं करती → मैंने ___ समय पर पूरा किया

Lesson 10 · Senior ko chhota update 6w, 3m, 6m

Give a 45-second update to a senior: status, risk, ask.

Say this: प्रोजेक्ट अस्सी प्रतिशत पूरा है। — The project is eighty percent done. · रिस्क यह है कि क्लाइंट लेट बोल रहा है। — The risk is that the client is speaking late. · मुझे आपके एक साइन की ज़रूरत है। — I need one sign from you. · कल शाम तक अगला अपडेट दूँगी / दूँगा। — I will give the next update by tomorrow evening.

Warm-up. Timer. Teacher is the busy director. Sit; don’t perform a speech. Teach. स्थिति, रिस्क, साइन/मंज़ूरी, अगला अपडेट. Numbers spoken slowly. Guided. Pairs cut a rambling update down to four lines. Free. Director asks क्यों लेट? You answer one cause, one fix. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: status, risk, ask — 45 seconds.

Slips. वी आर ऑलमोस्ट डन → प्रोजेक्ट अस्सी प्रतिशत पूरा है; देयर इज़ अ रिस्क → रिस्क यह है कि...; आई नीड योर साइन → मुझे आपके एक साइन की ज़रूरत है

Lesson 11 · Pehle bolo, phir email 6m

Speak the email first: purpose, ask, by when — then the written mail will be easy.

Say this: मेल का मकसद यह है... — The purpose of the mail is... · मुझे आपसे एक जवाब चाहिए। — I need one reply from you. · कल दोपहर तक भेज दीजिए। — Please send it by tomorrow afternoon. · थैंक्स, मैंने अभी मेल कर दी। — Thanks; I have just mailed it.

Warm-up. No laptops required. Speak a four-line mail to a partner who writes keywords only. Teach. मकसद, जवाब, तक. Kill empty padding: further to our conversation, as discussed, circling back. Guided. Three spoken mails: delay, request, thanks. Then partner reads the keywords back as a check. Free. Angry spoken draft; rewrite it kind and short, still in Hindi. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Before you send a real mail this week, say it aloud in Hindi once.

Slips. फर्दर टू आवर कॉन्वर्सेशन → कल वाली बात के बाद, मकसद यह है; प्लीज़ डू द नीडफुल → कल दोपहर तक जवाब भेज दीजिए; सर्कलिंग बैक → एक रिमाइंडर: जवाब अभी बाकी है

Lesson 12 · Kathin customer ya parent 3m, 6m

Hold a difficult customer or parent: hear, repeat the issue, offer one next step.

Say this: आप नाराज़ हैं, मैं सुन रही हूँ / रहा हूँ। — You are angry; I am listening. · समस्या यह है कि डिलीवरी लेट हुई। — The problem is that the delivery was late. · आज शाम तक नया स्लॉट देती हूँ / देता हूँ। — I will give a new slot by this evening. · अगर फिर दिक्कत हो तो मुझे सीधे कॉल कीजिए। — If there is trouble again, please call me directly.

Warm-up. One angry voice, one calm. Volume down, not up. Teach. सुन रही/रहा हूँ, समस्या यह है, आज शाम तक. School-office parents and shop clients use the same spine. Aap stays. Guided. Pairs: late cab, wrong tiffin, marks shock. Listener must paraphrase before solving. Free. Person shouts; you ask for one minute, then the next step. No matching heat. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: paraphrase plus one next step, 35 seconds.

Slips. मैडम कलम डाउन → आप नाराज़ हैं, मैं सुन रही/रहा हूँ; इट्स नॉट माई फॉल्ट → समस्या यह है कि... हम यह करेंगे; कॉल मी डायरेक्ट ओनली → फिर दिक्कत हो तो मुझे सीधे कॉल कीजिए

Lesson 13 · Kaam ki proof kahani 6w, 3m, 6m

Tell a proof story: situation, what you did, result — home ops allowed.

Say this: स्कूल की सालगिरह पर खाना कम पड़ रहा था। — Food was running short at the school anniversary. · मैंने पास की दुकान से ऑर्डर बढ़ाया। — I increased the order from a nearby shop. · सब बच्चों को प्लेट मिल गई। — Every child got a plate. · टीम को मैंने पहले ही वॉट्सऐप कर दिया था। — I had already WhatsApp’d the team.

Warm-up. STAR on four fingers, no English acronym lecture. Teacher models a kitchen-logistics story and an office story. Teach. मैंने + past. Result in one line. Homemaking is proof. Delete मैंने तो कुछ खास नहीं किया. Guided. Partner stories, two minutes. Listener repeats the result only. Free. Tell the same story in 90 seconds, then in 40. Keep the result. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: one proof story, 50 seconds, with a result.

Slips. आई जस्ट हैंडल्ड इट → मैंने ___ किया, नतीजा यह रहा; मैं तो घर पे ही थी, प्रूफ नहीं → मैंने घर/स्कूल का काम समय पर निकाला; वी डिड अ गुड जॉब → नतीजा: समय पर पूरा हुआ

Lesson 14 · Agar main yeh chala rahi / raha hoti 6m

Say if I were running this: one change, one reason, one risk.

Say this: अगर मैं यह मीटिंग चला रही होती / रहा होता, तो एजेंडा छोटा रखती / रखता। — If I were running this meeting, I would keep the agenda short. · एक बदलाव यह होता: सब दो मिनट बोलें। — One change would be: everyone speaks two minutes. · रिस्क यह है कि कोई छूट जाए। — The risk is that someone gets left out.

Warm-up. A stuck weekly meeting. People may speak seated. No TED energy. Teach. अगर मैं ... होती/होता तो. One change only. Return-to-work adults may run a house or a PTA stall as ‘this’. Guided. Pairs: canteen, stand-up, society WhatsApp group. One change each. Free. Hot seat: you have inherited a late project. One change, one ask to the team. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: one अगर मैं line about a real room you sit in.

Slips. इफ आई वर रनिंग → अगर मैं यह चला रही/रहा होती/होता तो; आई वुड चेंज एवरीथिंग → एक बदलाव यह होता; नो रिस्क → रिस्क यह है कि...

Lesson 15 · Doosri team ke saath 3m, 6m

Talk across teams: what we need, what we can give, by when.

Say this: हमारी टीम को फाइल कल चाहिए। — Our team needs the file tomorrow. · हम आपको स्लाइड्स आज शाम तक दे सकते हैं। — We can give you the slides by this evening. · आपकी प्राथमिकता क्या है? — What is your priority? · एक कॉमन डेडलाइन तय कर लें। — Let’s fix one common deadline.

Warm-up. Two tables: ‘sales’ and ‘accounts’, or ‘home’ and ‘school office’. Teach. चाहिए, दे सकते हैं, प्राथमिकता. Aap if the other team is senior or new. No blame history. Guided. Negotiate one deadline. Write it only after both say it. Free. Other team is overloaded; you cut the ask, you don’t guilt them. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. If you message another team this week, say the need and the by-when in Hindi first.

Slips. वी नीड इट टुमॉरो → हमारी टीम को फाइल कल चाहिए; वी कैन गिव स्लाइड्स → हम स्लाइड्स आज शाम तक दे सकते हैं; योर प्रायॉरिटी? → आपकी प्राथमिकता क्या है?

Lesson 16 · Beech meeting ka mock 6w, 3m, 6m

Run an eight-minute meeting: agenda, two points, disagreement, close.

Say this: आज तीन बातें हैं। — There are three things today. · समय दो-दो मिनट। — Two minutes each. · यह पार्क करते हैं। — We’ll park this. · अगला कदम यह है, मालिक ___ हैं। — The next step is this; ___ owns it.

Warm-up. Roles on slips: chair, timekeeper, quiet one, over-talker. Seniors may be timekeeper seated. Teach. Chair uses seat phrases from earlier weeks. Teacher only watches the clock. Guided. First mock: messy on purpose. Second mock: same agenda, cleaner. Free. Debrief in Hindi: किसने सीट ली, कौन दब गया. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Note one line you will steal for a real meeting.

Slips. लेट्स जस्ट स्टार्ट → आज तीन बातें हैं; मूविंग ऑन → यह पार्क करते हैं; एक्शन आइटम इज़ → अगला कदम यह है, मालिक ___ हैं

Lesson 17 · Ginti zabaan se bolo 6m

Say work numbers out loud: percent, lakh, deadline dates, floors of a target.

Say this: यह साढ़े तीन लाख का ऑर्डर है। — This is an order of three and a half lakh. · वृद्धि बारह प्रतिशत है। — Growth is twelve percent. · डेडलाइन तीस तारीख है, रात बारह बजे नहीं। — The deadline is the 30th, not midnight theatre. · हम लक्ष्य से थोड़े नीचे हैं। — We are a little below target.

Warm-up. Board: %, लाख, तारीख. Slow chorus once. Return-to-work: fees, EMI, kiraya counts. Teach. साढ़े, प्रतिशत, लक्ष्य. Don’t race. If a number is private, invent one. Guided. Partner dictation: you say numbers; they repeat, then swap. Free. Update a senior with three numbers and one ask — no extra story. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Read one real bill or dashboard number aloud in Hindi at home.

Slips. थ्री पॉइंट फाइव लाख → साढ़े तीन लाख; ट्वेल्व परसेंट ग्रोथ → बारह प्रतिशत वृद्धि है; वी आर बिलो टारगेट → हम लक्ष्य से थोड़े नीचे हैं

Lesson 18 · Bin padhe present karo 3m, 6m

Present two minutes without reading: three headings, one story, one ask.

Say this: तीन बातें: समय, पैसा, लोग। — Three things: time, money, people. · एक मिसाल यह रही... — One example was... · आपसे मंज़ूरी चाहिए। — I need approval from you. · सवाल हों तो पूछिए। — If there are questions, please ask.

Warm-up. One card with three words only. Phone screens face down. Teach. Headings + example + ask. Reading a paragraph is the fail state. Homemakers present a function plan the same way. Guided. Pairs: 90 seconds, then questions. Sit to present if that helps the voice. Free. Someone asks a hard why; you use समझ गई/गया, एक लाइन में. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise a two-minute present at home to a wall or a person.

Slips. रीडिंग द स्लाइड → तीन शब्द, फिर बोलो; एग्ज़ांपल इज़ लाइक → एक मिसाल यह रही; प्लीज़ अप्रूव → आपसे मंज़ूरी चाहिए

Lesson 19 · Hybrid aur video room 6m

Handle a video or hybrid room: mute, overlap, ‘can you hear’, Hindi first.

Say this: क्या आवाज़ आ रही है? — Is the sound coming through? · मैं म्यूट पर थी / था, दोबारा कहती हूँ / कहता हूँ। — I was on mute; I will say it again. · जो घर से हैं, पहले बोलें। — Those at home, please speak first. · स्लाइड नंबर चार पर आइए। — Please come to slide number four.

Warm-up. If you have no camera, mime laptops. Overlap on purpose, then repair. Teach. म्यूट, आवाज़, स्लाइड. Aap on a mixed call. Don’t disappear when the video lags. Guided. Three glitches: mute, echo, two people start. Repair in Hindi. Free. Hybrid: two ‘in room’, two ‘on phone’. Chair includes the remote people. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. On your next real video call, use one Hindi repair line.

Slips. कैन यू हियर मी → क्या आवाज़ आ रही है?; आई वाज़ ऑन म्यूट → मैं म्यूट पर थी/था; रिमोट पीपल फर्स्ट → जो घर से हैं, पहले बोलें

Lesson 20 · Ruko, badlo, wapas aao 3m, 6m

Talk stay, switch, or return-to-work as a plan, not a confession.

Say this: मैं इस भूमिका में और एक साल रहना चाहती हूँ / चाहता हूँ। — I want to stay in this role one more year. · मुझे बदलाव चाहिए, सीखना थम गया है। — I need a change; learning has stalled. · तीन साल बाद वापस आ रही हूँ / रहा हूँ, घर का काम भी प्रूफ है। — I am returning after three years; home work is also proof. · सैलरी बाद में, पहले काम की बात। — Salary later; the work talk first.

Warm-up. Three doors on the board: रुको, बदलो, वापस. People pick one. No advice from classmates. Teach. चाहती/ता हूँ, वापस आ रही/रहा हूँ. Shame phrases banned. Gap is a chapter. Guided. 90-second career talk. Partner only asks क्या अगला कदम है? Free. A well-meaning uncle says घर सँभालो; you answer with one dignified line. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: stay, switch, or return — one minute, no apology.

Slips. मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ, जॉब नहीं → मैं वापस आ रही/रहा हूँ; घर का काम मेरी प्रूफ है; आई वांट अ चेंज → मुझे बदलाव चाहिए क्योंकि...; मनी फर्स्ट → पहले भूमिका, फिर सैलरी

Lesson 21 · Kaam ki neeti 6m

Name an ethics moment at work or in a building contract: what you will not do.

Say this: यह बिल गलत है, मैं साइन नहीं करूँगी / करूँगा। — This bill is wrong; I will not sign. · क्लाइंट को सच बताना चाहिए। — The client should be told the truth. · यह बात कमरे में रहनी चाहिए। — This matter should stay in the room. · मुझे समय दीजिए, मैं चेक करके बताती हूँ / बताता हूँ। — Give me time; I will check and tell you.

Warm-up. Warn: invented cases only. No real company secrets in class. Teach. साइन नहीं, सच, कमरे में. Calm voice. Aap to the person pressuring you. Guided. Pairs: extra cash, fake attendance, child’s project copied. Choose a line. Free. Pressure from a senior; you delay, you don’t lie. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Optional: one line you want in your pocket for a real squeeze.

Slips. आई कैनॉट साइन दिस → यह बिल गलत है, मैं साइन नहीं करूँगी/गा; क्लाइंट को मत बताना → क्लाइंट को सच बताना चाहिए; लेट मी चेक → मुझे समय दीजिए, चेक करके बताती/बताता हूँ

Lesson 22 · Tez meeting workshop aur briefing 6w, 3m, 6m

Workshop a four-minute briefing: context, three points, risk, ask — then speed-run a meeting.

Say this: बैकग्राउंड एक लाइन। तीन पॉइंट। रिस्क। आपसे यह चाहिए।

Warm-up. Teacher models four minutes sitting. Then a five-minute meeting using only seat and park lines. Teach. Keywords card. Return-to-work briefings may be a stall, a PTA fund, or a shop shift. Same spine. Guided. Rehearse briefing twice. Then a speed meeting: six people, five minutes, one decision. Free. Two volunteers brief the room. Class asks one hard question. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise the four-minute briefing at home with a timer.

Slips. लॉन्ग बैकस्टोरी → बैकग्राउंड एक लाइन; फिलर: बेसिकली एक्चुअली → तीन पॉइंट, फिर रिस्क; मीटिंग विद नो क्लोज़ → एक निर्णय, मालिक का नाम

Lesson 23 · Briefing manch aur aakhri test 6w, 3m, 6m

Give the four-minute briefing and take one hard question. Close the course.

Say this: Four-minute briefing. One disagreement or ethics question from the teacher.

Warm-up. Quiet rehearsal. Sit if you wish. No ranking. Teach. End test: briefing + one client/parent or disagreement. Score clear ask and keep-going. Guided. Audience writes one thing they understood — in Hindi, one line. Free. Circle: मैं कमरे में हिंदी में बोल सकती / सकता हूँ. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. No homework. Interview course if a panel is coming; otherwise keep speaking at work.

Slips. Fixing every gender on stage → Thank them. Note one grow privately; Guaranteeing a promotion → Guarantee clearer speech only

Urban Spoken Hindi · Adults · Interview, izzat ke saath

Adults · Interview, izzat ke saath

Adults 26–50 Interview speaking 17 lessons

Career chapters, gaps, return-to-work, salary, mocks. Crash option included.

Who: Adults changing jobs, returning after a break, or facing a Hindi/mixed panel after years of silent competence.

Done when: They can tell their life in 90 seconds, prove two strengths, explain a gap, and sit a 15-minute mock.

Place here when: Not for Absolute Beginners. They must already handle Everyday or Fluency talk.

Teacher note: Delete shame phrases: ‘main toh ghar pe hi hoon’, ‘sirf gharwala kaam’. Never guarantee a job.

Tests

StartCurrent intro. Time it. Listen for shame words.
MidFull mock 1.
EndHarder mock 2 plus public intro.

Lessons

Lesson 01 · Interview ek baat-cheet hai crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Treat the interview as a conversation: greet, sit, one human line, then work.

Say this: नमस्ते, आपसे मिलकर अच्छा लगा। — Namaste, glad to meet you. · यहाँ बैठूँ? — May I sit here? · ट्रैफिक ठीक था, समय पर पहुँच गई / गया। — Traffic was fine; I reached on time. · हाँ, मैं तैयार हूँ। — Yes, I am ready.

Warm-up. A door and a chair. Practise sitting without rushing. No handshake drama if it is not your culture. Teach. Aap on, always, in this room. Conversation ≠ chat about gossip. One human line, then wait. Guided. Pairs: enter, namaste, sit, water offered, begin. Swap. Free. Panel of two. You greet both. Names if you heard them. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Practise the entry once at home, standing then sitting.

Slips. गुड मॉर्निंग सर, हाउ आर यू स्पीच → नमस्ते, आपसे मिलकर अच्छा लगा; शैल आई सिट → यहाँ बैठूँ?; आई एम सो नर्वस हैहा → हाँ, मैं तैयार हूँ

Lesson 02 · Adult intro, 90 second crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Give a 60–90 second adult intro: now, proof, what you want — no life novel.

Say this: मैं ___ हूँ, पिछले ___ साल ___ काम किया। — I am ___; I have worked ___ years in ___. · घर का ऑपरेशन भी मैंने चलाया है। — I have also run the home operation. · अभी मैं ___ भूमिका ढूँढ रही हूँ / रहा हूँ। — Right now I am looking for the ___ role. · एक मिसाल: मैंने ___ पूरा किया। — One example: I completed ___.

Warm-up. Timer 90 seconds. Teacher models a return-to-work intro with zero shame. Teach. Now + proof + want. Homemaking is proof. Cut childhood school names unless asked. Guided. Record on a phone if people agree. Listen for मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ and rewrite. Free. Two intros: to HR, then to a technical senior. Same spine, fewer English toys for HR. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: 75-second intro. Kill one shame phrase if it appeared.

Slips. मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ → मैंने घर का ऑपरेशन चलाया है, अभी वापस आ रही/रहा हूँ; माई नेम इज़ एंड आई एम अ → मैं ___ हूँ, मैं ___ काम करती/करता हूँ; टू लॉन्ग चाइल्डहुड → अभी + एक प्रूफ + यह भूमिका

Lesson 03 · Career ke chapters crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Walk through a career in chapters, including a gap, without apology music.

Say this: पहला अध्याय: ___ में सीखा। — First chapter: I learned in ___. · फिर ___ टीम में जिम्मेदारी बढ़ी। — Then responsibility grew in the ___ team. · बीच में तीन साल घर सँभाला, वह भी काम है। — In between I ran the house for three years; that is also work. · अब अगला अध्याय यह भूमिका है। — Now the next chapter is this role.

Warm-up. Four boxes on the board. People fill by speaking, not writing essays. Teach. अध्याय, जिम्मेदारी, बीच में. Gap is a chapter. Contract, shop, teaching, home — all valid. Guided. Partner tells your chapters back. If they heard shame, you repair. Free. Panel: why this order? You answer with one thread, not ten jobs. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Draw four chapter words and speak them once at home.

Slips. देन आई टुक अ ब्रेक सॉरी → बीच में घर सँभाला, वह भी काम है; फर्स्ट आई डिड दिस, सेकंड → पहला अध्याय..., फिर...; नो थ्रेड, जॉब लिस्ट → एक धागा: लोग, पैसे, या ऑपरेशन

Lesson 04 · Yeh jagah, abhi kyun crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Answer why us and why now with the company/school plus your season of life.

Say this: आपकी टीम ___ काम करती है, वह मुझे चाहिए। — Your team does ___ work; I want that. · लोकेशन और समय मेरे घर के साथ बैठते हैं। — Location and timing fit with my home. · अब बच्चे बड़े हो गए हैं / अब मैं तैयार हूँ। — The children are older now / I am ready now. · मैं जल्दी जॉइनिंग दे सकती हूँ / सकता हूँ। — I can give an early joining.

Warm-up. A fake JD of five words. No reciting the About page. Teach. आपकी टीम + मेरा मौसम. Flattery without facts fails. Seniors changing work: abhi kyun still matters. Guided. Pairs: why this school, this shop, this BPO, this NGO. Free. They ask why not the bigger brand? You answer fit, not insult. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: why this place, 40 seconds, one fact about them.

Slips. आई एम पैशनेट अबाउट योर विज़न → आपकी टीम ___ करती है, वह मुझे चाहिए; बिकॉज़ चिल्ड्रन नाउ ओके → अब समय बैठता है, मैं नियमित दे सकती/सकता हूँ; आई कैन जॉइन इमीडिएटली → मैं जल्दी जॉइनिंग दे सकती/सकता हूँ

Lesson 05 · Asli kaam se taqat crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Name two strengths from real work, each with a tiny proof.

Say this: मेरी ताकत समय पर पूरा करना है। — My strength is finishing on time. · उदाहरण: स्कूल इवेंट का खाना मैंने टाइम पर निकाला। — Example: I got the school-event food out on time. · दूसरी ताकत लोगों को शांत रखना है। — A second strength is keeping people calm. · गार्ड और पेरेंट्स दोनों से मैंने बात की। — I spoke with both the guard and the parents.

Warm-up. Ban empty words: dedicated, hardworking, team player — unless proof follows in the same breath. Teach. ताकत + उदाहरण. Home ops proofs welcome. Two strengths, not seven. Guided. Partner steals your proof and tells it back. You fix what they missed. Free. HR says ‘team player’; you translate into one Hindi story. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: two strengths, two proofs, 50 seconds.

Slips. आई एम अ टीम प्लेयर → मैं लोगों के साथ काम निकालती/ता हूँ, उदाहरण...; आई एम हार्डवर्किंग → मैं समय पर पूरा करती/करता हूँ, उदाहरण...; नो एग्ज़ांपल → ताकत के साथ एक मिसाल

Lesson 06 · Kamzori aur gap saal crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Talk a weakness and a gap year with a fix, without confessing a crime.

Say this: एक कमज़ोरी: मैं कभी जल्दी हाँ कर देती हूँ / देता हूँ। — One weakness: I sometimes say yes too fast. · अब पहले समय देखती हूँ / देखता हूँ। — Now I look at time first. · गैप में मैंने घर चलाया, और एक कोर्स किया। — In the gap I ran the house, and did one course. · वापस आने की तैयारी यह है... — The preparation to return is...

Warm-up. Teacher models a boring honest weakness, not ‘I work too hard’. Teach. कमज़ोरी + अब. गैप + क्या किया. Never मैं तो घर पे ही थी as the whole answer. Guided. Pairs write nothing. Speak, then swap a worse shame version and repair it. Free. Panel pushes: three years is long. You name skills that continued. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: weakness plus gap, 45 seconds, no sorry-loop.

Slips. आई वर्क टू हार्ड → एक असली कमज़ोरी + अब मैं क्या करती/करता हूँ; गैप वाज़ जस्ट होम सॉरी → गैप में घर चलाया; यह ऑपरेशन है; आई हैव नो वीकनेस → एक कमज़ोरी, एक इलाज

Lesson 07 · Kahaniyon ka bank crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Build a bank of three stories: a win, a fight-fixed, a mistake-repaired.

Say this: जीत: मैंने ___ समय पर किया। — Win: I did ___ on time. · झगड़ा: दो लोगों में टेंशन थी, मैंने बीच में बात की। — Conflict: two people were tense; I spoke in between. · गलती: मैंने गलत ऑर्डर भेज दिया, फिर उसी दिन सुधारा। — Mistake: I sent a wrong order, then fixed it the same day.

Warm-up. Three pockets. People may use school, shop, office, or building. Teach. Same STAR spine. Keep names out of class if the story is real and sharp. Guided. Tell all three in one minute each. Then pick the one this JD needs. Free. Listener asks क्या सीखा? Speaker answers one line. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Keep a three-line bank on paper. Speak it, don’t read it, once tonight.

Slips. आई सॉल्ड अ कॉन्फ्लिक्ट → दो लोगों में टेंशन थी, मैंने बात करवाई; मिस्टेक वाज़ नॉट माई फॉल्ट → गलती हुई, उसी दिन सुधारा; थ्री जॉब लिस्ट्स, नो स्टोरी → तीन कहानियाँ, एक-एक मिनट

Lesson 08 · HR ki classic baatein crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Answer HR classics: five years, salary, notice, night shift, bond — with dignity.

Say this: पाँच साल में मैं ___ भूमिका में होना चाहती हूँ / चाहता हूँ। — In five years I want to be in the ___ role. · सैलरी पर बात रोल क्लियर होने के बाद। — Salary talk after the role is clear. · नोटिस तीस दिन है, बात हो सकती है। — Notice is thirty days; we can talk. · नाइट शिफ्ट अभी मुश्किल है, सुबह दे सकती हूँ / सकता हूँ। — Night shift is hard right now; I can give mornings.

Warm-up. A rapid HR. No trick-shame about marriage or babies. If it comes, practise a boundary. Teach. चाहती/ता हूँ, बात हो सकती है. You may refuse night shift. You may not know a number yet. Guided. Drill: five years, why leave, salary range, relocate, bond. Short answers. Free. Illegal-feeling question (plans for a child): मैं काम नियमित दूँगी/गा, बाकी निजी है. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Voice note: five-years plus one boundary line.

Slips. आई डोंट नो, एनीथिंग इज़ फाइन → पाँच साल में ___ भूमिका; सैलरी व्हाटेवर यू से → रोल क्लियर हो, फिर नंबर; नाइट शिफ्ट ओके सॉरी फॉर फैमिली → अभी नाइट मुश्किल है, सुबह दे सकती/सकता हूँ

Lesson 09 · Wapas-kaam mock crash, 3m, 6m

Run a return-to-work mock: intro, gap, one proof, why now.

Say this: मैं तीन साल बाद वापस आ रही हूँ / रहा हूँ। — I am returning after three years. · घर का काम मैंने प्रोजेक्ट की तरह चलाया। — I ran the house like a project. · अभी समय बैठता है, मैं फुल-टाइम दे सकती हूँ / सकता हूँ। — Time fits now; I can give full-time. · मुझसे टेक-टूल पूछिए, मैं सच बोलूँगी / बोलूँगा। — Ask me about tools; I will tell the truth.

Warm-up. Panel of two. Returners go first if they want. Others play the gap as a career pause. Teach. Delete सरफ घरवाला काम. Tools: be honest. Offer to learn, don’t fake Excel. Guided. Eight-minute mock. Glow and grow from the panel in Hindi. Free. Second pass: cut the apology words you still used. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Record the four-line return spine once more tonight.

Slips. सरफ घरवाला काम किया → घर का ऑपरेशन प्रोजेक्ट की तरह चलाया; आई एम रस्टी सॉरी → कुछ टूल दोबारा सीख रही/रहा हूँ, सच यह है; प्लीज़ गिव मी अ चांस → मैं फुल-टाइम दे सकती/सकता हूँ, प्रूफ यह है

Lesson 10 · GD aur panel crash, 3m, 6m

Join a GD or a panel: enter, add, disagree, include the quiet person.

Say this: मैं एक पॉइंट जोड़ूँ। — I’ll add one point. · आपकी बात से सहमत हूँ, एक जोड़ यह है। — I agree with you; one add is this. · जो अभी नहीं बोले, उनकी भी सुनें। — Let’s also hear those who haven’t spoken. · समय कम है, निष्कर्ष यह हो सकता है। — Time is short; the conclusion could be this.

Warm-up. Topic on a slip: school phones, WFH, city parks. Four people. Two minutes prep, six minutes talk. Teach. जोड़ूँ, सहमत, निष्कर्ष. Don’t chair unless asked. Don’t vanish. Guided. Two GDs. Second time, the quietest person must speak first. Free. Panel follow-up: one of you is asked to summarise. 30 seconds. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. One opinion plus one include-line, voice note, 30 seconds.

Slips. आई एग्री विद हिम → आपकी बात से सहमत हूँ; लेट मी लीड द GD → एक पॉइंट जोड़ूँ — don’t steal the room; क्वाइट पर्सन इग्नोर → जो नहीं बोले, उनकी भी सुनें

Lesson 11 · Aap kya poochhenge crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Ask two good questions: the work, the team, the first month — not about the canteen only.

Say this: पहले महीने की सफलता कैसी दिखेगी? — What will success look like in the first month? · टीम में कितने लोग हैं, रिपोर्ट किसे करनी है? — How many people in the team, whom do I report to? · सीखने का समय कितना मिलेगा? — How much time will there be to learn? · यह भूमिका छह महीने बाद कैसे बदलेगी? — How will this role change after six months?

Warm-up. Ban: कोई सवाल नहीं. Ban only-salary as the first question. Teach. Work questions first. Salary later if they open it. Aap. Write nothing in the interview; remember two. Guided. Pairs fire questions at a fake hiring manager. Manager answers shortly. Free. You already asked two; they say any more? You ask one about the first-week buddy. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Write two questions on a card. Speak them without reading, twice.

Slips. नो क्वेश्चन्स, आई एम फाइन → पहले महीने की सफलता...; वॉट इज़ द CTC → रोल के बाद सैलरी, पहले काम; कैंटीन कैसी है ओनली → टीम और रिपोर्टिंग पूछिए

Lesson 12 · Phone screen crash, 6m

Handle a 12-minute phone screen: name, noise, intro, one story, your question.

Say this: हेलो, मैं ___ बोल रही हूँ / रहा हूँ, आपकी कॉल का जवाब दे रही हूँ / रहा हूँ। — Hello, this is ___, answering your call. · एक सेकंड, मैं शांत जगह लेती हूँ / लेता हूँ। — One second, I will take a quiet place. · आप मुझे थोड़ा और भूमिका के बारे में बताइए। — Please tell me a little more about the role. · लाइन कट गई तो मैं वापस करती हूँ / करता हूँ। — If the line drops I will call back.

Warm-up. Back-to-back, real phones if allowed, speaker off. A child-noise drill: pause, don’t panic. Teach. बोल रही/रहा हूँ. Ask to move rooms. Smile is useless on a phone; voice is the face. Guided. Full mini screen: 8 minutes. Partner is HR with a bad connection. Free. They call while you are in an auto; you offer a callback time. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Do one real-style phone intro to a classmate’s voicemail or a family member.

Slips. हेलो हू इज़ दिस → मैं ___ बोल रही/रहा हूँ; सॉरी बेबी इज़ क्राइंग फॉरएवर → एक सेकंड, शांत जगह लेती/लेता हूँ; इफ कॉल ड्रॉप्स → लाइन कट गई तो मैं वापस करती/करता हूँ

Lesson 13 · Mock 1 crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Sit a 12-minute mock: intro, why us, one proof, one question you ask.

Say this: Full conversation. Teacher or peer panel of two.

Warm-up. Water, chair, phone away. Return-to-work papers only if they help, then close them. Teach. Clock 12 minutes. Do not stop them for grammar. Note shame words and empty padding. Guided. Panel asks why this role and one weakness. Candidate asks two questions. Free. Feedback: two glows, one grow, in Hindi, two minutes max. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Re-record only the intro tonight, incorporating one grow.

Slips. Stopping to teach gender mid-mock → Note it; finish the conversation; मैं तो घर पे → Rewrite that line before mock 2; ओवर-थैंकिंग एवरी एंसर → One proof, then stop

Lesson 14 · Repair clinic crash, 3m, 6m

Repair the mock: shame lines, ramble, aap slips, and the missing result.

Say this: वह लाइन हटाएँ: मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ। — Delete that line. · कहानी का नतीजा यह जोड़ें... — Add this result to the story. · आप हैं, आप हो नहीं। — It’s aap hain, not aap ho. · चालीस सेकंड में रोको। — Stop at forty seconds.

Warm-up. Board of actual slips from mock 1, anonymised if needed. Teach. Surgical fixes. Repeat the repaired sentence three times, slowly. Seniors in this course still sit and repeat. Guided. Clinic stations: intro, gap, salary, questions you ask. Free. Hot seat: the old shame line vs the new line. Class hears the difference. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Speak the three repaired sentences on a voice note.

Slips. मैं तो घर पे ही हूँ → मैंने घर का काम चलाया, अब यह भूमिका; आप कैसे हो सर → आप कैसे हैं?; स्टोरी विद नो एंड → नतीजा एक लाइन में

Lesson 15 · Interview ke baad 6m

After the interview: thank, mail, wait, and ask for a result without shrinking.

Say this: समय देने के लिए धन्यवाद। — Thank you for giving time. · मैंने आज वाली बात मेल पर लिख दी है। — I have put today’s points on mail. · क्या अगले कदम का समय मिल सकता है? — Could I have a time for the next step? · अगर सेलेक्ट नहीं हुई / हुआ, एक लाइन फीडबैक मिल जाए तो सीखूँगी / सीखूँगा। — If I am not selected, one line of feedback and I will learn.

Warm-up. No victory dance required. Practise the next-day mail as speech first. Teach. धन्यवाद, अगला कदम, फीडबैक. You may follow up once. You may not beg. Guided. Pairs: same-day thank you, day-five follow-up, a rejection that still asks a line. Free. Relative asks लग गई? You answer process, not shame. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Draft the thank-you as a 20-second voice note, then send a real mail if you have a panel pending.

Slips. प्लीज़ प्लीज़ कंसीडर मी → अगले कदम का समय मिल सकता है?; आई विल वेट फॉरएवर → पाँच दिन बाद एक शिष्ट फॉलो-अप; रिजेक्शन = आई एम फेल → एक लाइन फीडबैक, फिर अगली तैयारी

Lesson 16 · Mock 2 crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Sit a harder 15-minute mock: gap push, salary, panel overlap, your questions.

Say this: Longer panel. One person talks over you. One asks a sharp ethics or tools question.

Warm-up. Same dignity rules. Harder content, not a meaner room. Teach. 15 minutes. Include phone-style first minute if the batch needs it. Guided. Push on gap or job-hop. Candidate uses chapter language. Then two questions for them. Free. Feedback written as three bullets, spoken kindly. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. Sleep on it. One more intro recording only if the grow was the intro.

Slips. Collapsing on the gap → अध्याय + प्रूफ + अब; मैचिंग द ओवर-टॉकर → एक लाइन, रुकें, फिर पॉइंट; नो क्वेश्चन एट द एंड → दो काम वाले सवाल तैयार रखें

Lesson 17 · Aakhri manch crash, 6w, 3m, 6m

Give a public 90-second intro plus one proof story. Close with izzat, not a job promise.

Say this: The repaired intro. One proof. One question you would ask a real panel.

Warm-up. Audience of classmates. Sit or stand. Clap is optional. Teach. This is the end test. Score conversation, proof, and no-shame. Never guarantee a job. Guided. Each person. Others write one glow on a slip. Free. Circle: मैं अपनी ज़िंदगी izzat से बता सकती / सकता हूँ. Polish. Invite two or three students to speak. Correct only the target sentences. End with a class repeat.

Homework. No homework. Apply when a real panel comes. Keep the story bank warm.

Slips. Public marks out of 100 → Glow slip plus one private grow; Congratulating a hire that didn’t happen → Congratulating clearer speech
